                 auvai. cu. turaicāmip piḷḷai eḻutiya
     maṇimēkalaic curukkam, uraik kuṟippukkaḷuṭaṉ - pākam 1

                6. cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭam uraitta kātai

   ivvaṇṇam tōṉṟiya maṇimēkalā teyvam cutamatiyaiyum maṇimēkalaiyaiyum
   kaṇṭatu. piṉpu, at teyvam, cutamatiyaip pārttu, " iṅkē vāṭi niṟkiṉṟa
   nīvir eṉuṟṟīr?" eṉṟu viṉava, cutamati utaya kumaraṉ kūṟiya vaṉaittaiyum
   oḻiyātu urait tāḷ. appōtu at teyvam,
   "araciḷaṅkumaraṉ āyiḻai taṉmēl
   [1] taṇiyā nōkkam tavirvila ṉāki
   aṟattōr vaṉam eṉṟu akaṉṟaṉaṉ; āyiṉum,
   [2] puṟattōr vītiyil poruntutal oḻiyāṉ;
   ātalāl, nīṅkaḷ perunteruviṟ celvatai viṭuttu, iv vuvvaṉat taic
   cūḻntuḷḷa matiliṉ mēṟṟicaic ciṟuvāyil vaḻiyākac cakkara vāḷak
   kōṭṭattiṟkuc celka. aṅkē iravuppōtu kaḻiyiṉum uṅkaṭkuc ciṟu tīṅkum
   eytātu, "eṉṟu colliṟṟu, cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭam eṉṟu teyvam uraippak
   kēṭṭatum cutamati viyap puṟṟu, ataṉai nōkki, "ak kōṭṭattai iṅkuḷḷōr
   aṉai varum cuṭukāṭṭuk kōṭṭam eṉkiṉṟaṉar. eṉṉai iṅkē koṇarnta vañca
   viñcaiyaṉāṉa mārutavēkaṉ itaṉaic cakkara vāḷak kōṭṭam eṉṟu collak
   kēṭṭuḷēṉ; atuvēpōla, nīyum iṉṟu collak kēṭṭēṉ. itaṉaic cuṭukāṭṭuk kōṭ
   ṭam eṉṉātu, nīṅkaḷ cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭam eṉṟu colluva taṉ karutteṉṉai?"
   eṉṟu viṉaviṉaḷ.
   maṇimēkalā teyvam, ak karuttaiyaraikkap pukuntu, " itaṉai yāvarum
   cuṭukāṭṭuk kōṭṭam eṉpatu vaḻuvaṉṟu. in naṉkāṭu, campāpati eṉṉum innakar
   tōṉṟiya kālattē uṭaṉ tōṉṟiya mutukāṭē yākum. itaṉkaṇ,
   cuṭuvōr, iṭuvōr, toṭukuḻip paṭuppōr,
   [3] tāḻvayiṉ aṭaippōr, tāḻiyiṟ kavippōr,
   iravum pakalum [4] iḷivuṭaṉ tariyātu
   varuvōr peyarvōr māṟāc cummaiyum,
   eñciyōr maruṅkiṉ īmam cāṟṟi
   neñcu naṭukkuṟūum neyta lōcaiyum,
   tuṟavōr iṟanta [5]toḻuviḷip pūcalum,
   piṟavōr iṟanta aḻuviḷip pūcalum,
   nīḷmuka nariyiṉ [6] tīviḷik kūvum,
   [7] cāvōrp payirum kūkaiyiṉ kuralum,
   pulavūṇ poruntiya [8]kurāliṉ kuralum,
   [9] ūṇ talai tuṟṟiya āṇ talaik kuralum,
   nal nīrp [10] puṇari naḷi kaṭa lōtaiyiṉ
   iṉṉā icaiyoli eṉṟum niṉ ṟaṟātu.
   iṉṉa iyalpiṟṟāya cuṭukāṭu iruppak kaṇṭum, makkaḷ nilaitta vāḻvaṭaiyār
   pōl mayaṅkukiṉṟaṉar" eṉṟu viritturaikkat toṭaṅki, at teyvam,
   "tavattuṟai mākkaḷ, mikapperuñ celvar,
   [11]1 īṟṟiḷam peṇṭir, āṟṟāp pālakar,
   mutiyōr eṉṉāṉ, iḷaiyōr eṉṉāṉ,
   koṭuntoḻilāḷaṉ koṉṟaṉaṉ kuvippa, iv
   aḻalvāyc cuṭalai tiṉṉak kaṇṭum,
   kaḻiperuñ [12]celvak kaḷḷāṭ ṭayarntu
   mikka nalla ṟam virumpātu vāḻum
   makkaḷiṟ ciṟanta maṭavō ruṇṭō ?
   itu niṟka, orunāḷ iravu oru pēymakaḷ peṇ piṇa moṉ ṟiṉ karuntalaiyaik
   kaiyil ēnti,
   puyalō kuḻalō? kayalō kaṇṇō ?
   kumiḻō mūkkō? itaḻō kavirō?
   pallō muttō eṉṉātu, iraṅkātu,
   kaṇ [13] toṭṭuṇṭu [14] kavaiyaṭi peyarttut
   taṇṭāk kaḷippiṉ āṭum kūttu
   oṉṟai, avvaḻiyē aṟiyātu ceṉṟa, cārṅkalaṉ eṉṉum pārp paṉac ciṟuvaṉ
   oruvaṉ kaṇṭu añci naṭunaṭuṅki, taṉ vīṭṭiṟkuc ceṉṟu, taṉ tāyiṭam
   naṭantatu colli uyir viṭṭāṉ. avaṉ peṟṟōr mikka mutiyōrāvar. avaruḷ,
   avaṉ tantai kaṇṇiḻantavaṉ. kō tamai yeṉṉum avaṉatu tāy vayatu
   mutirntavaḷ.
   makaṉ pirivai āṟṟāta kōtamai, avaṉ uṭalaic cumantu īmappuṟaṅ kāṭṭiṉ
   matil vāyilil niṉṟu, campāpatiyai nōkki muṟaiyiṭṭaḻutāḷ. avaḷ kaṇmuṉ ac
   campāpati tōṉṟi, 'nī eṉṉai yaḻaitta kāraṇam eṉṉai?' eṉṟāḷ. kōtamai taṉ
   makaṉ iṟantu kiṭappatu kāṭṭi,
   ‘eṉ uyir koṇṭu ivaṉ uyir tantaruḷil, eṉ,
   kaṇṇil kaṇavaṉai ivaṉ kāttu ōmpiṭum;
   ivaṉ uyir tantu eṉ uyir vāṅku'
   eṉṟu irantaṉaḷ. atukēṭṭa campāpati,
   [15]'aiya muṇṭō, āruyir pōṉāl,
   ceyviṉai maruṅkil ceṉṟu piṟappeytutal;
   āṅkatu koṇarntu niṉ [16] ār iṭar nīkkutal
   īṅku eṉakku āvatoṉ ṟaṉṟu; nī iraṅkal.
   kolai aṟamām eṉum koṭuntoḻil mākkaḷ,
   [17] avalap paṭiṟṟurai āṅkatu; maṭavāy!
   ulaka maṉṉavarkku uyirkku uyir īvōr
   ilarō; inta īmap puṟaṅkāṭṭu
   aracarkku amaintaṉa āyiram kōṭṭam
   ataṉāl, nī niṉ karuttai viṭuka ' eṉṟaṉaḷ. itaṉālum maṉam teḷiyāta
   kōtamai, ' tēvarkaḷ ittakaiya varam taruvar eṉa nāṉmaṟai yantaṇar
   navilva tuṇṭu; ataṉāl, nī aruḷāviṭiṉ, yāṉ ippoḻutē uyir viṭuvēṉ' eṉṟu
   colla, campāpati, teyva vakai palavum viṭātu varavaḻaittu, avaḷ muṉ
   niṟutti, 'nī kēṭkum varattai ivar taravallarō, kāṇ' eṉṟāḷ. at teyvaṅkaḷ
   aṉaittum, campāpati kūṟiyavāṟē, ' iṟanta uyirai mīṭṭum taruvittal
   muṭiyātu' eṉṟu colla, kōtamai taṉ makaṉaic cuṭukāṭṭil iṭṭut tāṉum uyir
   tuṟantāḷ.
   campāpati taṉ āṟṟal tōṉṟa
   eṅkuvāḻ tēvarum kūṭiya iṭantaṉil,
   cūḻkaṭal vaḷai iya [18]āḻiyaṅ kuṉṟattu,
   naṭuvu niṉṟa mēruk kuṉṟamum,
   puṭaiyil niṉṟa eḻuvakaik kuṉṟamum
   [19] nālvakai marapiṉ māperun tīvum,
   ōrirāyiram ciṟṟiṭait tīvum,
   piṟavum āṅkataṉ iṭavakai uriyaṉa,
   peṟumuṟai marapiṉ aṟivu varak kāṭṭi,
   āṅkuvāḻ uyirkaḷum avvuyir iṭaṅkaḷum,
   pāṅkuṟa [20]maṇṇīṭṭil paṇpuṟa vakuttu
   mikka mayaṉāl iḻaikkappaṭṭa
   cakkara vāḷak kōṭṭam īṅku itukāṇ;
   iṭupiṇak kōṭṭattu [21]eyiṟpuṟa mātaliṉ,
   cuṭukāṭṭuk kōṭṭam eṉṟalatu uraiyār,
   ituvē itaṉ varalāṟu " eṉa am maṇimēkalā teyvam kūṟi muṭittatu. ataṉpiṉ
   cutamati uṟaṅkiviṭṭāḷ. at teyvam maṇi mēkalaiyait taṉ mantirattāl
   mayakkit taḻuvi yeṭuttukkoṇṭu vāṉ vaḻiyāka, teṉ ticaiyil muppatu
   yōcaṉait tolaivil kaṭal naṭuvaṇ irunta maṇipallavam eṉṉum oru tīvil
   kaṭaṟkarai yil maṇaṟparappil avaḷaik kiṭattiviṭṭu nīṅkiyatu.
   ----
   [1]. taṇiyā nōkkam - nīṅkāta kātal.
   [2]. puṟattōr vītiyil - veḷiyē ēṉai makkaḷ cellum teruvil.
   [3]. tāḻvayiṉ aṭaippōr -tōṇṭiya paḷḷaṅkaḷil putaippavar.
   [4]. iḷivuṭaṉ - aruvaruppōṭu. [5]. toḻuviḷippūcal - tuti muḻakkam.
   [6]. tiviḷik kūvum - ūḷaiyiṭum ōcaiyum.
   [7]. cāvōrp payirum- iṟappavarai aḻaikkum. kūkai - pērāntai.
   [8]. kurāl - kōṭṭāṉ.
   [9]. ūṉ talai tuṟṟiya āṇ talai - talai mūḷaiyai uṇavāka uṇṭa āṇ ṭalaip
   paṟavai. āṇ makaṉatu talai pōṉṟa vaṭivamuṭaiyatu ippaṟavai; ataṉāl
   itaṉai āṇṭalaippuḷ eṉṟaṉar.
   [10]. puṇari - alaikaḷ.
   [11]. īṟṟiḷam peṇṭir - makavīṉṟa iḷaiya maṭantaiyar.
   [12]. celvak kaḷḷāṭṭayarntu - celva mikutiyāl cerukkuṟṟu mayaṅki
   veṟuppavaikaḷaic ceytu.
   [13]. toṭṭu - tāṉ.
   [14]. kavaiya?. peyarttu - piḷavupaṭṭa aṭikaḷai iyakki. taṇṭāk kaḷippu
   - kuṟaiyāta makiḻcci.
   [15]. āruyir pōṉāl atu ceyta viṉaikkēṟpa vēṟē piṟappu eytum eṉṟa
   poruḷil aiyamuṇṭō, eṉṟatu: cettaniṉ makaṉatu uyir vēṟu piṟappai
   eytiyirukkum; ataṉai mīṭṭum taruvippatu eṉpatu muṭiyātu eṉṟavāṟu. |
   [16]. ār iṭar - poṟuppataṟ karitākiya tuṉpam.
   [17]. avalappaṭiṟṟurai - kēṭu viḷaivikkum poymmoḻi.
   [18]. āḻiyaṅkuṉṟam - cakkaravāḷakiri.
   [19]. nālvakai māperun tīvukaḷ, " mahāt vīpam nāṉku; ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu
   jamputvīpam' eṉpar putta nūlār.
   [20]. maṇṇīṭu - veṇ cutaiyāl ceyta vaṭivukaḷ.
   [21]. eyil - matil.
   -------

                          7. tuyileḻuppiya kātai

   maṇimēkalaiyaik kaippaṟṟa muṭiyātu, kaiyaṟṟut taṉ araṇ maṉaikku
   mīṇṭuvanta utayakumāraṉ, "ik kaṅkul kaḻintatum avaḷai eṉ
   kaiyakappaṭuttuvēṉ" eṉṟu eṇṇamiṭṭuk koṇṭu, aṉ ṟiravu kaṇṇuṟakkam
   peṟātu, taṉ paṭukkaiyil puraṇṭu koṇ ṭirun tāṉ. avaṉ kaṇmuṉṉē maṇimēkalā
   teyvam tōṉṟi, "maṉ ṉavaṉ makaṉē,
   kōlnilai tirintiṭiṉ, kōḷnilai tiriyum;
   kōḷ nilai tirintiṭiṉ, [1]māri vaṟaṅkūrum;
   mārivaṟaṅ kūriṉ, maṉ uyir illai;
   maṉṉuyir ellām maṇṇāḷ vēntaṉ
   taṉṉuyir eṉṉum takuti iṉṟākum
   [2] tavattiṟam pūṇṭōḷ taṉ mēl vaitta
   avattiṟam oḻika"
   eṉṟu uraittuviṭṭu nīṅki, uvavaṉam pukuntu aṅkē uṟaṅkik koṇṭirunta
   cutamatiyaik kaṇṭu avaḷatu tuyilai nīkkiṟṟu. avaḷ teḷinta kālai, avaḷ
   muṉṉē niṉṟu, " cutamati, añcēl, yāṉ maṇimēkalā teyvam. ivvūril
   naṭaipeṟum intiraviḻāvaik kāṇa vanta yāṉ, eṉ mantirattāl maṇimēkalaiyai
   mayakkik koṇṭupōy maṇipallavattil vaittuḷḷēṉ. avaḷ iṉṟaiya ēḻām nāḷ,
   taṉ paḻam piṟappai aṟintukoṇṭu iṅku varuvaḷ. avaḷukkup puttaṉatu
   nallaṟattaip piṉpaṟṟum nallūḻ vantiruk kiṟatu. avaḷ ivvūril taṉ
   urukkarantu ulaviyakālaiyum niṉ kaṇṇukkut taṉ uṇmai yuruvai oḷiyāḷ.
   itaṉai avaḷ tāy mātavikkut terivikka.
   tirai [3] irum pauvattut teyvamoṉ ṟuṇṭeṉak
   kōvalaṉ kūṟi ik koṭiyiṭai taṉṉai,eṉ
   [4]nāmam ceyta nal nāḷ naḷḷiruḷ
   kāmaṉ [5]kaiyaṟak [6]kaṭunavai yaṟukkum
   [7` māperun tavakkoṭi īṉ ṟaṉai eṉṟē
   naṉavē pōlak kaṉavakattu uraittēṉ;
   īṅku ivvaṇṇam āṅku avaṭku urai "
   eṉṟu colliviṭṭu maṟaintēkiyatu. cutamati tāṉ uṟaṅkik kiṭanta
   iṭattiṉiṉṟum eḻuntu cuṟṟumuṟṟum nōkkiṉāḷ. eṅkum ev vayirum uṟaṅkiṉa.
   iṭaiyiṭaiyē,
   [8] yāmam koḷpavar ēttoli yaravamum,
   # # #
   [9] niṟaiyaḻi yāṉai neṭuṅ kūviḷiyum,
   # # #
   ūr kāppāḷar eṟi [10] tuṭi yōtaiyum,
   putalvaraip payanta [11]puṉiṟu tīr kayakkam
   tīrviṉai makaḷir kuḷaṉāṭu aravamum,
   [12]valitta neñciṉ āṭavar iṉṟiyum,
   [13]pulikkaṇat taṉṉōr pūta catukkattuk
   koṭittēr vēntaṉ koṟṟam koḷ kaeṉa
   iṭikkural muḻakkattu [14]iṭumpali yōtaiyum
   īṟṟiḷam peṇṭir, āṟṟāp pālakar
   kaṭuñcūl makaḷir [15] neṭumpuṇ uṟṟōr
   tamtuyar keṭukkum mantira mākkaḷ
   maṉṟap pēymakaḷ vantu kaikkoḷka eṉa
   niṉṟeṟi paliyiṉ neṭuṅkural ōtaiyum
   piṟavum eḻuntu icaittaṉa. ivaṟṟāl uḷḷam kalaṅki naṭukkamuṟṟa cutamati,
   av vaṉattiṉ mēṟṟicaiyil uḷḷa ciṟu vaḻiyiṉūṭu, ayalatākiya cakkaravāḷak
   kōṭṭattai aṭaintāḷ.
   avviṭattē palarumpukumāṟu tiṟantavāyilaiyuṭaiya [16]ulakavaṟavi
   iruntatu. aṅkē ceṉṟa cutamati ataṉ orupuṭaiyē iruntaṉaḷ. avviṭattē
   niṉṟa tūṇil amaintirunta pāvai yoṉṟai uṭalākakkoṇṭu oru teyvam, avaḷai
   aḻaittu, " cutamati, nī muṉ piṟappil, acōtara nakarattu aracaṉāṉa
   iravivaṉmaṉ makaḷ. nī tuccayaṉ eṉṉum vēntaṟku maṉaivi. niṉakku
   muṟpiṟappil vīrai eṉpatu peyar. niṉakkut tamakkai yorutti yuṇṭu. avaḷ
   tārai eṉappaṭuvāḷ. avaḷ iṟantatu kēṭṭu nī uyirviṭṭāy. ippōtu nī niṉ muṉ
   piṟantāḷākiya tāraiyoṭu kūṭiyirukkiṉṟāy. iṉṟaiya ēḻām nāḷ niṉ paḻam
   piṟappiṉ taṅkai ilakkumi eṉpāḷ niṉ piṟappum taṉ piṟappum aṟintu
   varuvaḷ. nī añcaṟka" eṉṟu colliṟṟu. ic coṟkaḷāl cutamati tikaippuṇṭu
   añci maruṇṭirukkaiyil,
   [17]kāvalāḷar kaṇtuyil koḷḷa,
   tūmeṉ cēkkait tuyil kaṇ viḻippa,
   [18] valampuric caṅkam vaṟitu eḻuntārppa,
   pulampuric caṅkam poruḷoṭu muḻaṅka,
   [19] pukarmuka vāraṇam neṭuṅ kū viḷippa,
   poṟimayir vāraṇam kuṟuṅ kū viḷippa,
   [20] paṇai nilap puravi palaeḻun tālap
   [21] paṇai nilaip puḷḷum pala eḻuntāla,
   [22] pūmpoḻi lārkaip puḷḷoḷi ciṟappa,
   pūṅkoṭi yārkaip puḷḷoḷi ciṟappa,
   [23] kaṭavuṭ pīṭikaip pūppali kaṭaikoḷa,
   kalampakar pīṭikaip pūppali kaṭaikoḷa,
   [24] kuyiluvar kaṭaitoṟum paṇṇiyam paranteḻa
   koṭuppōr kaṭaitoṟum paṇṇiyam paranteḻa
   ūrtuyil eṭuppa, [25] uravu nīr aḻuvattuk
   [26]kār iruḷ cīttuk katiravaṉ muḷaittalum,
   cutamatikku accam nīṅkiṟṟu. avaḷ mayileṉa naṭantu mānakariṉ teruvaḻiyē
   ceṉṟu mā taviyai yaṭaintu nikaḻntatu muṟṟum vaḻu viṉṟik kūṟiṉāḷ.
   mātavi, atu kēṭṭu, maṇiyiḻanta nākampōl varuttam koṇṭaṉaḷ; cutamatiyō,
   maṇimēkalaiyiṉ pirivāṟṟātu uyiriḻanta uṭalpōlat tuyar uḻappāḷāyiṉaḷ.
   ------
   [1]. māri vaṟam kūrum - māri vaṟṟiviṭum; maḻai illaiyāy viṭum.
   [2]. tavattiṟam pūṇṭōḷ - tavattiṉ takutikoṇṭa maṇimēkalai .
   [3]. irumpauvam - periya kaṭal.
   [4]. nāmam ceyta nalnāḷ - peyar vaitta naṉṉāḷ,
   [5]. kaiyaṟā - ceyal aṟa. [6]. kaṭukavai - piṟavi nōy.
   [7]. māperuk tavakkoṭi - mikka periya tavattiṟam pūṇutaṟkuriya koṭi
   pōlum makaḷ.
   [8]. yāmam koḷpavar - nāḻikaik kaṇakkeṭuppōr.
   [9]. niṟaiyaḻiyāṉai neṭuṅkūviḷi - pōkkuvara viṉ ṟik kaṭṭuṇṭu kiṭattalāl
   uṇavu koḷḷātu varunti, pākar niṟuttum niṟai kaṭanta yāṉai kaḷ neṭitāy
   icaikkum piḷiṟal.
   [10]. tuṭi - pampai pōlum oruvakai icaikkaruvi.
   [11]. puṉiṟu tīr makaḷir - makavu peṟṟu vālāmai nīṅkum makaḷir; kayak
   kam tīr makaḷir - makavu peṟṟu varuttam nīṅkum makaḷir.
   kuṟippu:-makavu peṟṟa makaḷir pattām nāḷ iravil makappayanta mutiya .
   makaḷir puṭaicūḻac ceṉṟu mālaiyil nīrāṭal paṇṭaiyōr marapu.
   [12]. valitta neñciṉ āṭavar - pakaimai yiruttalāl atu kuṟitta neñciṉai
   yuṭaiya vīrar; valiya pakaivarkaḷ.
   [13]. pulikkaṇattu aṉṉōr - pulikkūṭṭam pōṉṟa vīrarkaḷ.
   [14]. iṭumpa.li yōtai - paliyiṭum ōtai - paliyiṭuvōr ceyyum
   perumuḻakkam.
   [15]. neṭum puṇ uṟṟōr - pōril periya puṇpaṭṭavar.
   [16]. ulaka aṟavi - ellāvakai makkaḷum vantu taṅku taṟkuriya aṟac
   cālai. |
   [17]. kāvalāḷar kaṇ tuyil koḷḷa - iravu muṟṟum kāval purinta vīrar
   viṭiyalil kaṇṇuṟakkam koḷḷa
   tūmeṉ cēkkait tuyil kaṇ viḻippa - tūya melliya paḷḷiyil tuyiṉṟa āṭavar
   makaḷir eṉṟa irupālār kaṇkaḷum viḻittukkoḷḷa.
   [18]3. valampuric caṅkam - valampuri ' eṉṉum caṅku. varitu eḻuntu ārppa
   - vaṟitē muḻaṅka. pulampuric caṅkam- pulavar kūṭṭam. poruḷoṭu muḻaṅka -
   poruḷ nirampiya moḻikaḷaic colli ōcai ceyya.
   [19]. pukarmuka vāraṇam - puḷḷi poruntiya paṭṭamaṇinta yāṉai, poṟimayir
   vāraṇam - poṟikaḷ poruntiya mayiriṉai yuṭaiya kōḻi.
   [20]. paṇai nilaippuravi - kutiraip pantiyil niṟkum kutiraikaḷ. āla -
   āṭa:
   [21]. paṇai nilaip puḷ - marakkiḷaiyil taṅkum paṟavaikaḷ. āla -muḻaṅka.
   [22]. pūmpoḻil - pūñcōlaikaḷil. ārkai - nirampiya. puḷ - paṟavai kaḷ.
   pūṅkoṭiyār - pūṅkoṭi pōlum makaḷir. kaippuḷ - kaiyil aṇinta vaḷaikaḷ.
   [23]. kaṭavuṭ pīṭikai . teyvaṅkaṭkuriya pīṭaṅkaḷ. pūppali - aḻakiya
   paṭaiccalkaḷ. kaṭaikoḷa - muṟṟuppeṟa.
   kalam pakar pīṭikai - aṇikalam viṟkum kaṭaitteru. kaṭaiyiṉ vāyi. lukkup
   pūccorintu poṭṭiṭṭup pūcai ceytal.
   [24]. kuyiluvar kaṭai - tōṟkaruvi vācippōruṭaiya vīṭṭu muṟṟam. paṇ iyam
   - paṇṇamainta icai,
   koṭuppōrkaṭai - koṭaivaḷḷal vīṭṭu muṟṟam. paṇṇiyam - pala paṇṭaṅkaḷ.
   [25]. uravu nīr aḻuvam - virinta nīrmikka kaṭal.
   [26]. kār iruḷ cīttu - mikka iruḷaik keṭuttu.

                  8. maṇipallavattut tuyaruṟṟa kātai

   maṇimēkalā teyvattāl taṉittu viṭappeṟṟa maṇimēkalai maṇipallavat tīviṉ
   kaṭaṟkaraiyil, tuyileḻuntu cuṟṟumuṟṟum pārkkaluṟṟāḷ. taṉṉō ṭirunta
   cutamatiyaiyum avaḷ kaṇṭilaḷ. avaḷ kaṇṇetirē,
   [1]ñāḻal ōṅkiya [2]tāḻkaṇ acumpiṉ
   āmpalum kuvaḷaiyum tāmpuṇarntu mayaṅki,
   vaṇṭuṇa malarnta [3] kuṇṭu nīr ilañci
   [4] muṭakkāl puṉṉaiyum maṭaṟpūn tāḻaiyum
   [5`veyil varavu oḻitta payilpūm pantar
   tōṉṟiṟṟu. aṅkē tōṉṟiya aṉaittum avaṭkup putumaiyā kavē tōṉṟiṉa. ciṟitu
   kālattē viṭiyaliṉ veḷḷiruḷ nīṅka, ñāyiṟu kiḻakkē eḻuntatu. maṇimēkalai,
   avviṭattai viyantu nōkki,
   "uvavaṉa maruṅkiṉil ōriṭam kol itu !
   cutamati! oḷittāy; tuyaram ceytaṉai;
   naṉavō, kaṉavō eṉpatai aṟiyēṉ;
   maṉam [6]naṭuk kuṟū um; māṟṟam tārāy,
   val iruḷ kaḻintatu; mātavi mayaṅkum;
   [7] elvaḷai vārāy; viṭṭakaṉ ṟaṉaiyō?
   [8] viñcaiyil tōṉṟiya viḷaṅkiḻai maṭavāḷ,
   vañcam ceytaṉaḷ kollō ! aṟiyēṉ
   orutaṉi añcuveṉ, tiruvē vā"
   eṉac collikkoṇṭu, cutamatiyait tēṭit tiriyalāṉāḷ. nīrt tuṟaikaḷ, maṇaṟ
   kuṉṟaṅkaḷ, perum poḻilkaḷ ākiya ivviṭam tōṟum tēṭiṉāḷ. eṅkum avaḷaik
   kāṇāmaiyāl, maṇimēkalai peritum accamum avalamum koṇṭu,
   9 kural talaik kūntal kulaintu piṉ vīḻa
   araṟṟiṉaḷ kūuy aḻutaṉaḷ ēṅki
   [10] vīḻtuyar eytiya [11]viḻumak kiḷaviyiṉ,
   tāḻtuyar uṟu vōḷ [12]tantaiyai uḷḷi
   “em itiṟ paṭuttum [13]vevviṉai uruppak
   [14]kōṟṟoṭi mātaroṭu vēṟṟunā ṭaṭaintu
   vaivāḷ [15]uḻanta maṇippūṇ akalattu
   aiyāvō"
   eṉṟu aḻalāṉāḷ. avvāṟu aḻupavaḷ muṉṉar, puttaṉākiya peruntava muṉivaṉ
   iruntu aṟam uraikkum taruma pīṭikaiyoṉṟu tōṉ ṟuvatāyiṟṟu. iḵtu,
   aṟavōṟku amainta ācaṉa meṉṟē
   [16] naṟumalar allatu piṟamaram coriyātu;
   paṟavaiyum mutirciṟai pāṅku ceṉṟu atirātu;
   tēvarkōṉ iṭṭa māmaṇip pīṭikai,
   [17]piṟappu viḷaṅku aviroḷi aṟattakai ācaṉam
   oru kālattē, nākavēntar iruvar ip pīṭikaiyaik kaṇṭu, ovvoruvarum
   tamakkē uriyateṉak karutikkoṇṭu eṭukkaluṟṟu āṟṟārāyiṉar. āyiṉum,
   ataṉpāṟ koṇṭa ācaiyāl iruva rum oruvarōṭoruvar peruntāṉaiyuṭaṉ neruṅkip
   pōr cey taṉar. atupōḻtu, avar muṉ puttar perumāṉ tōṉṟi, [18]"iruñ ceru
   oḻimiṉ; ītu ematu," eṉṟu colli, ataṉmītu iruntu aṟaṅ kūṟiṉaṉ. itu putta
   pīṭikaiyiṉ varalāṟu.
   ---
   [1].'ñāḻal - oruvakaik koṉṟai; pulinakakkoṉṟai .
   [2]. tāḻkaṇ acumpu - āḻnta nīr nilaic cēṟṟil.
   [3]. kuṇṭu nīr ilañci - āḻamāṉa nīraiyuṭaiya poykaik karai.
   [4]. muṭakkāl puṉṉai - vaḷainta aṭimarattaiyuṭaiya puṉṉai.
   [5]. veyil varavu oḻitta-veyil nuḻaiyātapaṭi neruṅkat taḻaitta.
   [6]. naṭukku ṟū um - naṭukkam koḷḷum.
   [7]. elvaḷai - viḷaṅkukiṉṟa vaḷaiyiṉai yuṭaiyavaḷē.
   [8`. viñcaiyil tōṉṟiya - vittaiyāl tōṉṟiṉa.
   [9]. kural talaik kūntal - pūṅkottukkaḷaic cūṭiya kūntal.
   [10]. vīḻtuyar - mikka tuyaram. [11]. viḻumakkiḷavi - tuyarattaip
   pulappaṭukkum col.
   [12]. tantai - kōvalaṉ. [13]. vevviṉai uruppa - tīviṉai varutta.
   [14]. kōṟṟoṭi- mātar - tiraṇṭavaḷai yaṇinta kātaliyākiya kaṇṇaki
   [15]. uḻanta - veṭṭuṇṭu varuntiya.
   [16]. naṟumalar allatu maram piṟacoriyātu - 5ṟiya pūkkaḷait tavira,
   ēṉai evaṟṟaiyum maraṅkaḷ utirppatillai.
   [17]. piṟappu viḷaṅku aviroḷi - aṟattakai ācaṉam- aṟavōr kāṇ pārkkup
   paḻampiṟappu viḷaṅkutaṟku ētuvākiya oḷi poruntiya vīṟ virukkum
   aḻakuṭaiya ācaṉam.
   [18]. iruñ ceru - mikka pōr.

             9. pīṭikai kaṇṭu piṟappu uṇarnta kātai

   ivvaṇṇam tōṉṟiya putta pīṭikaiyaik kaṇṭatum maṇi mēkalai taṉṉaimaṟantu,
   ataṉ arukē ceṉṟu, talaimēṟ kaikuvittu uvakaiyāṟ kaṇṇīr corintu, vaṇaṅki
   ataṉai mummuṟai valam vantu vaḻipaṭṭu niṉṟāḷ. annilaiyē avaṭkum paḻam
   piṟappiṉ ceytikaḷ niṉaiviṟku vantaṉa. uṭaṉē, avaḷ,
   [1]" toḻutakai mātava, [2] tuṇiporuḷ uṇarntōy!
   [3] kāyaṅ karaiyil nī uraittatai yellām,
   [4] vāyē ākutal mayakkaṟa uṇarntēṉ.
   kāntāra nāṭṭup pūruvatēya maṉṉaṉāṉa mattipati eṉṉum aracaṉ taṉṉāṭṭiṟku
   nikaḻavirunta perun tīṅkiṉaip piramatarumaṉ eṉṉum muṉivaṉāl uṇarntu,
   taṉṉāṭṭu makkaḷuṭaṉ iṭavayam eṉṉum taṉ nakariṉ nīṅki, avanti nōkkic
   ceṉṟu koṇṭiruk tāṉ. orukāl avaṉ kāyaṅkarai eṉṉum yāṟṟiṉ karaiyil pāṭi
   yamaittut taṅkiyirukkaiyil, am muṉivaṉ kūṟiyavāṟē nāṭu [5]nila
   naṭukkattāl keṭṭaḻiyak kēṭṭaṟintu varuntiṉaṉ. appōḻtu ammuṉivaṉ mīṇṭum
   vantu avvaracaṉukkum, kuṭimakkaṭkum nal laṟam uraittaṉaṉ. annāḷil,
   aravak kaṭaloli acōtaram, āḷum
   iravi vaṉmaṉ oruperun tēvi,
   [6] alattakac cīṟaṭi amutapati vayiṟṟu
   ilakkumi eṉṉum peyarpeṟṟup piṟantēṉ."
   attipatiyeṉum aracaṉ peruntēvi
   cittipuram āḷum cītaraṉ tirumakaḷ,
   nīlapati yeṉum nēriḻai vayiṟṟil,
   kālai ñāyiṟṟuk katirpōl tōṉṟiya
   irākulaṉ [7]taṉakkup pukkēṉ; avaṉoṭu
   [8] parāvaru marapiṉ niṉ pātam paṇitalum
   eṭṭiru nāḷil ivvirākulaṉ taṉṉait
   [9] tiṭṭi viṭam uṇum; cel uyir pōṉāl,
   tīyaḻal avaṉoṭu cēyiḻai mūḻkuvai;
   [10] ētu nikaḻcci īṅkiṉṟu; ātaliṉ,
   [11] kavēra kaṉṉip peyaroṭu viḷaṅkiya
   [12] tavākkaḷi mūtūrc ceṉṟu piṟappeytuti;
   aṇiyiḻai, niṉakku ōr [13]aruntuyar varum nāḷ,
   maṇimēkalā teyvam vantu tōṉṟi,
   aṉṟu appatiyil āriruḷ eṭuttut
   teṉ ticai maruṅkil ōr tīviṭai vaittalum,
   [14] vēka ventiṟal nākanāṭ ṭaracar
   [15]ciṉamācu oḻittu [16]maṉamācu tīrttu āṅku
   [17]aṟaccevi tiṟantu [18]maṟaccevi aṭaittup
   [19] piṟavippiṇi maruttuvaṉ iruntaṟam uraikkuma
   [20] tiruntoḷi yācaṉam ceṉṟu kai toḻuti;
   aṉṟaip pakalē uṉ piṟap puṇarntīṅku
   iṉṟu yāṉ uraitta uraiteḷivāy eṉa
   uraittaṉai; atukēṭṭu, eṉ kātalaṉ piṟappum kāṭṭāyō eṉṟu vēṇṭiṉēṉ.
   atupōtu, at tīviṭai niṉṉaikkoṇṭu ceṉṟa teyvam tōṉṟi,avaṉaik kāṭṭi, '
   ivaṉē niṉ kaṇavaṉ' eṉṟu collumeṉṟu moḻintaṉai. at teyvam iṉṉum
   vantilatē" eṉṟu uraittu niṉṟu aḻutāḷ.
   ------
   [1]. " toḻutakai mātava '- itu mutal maṇimēkalai muṉṉaip piṟavi yil taṉ
   muṉtōṉṟi nallavai kūṟiya piramatarumaṉ eṉṉum muṉivaṉai ap pīṭikaiyil
   etirpeytu koṇṭu kūṟukiṉ ṟāḷ. toḻutakai mātava - yāvarum toḻattakka
   periya tavattaiyuṭaiya piramataruma muṉiva.
   [2]. tuṇiporuḷ - meypporuḷ. [3]. kāyaṅkarai - oru yāṟu.
   [4]. vāy - uṇmai . [5]. nilanaṭukkam - pūkampam.
   [6]. alattakaccīṟaṭi amutapati - cempañciyūṭṭiya ciṟiya aṭikaḷai
   yuṭaiya amutapati eṉka.
   [7]. taṉakkuppukkēṉ - taṉakku maṉai viyāyiṉēṉ.
   [8]. parāvarum marapu - vaḻipaṭum muṟaimai.
   [9]. tiṭṭiviṭam - kaṇṇil viṭam uṭaiyatoru pāmpu.
   [10]. ētu nikaḻcci īṅku iṉṟu - ñāṉampiṟattaṟkuriya takuti ippiṟappil
   illai.
   [11]. kavērakaṉṉi - kāviri.
   [12]. tavākkaḷimū tūr - keṭāta celvak kaḷippiṉai yuṭaiya paḻaiya
   vūrākiya kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam.
   [13]. aruntuyar - utayakumaraṉāl maṇimēkalaik kuṇṭāvatu pōṉ ṟirukkum
   tuṉpam.
   [14]. vēkaventiṟal - ciṉamum mikka tiṟalumuṭaiya,
   [15]. ciṉa mācu - ciṉa mākiya kuṟṟam.
   [16]. maṉamācu - maṉa vaḻukkākiya kāmamum mayakkamum.
   [17]. aṟaccevi - aṟattaik kēṭṭaṟ kuriyakātu.
   [18]. maṟaccevi - pāvattaik kēṭkum kātu.
   [19]. piṟavippiṇi maruttuvaṉ - piṟavi nōykku maruntākiya ñāṉa maḷikkum
   puttaṉ.
   [20]. tiruntoḷi ācaṉam - aḻakiya oḷi viḷaṅkum pīṭikai.

                        10. mantiram koṭutta kātai

   maṇimēkalai taṉ paḻam piṟappuṇarntu niṟkaiyil, avaḷai at tīvil
   viṭṭucceṉṟa maṇimēkalai teyvam, ap putta pīṭikaiyaip puttaṉākavē
   karuti,
   uyirka ḷellām [1]uṇarvu pāḻākip
   poruḷ vaḻaṅku [2]cevittuḷai tūrntu aṟiviḻanta
   [3]vaṟantalai ulakattu [4]aṟampāṭu ciṟakkac
   [5]cuṭarvaḻakkaṟṟut taṭumāṟu kālaiyōr
   iḷavaḷa ñāyiṟu tōṉṟiyatu eṉṉa,
   nīyō tōṉṟiṉai; niṉ aṭi paṇintēṉ;
   nīyē āki, niṟku amainta ivvācaṉam
   [6]nāmicai vaittēṉ; talaimicaik koṇṭēṉ,
   eṉṟu colli vaṇaṅki niṉṟāḷ. avaḷaik kaṇṭatum, maṇi mēkalai, avaḷ aṭiyiṟ
   paṇintu, " niṉ tiruvaruḷāl eṉ paḻam piṟappai uṇarntēṉ; eṉ peruṅ kaṇavaṉ
   yāṅku uḷaṉ?" eṉṟu kēṭṭaṉaḷ. maṇimēkalā teyvam, avaṭku avaḷatu vara lāṟu
   kūṟattoṭaṅki, " nī muṟpiṟappil ilakkumi yeṉṉum peya ruṭaṉiruntāy.
   niṉakkuk kaṇavaṉ irākulaṉ eṉpavaṉ. oru kāl avaṉ niṉṉoṭukūṭi oru
   poḻiliṭattē taṅkiyirukkaiyil, avaṉōṭu nī ūṭiṉai. avaṉ niṉṉaip pulavi
   tīrttaṟku aṭivīḻntu vaṇaṅkiṉaṉ. appōḻtil, cātu cakkaraṉ eṉṉum muṉivaṉ
   vāṉ vaḻiyāka vantu niṟka, avaṉaik kaṇṭu nī vaṇaṅkiṉai; irākulaṉ
   uṇmaiyuṇarātu niṉṉai vekuṇṭāṉ. avaṉukku nī muṉi vaṉa tuciṟappuk kūṟi
   vaṇaṅkuvittaṉai. piṉpu am muṉivaṉai mīṭṭum vaṇaṅki,
   amara,kēḷ: [7]niṉ tamar alam āyiṉum
   antīntaṇṇīr amutoṭu koṇarkēṉ;
   uṇṭi; yām [8]uṉ kuṟippiṉam
   eṉṟu uraittu uṇavu koṭuttu ōmpiṉai; aṉṟu nī ceyta aṟa viṉai niṉṉaip
   piriyātu niṉ piṟappai aṟuppataṟkut tuṇai yāyiṟṟu.
   uvavaṉa maruṅkil uṉpāl tōṉṟiya
   utayakumaraṉ avaṉ, uṉ irākulaṉ;
   āṅku avaṉ aṉṟiyum avaṉ pāl uḷḷa
   [9]nīṅkāt taṉmai niṉakkum uṇṭākaliṉ,
   [10] kanta cāliyiṉ kaḻiperu vittu ōr .
   [11] ventuku veṇkaḷar [12]vīḻvatu pōṉm eṉa
   aṟattiṉ vittu āṅku ākiya uṉṉai ōr
   [13] tiṟappaṭaṟku ētuvāc cēyiḻai! ceytēṉ;
   iṉṉum kēḷ : niṉakku muṉ piṟanta tārai, vīrai eṉṟa iru maṭantaiyarum
   tattam kaṇavaṉmāruṭaṉ kaṅkaippēryāṟṟaṅ karai yil
   viḷaiyāṭikkoṇṭirukkaiyil, aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷ vantu cērnta ṉar. avarai im
   maṭantaiyar vaṇaṅki vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. avar kaṭku, aṭikaḷ, " maṅkaimīr, yāṉ
   puttaṉ iruntu aṟam uraitta kuṉ ṟiṉ arukil uḷḷa pātapaṅkaya malaiyait
   toḻutu valamkoḷḷa vantēṉ; nīvirum ceṉṟu vaḻipaṭumiṉ " eṉṟu
   uraittēkiṉar. avarum avvāṟē ceytu ip piṟappil mā taviyum
   cutamatiyumāyiṉar," eṉṟu ivvāṟu kūṟiya at teyvam mēlum kūṟattoṭaṅki,
   [14] " aṟipiṟap puṟṟaṉai, [15]aṟampā ṭaṟintaṉai;
   [16] piṟavaṟam uraippōr peṟṟiyum kēṭkuvai;
   palvēṟu camayap [17]paṭiṟṟurai ellām
   [18] alliyaṅ kōtai kēṭkum annāḷ,
   iḷaiyaḷ, vaḷai yōḷ eṉṟu uṉakku yāvarum
   viḷai poruḷ uraiyār; vēṟṟuru eytavum
   [19]antaram tiriyavum ākkum [20] ivvaruntiṟal
   mantiram koḷka" eṉa vāymaiyiṉ ōti,
   muṭivil, " puttaṉaruḷiya tiruvaṟam eytutal orutalai " iṉi, nī . ip
   putta pīṭikaiyai vaṇaṅki niṉ ūrkkuc celka" eṉṟu moḻintu antarattē
   cellat toṭaṅkiṟṟu.
   at teyvam ciṟitu tolaivu ceṉṟu, caṭṭeṉat tirumpi vantu, " niṉakku oṉṟu
   colla maṟantēṉ, kēḷ," eṉṟu colli,
   ciṟanta [21]koḷkaic cēyiḻai, kēḷāy:
   makkaḷ yākkai [22] uṇaviṉ piṇṭam;
   ipperum mantiram irumpaci aṟukkum
   eṉṟu oru mantirattai vaḻaṅki, vicumpu vaḻiyāka mēṟ celvatā yiṟṟu.
   -----
   [1]. uṇarvu pāḻāki - aṟivu keṭṭu.
   [2]. cevit tuḷai - ceviyil naṟporuḷaik kēṭkum tiṟam.
   [3]. vaṟantalaiyulakam - vaṟumaiyuṟṟa ulakil (aṟivu vaṟumai).
   [4]. aṟam pāṭuciṟakka - aṟam tōṉṟi viḷaṅka.
   [5]. cuṭar vaḻakku aṟṟu taṭumāṟukālai - veyilavaṉ tōṉṟuvatiṉṟi eppōtum
   iruḷēyāki yāvum mayaṅkum kālattu.
   [6]. nāmicai vaittēṉ - nāvāl paravukiṉṟēṉ.
   [7]. niṉ tamar alam - yām uṉakkuc ciṟanta aṉpuṭaiyavarkaḷallēm.
   [8]. uṉ kuṟippiṉam - uṉ kuṟippiṉpaṭiyē niṉṟu oḻukuvēm.
   [9]. nīṅkāttaṉmai - eppōtum niṉaittuk koṇṭirukkum ' taṉmai .
   [10]. kantacāli - mikka maṇamuḷḷa oruvakai tel.
   [11]. ventuku veṇkaḷar - ventu karintu utirum veṇmaiyāṉa kaḷar nilam.
   [12]. vīḻvatupōṉm - vitaikkappaṭuvatu pōlum; nī utayakumaraṉaic
   cērvatu, kantacāliyiṉ vitaiyaik kaḷar nilattē vitaippatu pōlap
   payaṉaṟṟuppōm.
   [13]. tiṟappaṭaṟku ētuvā - nalla neṟiyiṟ celvataṟku ētuvāka.
   [14]. aṟipiṟappu - aṟitaṟku vēṇṭiyavaṟṟai aṟiyum piṟappai.
   [15]. aṟampāṭu - aṟattiṉ tōṟṟam.
   [16]: piṟavaṟam uraippōr peṟṟi - piṟacamaya vātikaḷiṉ camaya aṟaṅkaḷ.
   [17]. paṭiṟṟurai - poyyurai. [18]. alliyaṅkōtai - maṇimēkalaiyē.
   [19]. antaram - vāṉam. [20]. aruntiṟal - mikka vaṉmai .
   [21]. koḷkai - oḻukkam; viratamumām.
   [22]. uṇaviṉ piṇṭam - uṇaviṉālākiya tacaippiṇṭam.
   -------------

                       11. pāttiram peṟṟa kātai

   maṇimēkalā teyvam nīṅkiya piṉ maṇimēkalai eḻuntu maṇi pallavattuḷ
   ceṟiyat tōṉṟiya veṇmaṇaṟ kuṉṟukaḷ, viripūñ cōlaikaḷ mutaliya kāṭcikku
   iṉiya iṭaṅkaḷaik kaṇṭuvantaṉaḷ. ivvāṟu oru kāvatat tolaivu ivaḷ
   tirintuvarukaiyil, ava ḷetirē tīvatilakai eṉpavaḷ teyvak kōlattuṭaṉ
   tōṉṟi ṉaḷ. tīvatilakai maṇimēkalaiyiṉ taṉimaikaṇṭu, [1]" ilaṅku toṭi
   nallāy, nī yār?" eṉṟu viṉaviṉaḷ. avaṭku maṇimēkalai,
   eppiṟap pakattuḷ yār nī eṉṟatu?
   [2]poṟkoṭi yaṉṉāy, poruntik kēḷāy :
   pōya piṟaviyil [3]pūmiyaṅ kiḻavaṉ
   irākulaṉ maṉaiyāṉ; ilakkumi [4]eṉpēṉ;
   [5]āya piṟaviyil āṭalaṅ kaṇikai
   mātavi īṉṟa maṇimēkalai yāṉ;
   eṉ peyart teyvam īṅku eṉaik koṇara, im
   [6] maṉ perum pīṭikai eṉ piṟap puṇarntēṉ;
   īṅku eṉ varavu itu; īṅku eytiya payaṉ itu,
   eṉṟu taṉ varalāṟukūṟi, at tīvatilakaiyiṉ varalāṟṟai viṉa viṉaḷ.
   atukēṭṭa tīvatilakai, "ittīviṟku ayalē irattiṉat tīvu eṉpatoṉṟuṇṭu,
   ataṉkaṇ camantam eṉṉum malaiyoṉ ṟuḷatu. ataṉ ucciyil puttar perumāṉatu
   tiruvaṭi amain tiruk kiṟatu. ataṉait toḻutu valaṅkoṇṭu vanta yāṉ,
   iṅkuḷḷa putta pīṭikaikku vantēṉ. eṉṉai intiraṉ iṅkē irutti ita ṉaik
   kāvalpurintu varumāṟu kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭāṉ. yāṉum atu mutal iṅkē itaṉaik
   kāttu varukiṉṟēṉ; eṉ peyar tīvatilakai eṉ patu ” eṉṟāḷ.
   tīvatilakaiyiṉ varalāṟukēṭṭa maṇimēkalai maṉam makiḻ kaiyil, at
   tīvatilakai mēlum kūṟaluṟṟup, " puttar perumāṉ uraitta nallaṟattai
   yuṇarntu, avaṉ vaḻaṅkiya nōṉpiṉai mēṟ koṇṭavar ip pīṭikaiyaik kaṇṭu
   toḻutu tam paṇṭaip piṟappai aṟintu koḷvar," eṉṟu uraittāḷ. ataṉ piṉ, ip
   pīṭikaiyiṉ muṉṉē kōmukiyeṉṉum nīrnilai yoṉṟu uḷatu. ataṉkaṇ, āṇṭutōṟum
   vaikācit tiṅkaḷ patiṉāṉkām nāḷā kiya vicākattaṉṟu, amutacurapi eṉṉum
   uṇkalamoṉṟu tōṉ ṟuvatuṇṭu. innāḷ annāḷākum. nī celvāyāyiṉ, ak kalam
   niṉkaiyiṭattēvantu poruntum, atu muṉṉāḷil āputtiraṉ kaiyil iruntatu.
   āṅku atiṟpeyta [7]āruyir maruntu
   vāṅkunar kaiyakam varuttuta lallatu,
   tāṉ [8]tolaivillāt takaimaiyatākum;
   naṟumalark kōtai ! niṉ [9] ūrāṅkaṇ
   aṟavaṇaṉ taṉ pāl kēṭkuvai itaṉ tiṟam
   eṉṟu moḻintāḷ. maṇimēkalaiyum uṭaṉē eḻuntu ceṉṟu pāta pīṭikaiyait
   toḻutu valaṅkoṇṭu, kōmukiyaiyum av vāṟē vaṇaṅkittoḻutu niṉṟāḷ. ciṟitu
   pōḻtil, tīvatilakai moḻinta vaṇṇamē, amuta curapiyeṉṉum uṇkalam tōṉṟi,
   avaḷ kaiyakattēvantu poruntiṟṟu.
   pāttiram peṟṟa paintoṭi maṭavāḷ
   [10] māttirai yiṉṟi maṉamakiḻ veyti,
   [11] māraṉai vellum vīra, niṉṉaṭi,
   [12] tīneṟik kaṭumpakai kaṭintōy, niṉṉaṭi,
   piṟarkku aṟam muyalum periyōy, niṉṉaṭi,
   [13] tuṟakkam vēṇṭāt tollōy, niṉṉaṭi,
   [14] eṇ piṟakkoḻiya iṟantōy, niṉṉaṭi,
   [15 kaṇ piṟarkku aḷikkum kaṇṇōy, niṉṉaṭi,
   tīmoḻik kaṭaitta ceviyōy, niṉṉaṭi,
   [16] vāymoḻi ciṟanta nāvōy, niṉṉaṭi.
   [17] narakar tuyar keṭa naṭappōy, niṉṉaṭi
   [18] urakar tuyaram oḻippōy, niṉṉaṭi
   vaṇaṅkuta lallatu vāḻttal, eṉ nāviṟku
   aṭaṅkātu
   eṉṟu ivvāṟu maṇimēkalai paraviṉāḷ. tīvatilakaiyum ap pāta pīṭikaiyaip
   paṇintu pōṟṟi, maṇimēkalaikku avvuṇkalattiṉ uyarvu collat toṭaṅki,
   "kuṭippiṟap paḻikkum, [19]viḻuppam kollum,
   piṭitta kalvip perumpuṇai [20] viṭūum,
   nāṇ aṇi kaḷaiyum, āṇ eḻil citaikkum,
   pūṇ mulai mātaroṭu [21]puṟaṅkaṭai niṟuttum
   pacippiṇi eṉṉum pāvi; atu tīrttōr,
   [22] icaic col aḷavaikku eṉ nā nimirātu "
   eṉṟu coṉṉāḷ. piṉpu, orukālattē peru vaṟam ulakil tōṉṟi, varutta,
   evvuyirum pacippiṇiyāl peruntuyar uḻappa, kōcika muṉivaṉ, pacimikutiyāl
   nāyūṉait tiṉṉa muyaṉṟu, ataṉaippeṟṟu, muṟaimaippaṭi toṭakkattē
   intiraṉukkup paṭaittup piṉ tāṉ uṇṇaluṟṟāṉ. uṭaṉē intiraṉ tōṉṟi
   maḻaivaḷam curantāṉ. ataṉ piṉpē ulakil vaciyum vaḷamum perukiṉa. at
   tēvaṉum nalla ciṟappaip peṟṟāṉ.
   [23]āṟṟunarkku aḷippōr, [24] aṟavilai pakarvōr,
   [25]āṟṟā mākkaḷ arumpaci kaḷai vōr,
   mēṟṟē ulakil [26] meynneṟi vāḻkkai;
   [27] maṇ tiṇi ñālattu vāḻvōrk kellām,
   uṇṭi koṭuttōr uyirkoṭut tōrē,
   ivvāṟu tīvatilakai pacittōrkku uṇavu nalkum aṟattuṟai yiṉ pāṅkiṉai
   uraikka, maṇimēkalai, "muṟpiṟappil, yāṉ cātu cakkara muṉivaṉukku
   uṇavūṭṭiya aṟappayaṉāl ip piṟappil ic curapiyiṉaiyaṭaivēṉāyiṉēṉ. niṟka,
   in nāvalantīvil veyileṉa muṉiyātu puyaleṉa maṭiyātu paci nōyāl varunti,
   piṟar piṟar illantōṟum ceṉṟu irappōr palar uḷar. avarkku uṇavu tantu
   avar mukam kuḷirvataik kāṇa vēṭkiṉṟēṉ" eṉṟu urait tāḷ. avaṭku at
   tīvatilakaiyum, " aṉṉāy, ic curapitāṉum, aruḷ mikavuṭaiya
   ciṟantōrkkallatu, cevvaṉam uṇavu curavātu; iḵtu ataṉ iyalpu; itaṉai
   muṉpē colla maṟantēṉ" eṉṟāḷ.
   ciṟitu ivvāṟu pēciyiruntapiṉ maṇimēkalai tīvatilakaipāl viṭai
   peṟṟukkoṇṭu, putta pīṭikaiyai valam vantu vaṇaṅki, kāvirippūm
   paṭṭiṉattiṟku vāṉvaḻiyāka vantu cērntaṉaḷ. ivaḷ varutaṟkuḷ, nāṭkaḷ ēḻu
   ceṉṟaṉa. " maṇimēkalā tey vam uraittavāṟu iṉṉum maṇimēkalai vantilaḷē"
   eṉṟu mātavi yum cutamatiyum maṉam mayaṅkiyiruntaṉar. caṭēreṉa
   maṇimēkalai avar muṉṉē vantu tōṉṟi niṉṟaṉaḷ. avviruvarum avaḷait taḻuvi
   makiḻcci mikkaṉar. avarkaṭku maṇimēkalai, nikaḻntatu muṟṟum kūṟi, avar
   tam paḻam piṟappaiyum eṭuttōtaluṟṟu,
   iravi vaṉmaṉ oru perumakaḷē,
   [28]turakat tāṉait tuccayaṉ tēvi,
   amutapati vayiṟṟu [29]aritiṉ tōṉṟit
   [30] tavvaiya rākiya tāraiyum vīraiyum
   [31]avvaiya rāyiṉīr nummaṭi toḻutēṉ;
   vāyvatāka māṉiṭa yākkaiyiṉ
   tīviṉai yaṟukkum ceytavam numakku īṅku
   aṟavaṇa vaṭikaḷ tampāl peṟumiṉ
   ceṟitoṭi nallīr umpiṟappu īṅkiḵtu
   eṉṟu colliviṭṭu, " iḵtō, eṉ kaiyiluḷḷa amuta curapi paṇṭu āputtiraṉ
   kaiyakat tiruntatākum; itaṉai nīvirum toḻu miṉ " eṉṟu moḻintāḷ.
   avarkaḷum avvaṇṇamē ceytaṉar. piṟaku mūvarum aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷaik kaṇṭu
   vaṇaṅkaṟkuc ceṉṟaṉar.
   -----
   [1]. ilaṅku toṭi - viḷaṅkukiṉṟa vaḷaiyiṉaiyaṇinta.
   [2]. poṟkoṭi - kāmavallikkoṭi. [3]. pūmiyaṅ kiḻavaṉ - aracaṉ.
   [4]. eṉpēṉ - eṉṟu peyar kūṟappaṭuvēṉ.
   [5]. āya piṟaviyil - ippōtu ākiya ip piṟappil.
   [6]. maṉ perum pīṭikai - mikka perumaiyāṉa pāta pīṭikaiyāl.
   [7]. āruyir maruntu - cōṟu. [8]. tolaivu - kuṟaital.
   [9]. ūr āṅkaṇ - ūriṭattē .
   [10]. māttirai - aḷavu .
   [11]. māraṉ - aṟattiṟkumāṟāṉa ācaikaḷai maṉattil eḻuppum oru tēvaṉ.
   ivaṉ maṉmataṉ allaṉ.
   [12]. tīneṟik kaṭumpakai - pāva vaḻikaḷākiya mikka pakai. pāvaneṟi
   oruvaṉaip pakaivaṉpōlat tuṉpattukkuḷḷākkutaliṉ " kaṭumpakai ' yeṉṟār,
   [13]. aḻivillāta niruvāṇattai virumputalāl, aḻiyum iyalpiṉa tāṉa
   tuṟakkam vēṇṭappaṭātāyiṟṟu.
   [14]. eṇ piṟakku oḻiya iṟantōy - māntar eṇṇamuṭiyāta uyar nilai
   yaṭaintōy. piṟakku oḻiya - piṟpaṭa.
   [15]. kaṇ - ñāṉakkaṇ . kaṇṇōy - ñāṉiyē. intiraṉukkuk kaṇ ṇiraṇṭum
   koṭutta varalāṟu uṇmaiyiṉ, kaṇṇaik koṭuttōy eṉ ṟumām.
   [16]. vāymoḻi - meymmai . [17]. narakar tuyar - narakar eṉpavarkkuṟṟa
   tuṉpam.
   [18]. urakar - nākar. ivar paṇṭaiyil pāmpu vaṇakkam purinta āriyar
   kūṭṭattu oru piriviṉar eṉṟum kūṟupa.
   [19]. viḻuppam - perumai. [20]. viṭū um - nīkkum.
   [21]. puṟaṅkaṭai - vāyiṟpuṟattē. [22]. icai - pukaḻ.
   [23]. āṟṟunarkku aḷippōr - piṟar ceyyum mikaiyiṉaip poṟuk kum
   vaṉmaiyākiya celvarkku utavi ceypavar.
   [24]. aṟavilai pakarvōr - payaṉ nōkki utavi ceypavarāvar.
   [25]. āṟṟāmākkaḷ - mikka vaṟiyar.
   [26]. meynneṟi vāḻkkai - meyyākiya aṟaneṟikkaṭ celuttap paṭum vāḻkkai .
   [27]. maṇ tiṇi ñālattu - aṇukkaḷ ceṟinta nilavulakattil.
   [28]. turakattāṉai - kutiraippaṭai. [29]. aritiṉ tōṉṟi - arumaiyākap
   piṟantu.
   [30]. tavvaiyar - tamakkaiyar. [31]. avvaiyar - tāymār.
   [31]. peṟumiṉ - aṟintukoṇmiṉ.
   -----------

                      12. aṟavaṇart toḻuta kātai

   maṇipallavattiliruntu tirumpivantu kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattai yaṭainta
   maṇimēkalai, " aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷ eṅkē uḷar?" eṉṟu viṉavicceṉṟu avarai
   aṭaintaṉaḷ. avar naraittu mutirnta yākkaiyuṭaiyar; [1]naṭuṅkāta
   nāviṉaiyuṭaiyar. aṟam uraikkum avvaṭikaḷāraik kaṇṭa maṇimēkalai avarai
   valamvantu vaṇaṅkit toḻutu niṉṟu, tāṉ cutamatiyuṭaṉ malarvaṉam
   pukuntatu, utaya kumaraṉ taṉṉai nōkki vantatu eṉpaṉa mutalāka,
   maṇipallavattiṟ putta pīṭikaiyaik kaṇṭu paḻam piṟappuṇarntatu, amuta
   curapipeṟ ṟatu īṟāka nikaḻntavai aṉaittaiyum oṉṟum viṭātu uraittāḷ.
   ivaṟṟaik kēṭṭatum aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷ mikka makiḻccikoṇṭu " poṟṟoṭi mātarāy,
   [2] naṟṟiṟam ciṟakka" eṉa vāḻtti, maṇimē kalaikkup paḻam piṟappiṉ
   varalāṟṟaip paṟṟit tāṉum cila col lal toṭaṅkiṉar. 'yāṉ āti mutalvaṉ
   aṭiyiṇaiyaip paravu taṟkup pātapaṅkaya malaikkuc ceṉṟēṉ. vaḻiyil oru
   poḻilil kaccaya nāṭṭaracaṉ tuccayaṉaik kaṇṭu, "maṉṉa, niṉṉōṭu niṉ
   tēviyarkkut tītiṉṟō ?" eṉa nalam ucāviṉēṉ. avaṉ maṉam aḻuṅki,
   kaṇkaluḻntu, ' kaḷimayakkattāl vīrai yāṉai muṉ vīḻntu iṟantaṉaḷ.
   attuyar poṟāta tārai, aramiya muṟṟattiṉmēl ēṟi, āṅku niṉṟum vīḻntu uyir
   tuṟantāḷ,' eṉṟaṉaṉ. atu kēṭṭu, yāṉ, aracē, itu paḻaviṉaippayaṉ; nī [3]
   pariyal eṉṟēṉ. iṉṟu āṭum kūttiyar pōla mātaviyum cutamatiyum
   maṇimēkalaiyumāka nīvir vēṟṟurukkoṇṭu niṟpataik kāṇkiṉṟēṉ " eṉṟu kūṟi,
   mēlum kūṟattoṭaṅki,
   "piṟaviyum [4] aṟaviyum peṟṟiyiṉ uṇarnta
   naṟumalark kōtāy! nalkiṉai kēḷāy :
   [5] taruma talaivaṉ talaimaiyiṉ uraitta
   perumaicāl nalla ṟam perukātāki
   iṟutiyil naṟkaṟi cellum peruvaḻi
   aṟu kaiyum neṟiñciyum aṭarntu [6]kaṇṇaṭait tāṅkuc
   [7]ceyir vaḻaṅku tīkkati tiṟantu kalleṉṟu
   uyirvaḻaṅku peruneṟi orutiṟampaṭṭatu :
   [8] taṇpaṉi viḻuṅkiya ceṅkatir maṇṭilam
   uṇṭeṉa vuṇarta lallatu yāvatum
   kaṇṭu iṉitu viḷaṅkāk kāṭci pōṉṟatu.
   " itaṉāl makkaḷ aṟavuṇarviṉṟi melintu tīmaikaṉṟic ceytu uḻaluvārāyiṉar.
   itaṉaik kaṇṭa cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭat tut tēvarellām, tuṭitalōkam ceṉṟu,
   āṅku uṟaiyum pirapā pālaṉ eṉṉum periyōṉ tiruvaṭiyil vīḻntu kuṟaiyirantu
   niṉṟaṉar. avar vēṇṭukōṭku icaintu,
   "iruḷ parantu kiṭanta [9]malartalai yulakattu
   [10] virikatirc celvaṉ tōṉṟiṉaṉ eṉṉa;
   [11] īreṇṇūṟṟōṭu īreṭṭāṇṭil
   pēraṟivāḷaṉ tōṉṟum;
   "ataṉpiṉ avaṉāl, ivvulakil,
   "peruṅkuḷa maruṅkil [12]curuṅkaic ciṟuvaḻi
   irumperu nīttam pukuvatu pōla
   [13] aḷavāc ciṟucevi aḷapparu nallaṟam
   uḷamali yuvakaiyōṭu uyirkoḷap pukūum,
   "mēlum, aṟam uraikkum tiṟalōṉākiya antap periyōṉ, ivvulakil tōṉṟuṅkāl,
   “tiṅkaḷum ñāyiṟum [14] tīṅkuṟā viḷaṅkat
   taṅkā nāṇmīṉ takaimaiyiṉ naṭakkum;
   vāṉam poyyātu; mānilam vaḷampaṭum;
   ūṉuṭai yuyirkaḷ uṟutuyar kāṇā;
   [15] vaḷivalam koṭkum mātiram vaḷampaṭum;
   [16] naḷiyiru munnīr nalampala tarūum;
   [17]kaṟavai kaṉṟārttik kalaniṟai poḻiyum;
   paṟavai [18]payaṉ tuyttu uṟaipati nīṅkā;
   vilaṅkumakkaḷum [19]verūup pakai nīṅkum
   “akkālattē, avaṉ uraikkum aruḷaṟattaik kēṭṭu oḻukuvōr piṟavittuṉpam
   nīṅkuvar. apperumāṉatu aṭiyiṇai kaḷaip piṟavitōṟum yāṉ maṟavātu
   ēttivarukiṉṟēṉ.
   " iṉi, niṉṉāl ivvūriṉkaṇ etu nikaḻccikaḷ pala nikaḻa virukkiṉṟaṉa. avai
   nikaḻntapiṉ, nī nallaṟam pala peṟuvāy. mātaviyum cutamatiyum, muṉ
   piṟaviyil pātapaṅkaya malaiyaip paraciyava rātalāl, iṉi avarkaḷ
   apputtaṉatu tiruvaṭi toḻutu viṉai nīṅki naṉṉeṟip paṭarvar. nī āruyir
   maruntākiya amutacurapi peṟṟuḷāy . atukoṇṭu pacippiṇi tīrkkum
   pēraṟattaip paṇṇuvāyāka” eṉṟu ivvāṟu aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷ maṇimēkalaikku aṟam
   uraittār. itaṉai maṉamkoḷḷak kēṭṭa maṇimēkalai, pacippiṇi tīrkkum
   perumpaṇiyai mēṟkoṇṭu āṟṟa eḻuntaṉaḷ.
   -----
   [1`. naṭuṅkāta - piḻaipaṭāta. [2]. naṟṟiṟam - 5lla kūṟupāṭukaḷ.
   [3]. pariyal - varuntātē.
   [4]. aṟavi - aṟattoṭu kūṭiya vaḻi. peṟṟiyiṉ - muṟaippaṭi, nalkiṉai -
   uvantu.
   [5]. taruma talaivaṉ - puttaṉ.
   [6]. kaṇ aṭaittāṅku - iṭamellām nirappi aṭaittuviṭṭāṟ. pōla.
   [7]. ceyir vaḻaṅku tīkkati - kuṟṟam nilavum tuṉpakkati. uyir vaḻaṅku
   peruneṟi - uyirkaḷellām cellum vaḻi.
   [8]. taṇpaṉi...maṇṭilam - kuḷirnta paṉiyaip pōkkiya civanta katir
   kaḷaiyuṭaiya ñāyiṟu.
   [9]. malar talai yulakam - iṭam virinta ulakam. [10]. viri...celvaṉ -
   ñāyiṟu.
   [11]. āyirattu aṟu nūṟṟup patiṉāṟām yāṇṭu. pēraṟivāḷaṉ - puttaṉ.
   [12]. curuṅkaic ciṟuvaḻi - curuṅkaiyākiya ciṟiyavaḻi, curuṅkai -
   mataku.
   [13]. aḷavā-pōtiya aḷaviṟṟākāta. nallaṟam pukūum eṉa iyaikka.
   [14]. tīṅkuṟā - māṟupāṭiṉṟi. [15]. vaḷi...koṭkum - kāṟṟu valamākac
   cuḻalum.
   [16]. naḷiyirumunnīr - nīrceṟinta periya kaṭal.
   [17]. kaṉṟu ārtti - kaṉṟai uṇpittu. kalaniṟai poḻiyum - kaṟa vaikkalam
   niṟaintu vaḻiyumpaṭip poḻiyum.
   [18]. payaṉ - paḻam. " paḻantēr vāḻkkaip paṟavai " eṉṟār piṟa rum.
   uṟaipati - vāḻumiṭam.
   [19]. verūuppakai - añcutaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa pakai.
   -------------

                 13. āputtiraṉ tiṟam aṟivitta kātai

   vaṭa nāṭṭil uḷḷa kāci nakarattukku vāraṇāci eṉṟum peya ruṇṭu. ataṉkaṇ
   apañcikaṉ eṉṉum ōr pārppāṉ vāḻntu vantāṉ. avaṉ maṟai yōtuvikkum
   toḻiliṉaṉ. avaṉuṭaiya maṉaivi cāliyeṉṉum peyaruṭaiyavaḷ. avaḷ orukāl
   taṉ kaṟpu nilai yiḻantu karuppamuṟṟāḷ. kaṇavaṉai vañcitta ik
   kuṟṟattukkuk kaḻuvāy nāṭi, teṉkumariyil nīrāṭak karutit teṉṉāṭu nōkki
   vantu koṇṭiruntāḷ. pāṇṭi nāṭṭuk koṟkai nakarukkut teṟkē orukāvatat
   tolaivil kōvalar 'vāḻum oru ciṟṟūril, oru cōlaiyil vayāvum
   varuttamumuṟṟu āṇ kuḻantai yoṉṟaik karuvuyirttāḷ.
   piṟanta kuḻantaiyaik kaṇṭum, ap pārppaṉi maṉam ciṟitum teḷiyātu,
   ataṉpāl aṉpō aruḷō iṟaiyum koḷḷātu, ac cōlaiyilē taṉikkaviṭṭuc
   ceṉṟoḻintāḷ. aṅkē taṉit tukkiṭanta kuḻaviyaip pacuvoṉṟu kaṇṭu, ataṉpāl
   irakkamuṟ ṟut taṉ nāvāl nakkit taṉ maṭicurantu pālai ūṭṭi eḻunāḷ varai
   yil kāttu ōmpivantatu. kaṟpuc ciṟappillā avvaṉṉeñcap pārppaṉiyiṉ ceyal
   orupuṟamirukka, āviṉ aruṭceyal vēṟoru puṟattē ciṟakka, avvaḻiyē vanta
   iḷampūti eṉṉum antaṇaṉ kaṇṭu, ak kuḻavipāl aṉpumikakkoṇṭu, vayaṉaṅkōṭu
   eṉṉum taṉ ūrukku eṭuttucceṉṟu, taṉ maṉaiviyuṭaṉ tāṉ peṟṟa maka ṉākavē
   koṇṭu vaḷarttu varalāṉāṉ. am makaṉum pārp pārkkuriya neṟimaippaṭiyē
   vaḷarntu kalaipala vuṇarntu aṟivumikap paṭaitta nallāṇ makaṉākac
   ciṟappuṟṟaṉaṉ.
   iḵtu iṅṅaṉamāka, orukāl maṟaiyōtum pārppaṉar palar kūṭip pacu
   vēḷviyoṉṟu ceyyaluṟṟaṉar. av vēḷviyiṟ kolai ceyvataṟkākak kaṉṟīṉṟa
   kaṟavaiyoṉṟaik koṇarntiruntaṉar. ak kayavaratu ceyalai evvāṟō
   uṇarntukoṇṭa ak kaṟavai kataṟik kattik kaṇṇīrukuttu niṉṟatu. ataṉaik
   kaṇṭa iḷampūti yiṉ vaḷarppuc ciṟuvaṉ, ak kaṟavaiyiṉpāl kaḻipōruḷ koṇṭu,
   naḷḷiruṭ pōtil ak kaṟavaiyai vēṟeṅkēṉum kaikkoṇṭu pōkak karuttil
   eṇṇiṉāṉ.
   ' ac ciṟuvaṉ, tāṉ eṇṇiya vaṇṇamē, annāḷ iravil, kaṟavaiyaik kaḷaviṉāl
   paṟṟikkoṇṭu oru kāṭṭu vaḻiyē pōvā ṉāyiṉaṉ. poḻutu viṭintatum, pārppaṉar
   tam vēḷvikkuriya kaṟavai kāṇātu pōṉamaikaṇṭu, ataṉait tēṭi nāṟpuṟamum
   tiriya luṟṟaṉar. avaruḷ oru cilar ic ciṟuvaṉ celvataik kaṇṭaṟintu
   koḷḷavē, aṉaivarum ik kāṭṭiṭattukku vantu, icciṟuvaṉaiyum kaṟavaiyaiyum
   vaḷaintu paṟṟikkoṇṭaṉar. ic ciṟuvaṉaip pala vāṟu vaitaṉar; vaḷaintu
   paṟṟik kōl koṇṭu avaṉai naiyap puṭaittu varuttiṉar. an nilaiyil, ak
   kaṟavai vekuṇṭu, ap pārppaṉaruḷ muṉṉaṇiyil niṉṟa vēḷvi yācāṉait tākkit
   taṉ kompiṉāṟ kuttik kuṭar carintu vīḻac ceytuviṭṭu ōṭiviṭṭatu.
   pārppaṉar taṉṉai mikavum aṭittu varuttiya vaḻiyum, ac ciṟuvaṉ,
   maṉaccōrvu koḷḷātu, avarkaḷai nōkki, pārppaṉarkaḷē,
   nōvaṉa ceyyaṉ miṉ; [1]noṭivaṉa kēṇmiṉ :
   [2]viṭu nila maruṅkil paṭupul ārntu,
   neṭunila maruṅkil makkaṭ kellām
   piṟantanāḷ toṭṭum ciṟantataṉ tīmpāl
   aṟantaru neñcōṭu aruḷ curan tūṭṭum
   itaṉoṭu vanta ceṟṟam eṉṉai?
   eṉṟu viṉaviṉaṉ; ataṟku av vētiyar kaḷ, nēriya kāraṇam uraik kum nīrmai
   illāmaiyāl,
   "poṉṉaṇi [3]nēmi valaṅkoḷ cakkarakkai
   maṉṉuyir mutalvaṉ makaṉ emakku aruḷiya,
   arumaṟai naṉṉūl aṟiyātu ikaḻntaṉai;
   [4]terumaral uḷḷattuc ciṟiyai; nī av
   āmakaṉ ātaṟku ottaṉai; aṟiyā
   nī makaṉallāy"
   eṉa ikaḻntu pēcat toṭaṅkiṉar.
   "ivaṉ makkaḷiṉattu makaṉallaṉ; vilaṅkiṉattu āṉiṉ makaṉ " eṉṟu ap
   pārppaṉar ikaḻntu pēca, "atu kuṟṟamaṉṟu" eṉṟaṟku ac ciṟuvaṉ,
   [5]"āṉ makaṉ acalaṉ; māṉ makaṉ ciruṅki;
   pulimakaṉ viriñci; [6]puraiyōr pōṟṟum
   narimakaṉ allaṉō kēcakampaḷaṉ;
   īṅkivar numkulattu iruṭikaṇaṅkaḷ eṉṟu
   [7]ōṅkuyar peruñciṟappu uraittalum uṇṭāl,"
   eṉa avar kūṟṟai maṟuttu, " āvoṭuvanta aḻikulamuṇṭō ?" eṉṟu avarkaḷ vāy
   aṭaippuṇṇak kaṭāviṉaṉ. yāvarum pēcum tiṟamiṉṟip pētuṟukiṉṟapōtu, vēṟoru
   pārppaṉaṉ, pēcutaṟku vēṟu viraku teriyātu, ic ciṟuvaṉ piṟappaip
   paḻikkat toṭaṅki, "ivaṉ taṉ piṟappu varalāṟṟiṉai yāṉ aṟikuvēṉ; kēḷmiṉ"
   eṉak kūṟaluṟṟāṉ :
   [8]"naṭavai varuttamoṭu nalkūr mēṉiyaḷ :
   vaṭamoḻi yāṭṭi; maṟaimuṟai eytik
   kumari pātam koḷkaiyiṉ vaṇaṅki,
   [9]tamaril tīrnta cāliyeṉ pōḷ taṉai
   'yātu niṉṉūr? īṅku eṉ varavu ' eṉa,
   māmaṟai yāṭṭi varutiṟam uraikkum:
   ‘vāraṇāci yōr māmaṟai mutalvaṉ,
   āraṇa vuvātti arumpeṟal maṉaiviyāṉ;
   pārppārkku ovvāp paṇpiṉ oḻukik
   [10]kāppuk kaṭaikaḻintu kaṇavaṉai iḻantēṉ;
   [11] eṟipayam uṭaimaiyiṉ iriyal mākkaḷoṭu
   teṟkaṭkumari āṭiya varuvēṉ
   poṉtērc ceḻiyaṉ koṟkaiyam pērūrk
   kāvatam kaṭantu kōvalar irukkaiyiṉ ī
   ṉṟa kuḻavikku iraṅkēṉāki
   [12]tōṉṟāt tuṭavaiyiṉ iṭṭaṉeṉ pōntēṉ;
   celkati yuṇṭō tīviṉaiyēṟku eṉṟu
   [13]allaluṟ ṟaḻuta avaḷ makaṉ īṅku ivaṉ;
   [14]collutal tēṟṟēṉ coṟpayam iṉmaiyiṉ
   pullal ōmpaṉmiṉ, pulaimakaṉ ivaṉ
   eṉṟu moḻintāṉ. itu kēṭṭa ac ciṟuvaṉ, ap pārppaṉarai ikaḻntu nōkki
   nakaittu, “pārppaṉamākkaḷē, uṅkaḷ kulamuṟai kūṟuvēṉ; kēṇmiṉ :
   mutumaṟai [15]mutalvaṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟiya
   [16]kaṭavuṭ kaṇikai kātalañ ciṟuvar,
   arumaṟai mutalvar antaṇar iruvarum;
   [17]puri nūl mārpīr ! poyyurai yāmō?"
   eṉṟu collit "tēva vēcimakkaḷāṉa vaciṭṭaṉ, akattiyaṉ eṉṟa iruvaraiyum
   mēṟkoṇṭu avar vaḻiyē varum pārppaṉa mākkaḷākiya uṅkaḷ muṟaimaiyē
   nōkkiṉ, eṉṉaip payanta, cālikku oru kuṟṟamum illaiyāmaṉṟō ?" eṉa ap
   pārppaṉar mukam cuḷittut talai kuṉiyumāṟu pēci nakaiyāṭi eḷḷiṉaṉ. itu
   kaṇṭa iḷampūti, icciṟuvaṉait taṉ maṉaiyiṉiṉṟum nīkkiviṭ ṭāṉ. avaṉpiṉ,
   avvūriṭattu oṭēntic cōṟu irantuṇpāṉāyi ṉaṉ. avvūril vāḻnta kaṉmaṉap
   pārppaṉar, avaṉ cōṟu irak kum ōṭṭil, cōṟu iṭātu kallaiyum maṇṇaiyum
   eṟiyaluṟṟaṉar.
   avaṉ, piṉpu avvūriṉ nīṅki, teṉmaturai yaṭaintāṉ. aṅkē cintātēvi
   kōyililuḷḷa pīṭikaiyil taṅki, cōṟu iran tuṇṭu vantaṉaṉ. avaṉēntiya
   ōṭṭil peṟṟa uṇavai avaṉ av vūriṭattē iruntu paciyāl vāṭiya,
   [18]kāṇār, kēḷār, kāl muṭap paṭṭōr,
   [19]pēṇunar illōr, piṇinaṭuk kuṟṟōr,
   yāvarum varuka eṉṟu [20]icaittu uṭaṉūṭṭi,
   avaruṇṭoḻinta miccilait tāṉ uṇṭu, ataṉaiyē talaikku aṇai yākavum koṇṭu
   uṟaṅkiyuṟaivāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   kuṟippu :- ic ciṟuvaṉē, īṅkup pārppaṉarāl āmakaṉ eṉa iḻittuk
   kūṟappaṭṭāṉāyiṉum, maṟupiṟappil, cāvaka nāṭṭil oru pacu vayiṟṟiṟ
   piṟantu ciṟakkiṉṟāṉātaliṉ, nam katai iṉitu cel vatu vēṇṭi, ivaṉai iṉi
   āputtiraṉ eṉṟē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟōm.
   -------
   [1]. noṭivaṉa - colluvaṉavaṟṟai.
   [2]. viṭu nilam - mēyccalukkāka aracaṉāl viṭappaṭṭa pulveḷi. paṭu pul -
   tāṉē muḷaittuc ceḻitta pul. ceṟṟam - pakaimai.
   [3]. nēmi - vaṭṭam. nēmiyaiyuṭaiya cakkaram eṉka. mutalvaṉ - tirumāl.
   makaṉ - piramaṉ.
   [4]. terumaral uḷḷattu - taṭumāṟukiṉṟa neñcattaiyuṭaiya. vilaṅku makaṉ
   eṉa ikaḻvār, " āṉmakaṉātaṟku ottaṉai " eṉṟaṉar. . makaṉallāy -
   āṟaṟivuṭaiya makaṉ allai.
   [5]. āṉmakaṉ - āṉukku makaṉ. māṉ makaṉ'. māṉukkumakaṉ. acalaṉ, ciruṅki,
   viriñci mutaliya antaṇar muṟaiyē āṉukkum, māṉukkum, pulik
   kum piṟantavar eṉka.
   [6]. puraiyōr - uyarntōr. kēcakampaḷaṉ eṉpāṉ oru muṉivaṉ.
   [7]. ōṅkuyar - mikavuyarnta . [8]. naṭavai - naṭantu cellum vaḻi.
   [9]. tamar il tīrnta - taṉakku uriyavar illattiṉiṉṟum oḻukkak kēṭṭāl
   nīṅkiṉa.
   [10]. kāppukkaṭai kaḻintu - koṇṭa kaṇavaṉatu kāvalaik kaṭantu
   (viyapicāram ceytu).
   [11]. eṟipayam uṭaimaiyiṉ - kaṇṭōr varuttuvar eṉṉum accat tāl. iriyal
   mākkaḷ - nīṅkic cellum māntaruṭaṉ. āṭiya - āṭuva taṟku. ceḻiyaṉ -
   pāṇṭiyaṉ.
   [12]. tōṉṟā pōntēṉ - piṟar kaṇṇukkut tōṉṟātapaṭi vantu viṭṭēṉ. tuṭavai
   - tōṭṭam. iṭṭaṉeṉ - vaittuviṭṭu.
   [13]. allal uṟṟu - tuyar mikuntu.
   [14]. collutal tēṟṟēṉ - colliṟṟilēṉ, coṟpayam iṉmai yiṉ - colvatāl oru
   payaṉum illāmaiyāl. pullal ōmpaṉmiṉ - tīṇṭa ṉ miṉ.
   [15]. mutalvaṉ - piramaṉ.
   [16]. kaṭavuṭkaṇikai - tilōttamai. ciṟuvar iruvarum eṉka. iru par -
   vaciṭṭaṉum akattiyaṉum.
   [17]. puri nūl - pūṇu nūl. [18]. kāṇār - kuruṭar.
   [19]. pēṇunar illōr - akatikaḷ.
   [20]. icaittu - aḻaittu. uṭaṉūṭṭi - oruṅkē yiruntu uṇṇac ceytu.

                     14. pāttiramarapu kūṟiya kātai

   itukāṟum tāṉ kūṟivantatai mikka ārvattōṭu kēṭṭa maṇimēkalaiyai nōkki,
   aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷ acciṟuvaṉāṉa āputtiraṉatu varalāṟṟai mēlum kūṟukiṉṟār.
   maturai nakariluḷḷa, cintātēvi tirukkōyiṟ pīṭikaiyil taṅkiyirunta
   āputtiraṉ, maḻaikkālattil orunāḷ naḷḷiravil iṉitu uṟaṅkik
   koṇṭirukkaiyil, neṭuntolaivu naṭantu paciyāl melintu cilar vantu avaṉai
   eḻuppi, " aiyaṉē, vayiṟu kāy . perum paciyāl mikavum varuntukiṉṟēm ”
   eṉṟu vaṇaṅki moḻin taṉar. avaṉō ēṟṟūṇ allatu vēṟṟūṇ illātavaṉ; ataṉāl,
   avaṉ avar paci kaḷaiyum vakaiyaṟiyātu tikaittāṉ. appōḻtil, cintātēvi
   tōṉṟi, avaṉai viḷittu,
   ēṭā ! [1]aḻiyal; eḻuntu itu koḷḷāy;
   nāṭu [2]vaṟaṅkūriṉum ivvōṭu vaṟaṅkūrātu;
   vāṅkunar kaiyakam varuntutal allatu
   tāṉ [3]tolaivillāt takaimaiyatu
   eṉṟu moḻintu taṉ kaiyiṭat tirunta toru uṇavukkalattai avaṉ kaiyil
   aṉpōṭu aḷittatu.
   ataṉaip peṟtataṉāl pēruvakaik kaṭalmūḻkiya āputtiraṉ, attēviyaip
   paravi,
   [4]cintā tēvi | ceḻuṅkalai [5]niyamattu
   [6] nantā viḷakkē! [7]nāmicaip pāvāy!
   vāṉōr talaivi! maṇṇōr mutalvi!
   ēṉōr uṟṟa iṭarkaḷai vāy
   eṉac collit toḻutu, taṉpāl pacittuvantavaratu pacippiṇi pōkkiṉaṉ.
   aṉṟumutal, annakariṉum ataṉaic cūḻavirunta nāṭ ṭilum pacippiṇi
   ilatāyiṟṟu. āputtiraṉatu aṟappayaṉum ciṟantu mikuvatāyiṟṟu. ataṉāl
   viṇṇulaka vēntaṉāṉa intiraṉatu pāṇṭu kampaḷam tuḷaṅkat toṭaṅkiyatu.
   itaṉai yaṟinta av vintiraṉ, tiraittu naraittu mūttut taḷarnta
   maṟaiyōṉoruvaṉaip pōla āputtiraṉ muṉtōṉṟi, " yāṉ intiraṉ; niṉ aṟattiṉ
   ciṟappai aṟintēṉ; nī vēṇṭum varam yātu? kēḷ, taruvēṉ " eṉṟāṉ. avaṉai
   eḷḷi nakaippāṉ pōla āputtiraṉ vilāvaṟac cirittu,
   [8]" īṇṭuc ceyviṉai [9]āṇṭu nukarn tiruttal
   [10]kāṇtaku ciṟappiṉ num kaṭavuḷa rallatu
   aṟañcey mākkaḷ [11]puṟaṅkāttu ōmpunar,
   naṟṟavam ceyvōr [12]paṟṟaṟa muyalvōr
   [13]yāvarum illāt tēvar naṉṉāṭṭukku
   iṟaiva ṉākiya peruntiṟal vēntē!
   varunti vantōr arumpaci kaḷaintu avar
   [14]tiruntu mukam kāṭṭum eṉ teyvak [15]kaṭiñai
   eṉakku ītu oṉṟē amaiyum; niṉṉāl tarappaṭakkūṭiya uṇṭiyum uṭaiyum
   peṇṭirum piṟavum eṉakku vēṇṭā" eṉṟu viḷampiṉaṉ.
   iccoṟkaḷaik kēṭṭalum intiraṉukkuc ciṉam tōṉṟi yatu. " īttuvakkum iṉpa
   mikutiyāl ivaṉ ivvāṟu moḻikiṉ ṟāṉ; iṉi, innāṭṭil ivaṉpāl uṇavu ēṟpār
   ilarāmāṟu maḻaiyum viḷai nalaṉum mikakūṇṭākkuvēṉ ”. eṉṟu niṉaittu,
   niṉaitta vaṇṇamē ceytaṉaṉ. āputtiraṉum taṉpāl pacittup pōntu uṇavu
   koḷvōr ilarāṉamai kaṇṭu ūrtōṟum uṇa viṉṟi varuntuvōr uḷarō eṉat tēṭit
   tiriyalāṉāṉ.
   paṉṉiraṇṭāṇṭukaḷāka maḻaiyiṉmaiyāl peruvaṟam kūrntirunta pāṇṭi nāṭṭil,
   ippōtu peruvaḷam curantu pēriṉpam ceytataṉāl,
   [16]' vīṭarum tūrttarum [17]viṭṭēṟ ṟāḷarum
   [18] naṭavai mākkaḷum nakaiyoṭu vaiki
   [19] vaṭṭum cūtum vampak kōṭṭiyum
   [20] muṭṭā vāḻkkaiyum muṟaimaiya tāka,
   pacittōrait tēṭit tiriyam āputtiraṉai, "yār ivaṉ eṉṟē yāvarum ikaḻnta
   ṉar." ataṉāl, avaṉ,
   [21] tiruviṉ celvam peruṅkaṭal koḷḷa
   [22]orutaṉi varūum perumakaṉ pōlat
   tāṉē tamiyaṉ varuvōṉ taṉ muṉ
   [23] mānīr vaṅkam vantōr
   cilar vantu vaṇaṅki, " cāvaka nāṭṭil peyaliṉmaiyāl uyirkaḷ peritum
   varuntukiṉṟaṉa ” eṉṟaṉar. atu kēṭṭatum, āputtiraṉ,
   amararkōṉ āṇaiyiṉ [24]aruntuvōrp peṟātu
   [25]kumari mūtta eṉ pāt tiramēnti
   aṅkan nāṭṭup pukuvateṉ karuttu eṉa
   [26]vaṅka mākkaḷoṭu makiḻvuṭa ṉēṟi
   [27] kālvicai kaṭukak kaṭal kalak kuṟutaliṉ
   [28] māl itai maṇipallavattiṭai vīḻttut
   taṅkiyatu orunāḷ; tāṉ āṅku iḻintaṉaṉ.
   iḻintu ammaṇipallavat tīviṭai avaṉ tirintukoṇ ṭirukkai yil,
   vaṅkamākkaḷ, avaṉ vaṅkam ēṟikkoṇṭaṉaṉ eṉap piṟaḻa vuṇarntu pōyviṭṭaṉar.
   tirintu pōnta āputtiraṉ vaṅkam pōyviṭṭatai yaṟintu maṉam aḻintu,
   aṅku vāḻvōr yāvarum iṉmaiyiṉ,
   [29]maṉṉuyir ōmpum immāperum pāttiram
   eṉṉuyir ōmputal yāṉō [30]poṟē eṉ;
   [31]tavantīr maruṅkila taṉittuyar uḻantēṉ;
   cumanteṉ pāttiram eṉ ṟaṉaṉ toḻutu,
   kōmuki yeṉṉum poykaikkaṇ appāttirattai eṟintu, " ōrāṇṭil orunāḷil itu
   veḷippaṭuka; atupōtu, aruḷaṟam pūṇṭu āruyirōmpum periyōr uḷareṉil, avar
   kaikkaṇ itu ceṉṟu amarvatāka ” eṉak kūṟi, uṇṇā nōṉpāṟṟi uyir viṭṭāṉ.
   akkālattē ceṉṟu yāṉ avaṉai viṉaviya pōtu, avaṉ eṉakku icceytiyaik
   kūṟiṉaṉ. piṉpu, avaṉ cāvaka nāṭṭai yāḷum pūmi cantiraṉ eṉṉum
   vēntaṉuṭaiya kōl niḻa lil irunta maṇmukaṉ eṉṉum muṉivaraṉiṭam irunta
   āviṉ vayiṟṟil piṟantāṉ.
   ----
   [1]. aḻiyal - varuntātē. koḷḷāy - koḷvāyāka.
   [2]. vaṟam kūriṉum - vaṟitāy viṭiṉum. vaṟam kūrā - vaṟumaiyuṟātu.
   [3]. tolaivu - kuṟaital. [4]. cintā tēvi - cintā viḷakkākiya tēvi
   [5]. niyamam - kōyil.
   [6]. nantā - aviyāta. kalveṭṭukkaḷ " nuntā viḷakku ' eṉpatum ituvē.
   [7]. nāmicaippāvāy - nāviṭattē yiruntu nalla coṟkaḷaic col vikkum tēvi.
   [8]. īṇṭuc ceyviṉai - ivvulakattē ceyyum aṟattiṉ payaṉai.
   [9]. āṇṭu nukarntiruttal - tēvarulakattē nukarntukoṇ ṭiruttalai.
   [10]. kāṇṭaku ciṟappiṉ - kaṇṭukoṇṭirukkum ciṟappiṉai yuṭaiya.
   [11]. puṟaṅkāttu ōmpunar - piṟaraip pātukāppavar.
   [12]. paṟṟaṟa muyalvōr - iruvakaippaṟṟum keṭa muyalpavar.
   [13]. yāvarum - īvōrum koḷvōrumākiya evarum.
   [14]. tiruntu mukam - uvakaiyāl malarnta mukam. [15]. kaṭiñai -
   piccaikkalam.
   [16]. viṭar - tīyoḻukkamuṭaiya kāmukar.
   [17]. viṭṭēṟṟāḷar - piṟarai ikaḻntu kaṭuñcol collum tīyavar.
   [18]. naṭavaimākkaḷ - vaḻiccellum tīyavar. nakai - aṟpar.
   [19]. vaṭṭu - uruṇṭai yuruṭṭiyāṭal. vampakkōṭṭi - payaṉil col kūṟit
   tirivōr kūṭṭam.
   [20]. muṭṭā vāḻkkai - kuṟaivillāta vāḻvu.
   [21]. tiruviṉ celvam - kaṇṭōr matikkattakka peruñcelvam.
   [22]. orutaṉi - taṉṉantaṉiyāka. perumakaṉ - aracaṉ, intiraṉumām.
   intiraṉ celvam muṉivaṉ cāpattāl kaṭal koḷḷappaṭṭateṉṟoru kataiyuṇṭu.
   [23]. mānīr vaṅkam - kaṭaliṟ cellum kalam.
   [24]. aruntuvōr - uṇavu ēṟṟu uṇpōr,
   [25]. kumari mūtta pāttiram - vaṟitē neṭunāḷ kiṭanta kalam.
   [26]. vaṅkamākkaḷ - marakkalattukku uriyavar. [27]. kāl - kāṟṟu.
   [28]. māl itai - marakkalattiṉ periya pāy.
   [29]. maṉṉuyir - nilaipeṟṟa uyirkaḷ. [30]. poṟēeṉ - virumpēṉāyiṉēṉ.
   [31]. tavantir maruṅkil - tīviṉaippakkattēṉāy. pāttiram cumantu eṉ -
   ēṟpōrillāta pōtu ip pāttirattaic cumattalāl peṟum payaṉ eṉṉai ?
   --------------

                15. pāttiraṅ koṇṭu piccaipukka kātai

   āputtiraṉai īṉṟa cāliyeṉpavaḷ viṭṭuppōṉa piṉ, avaṉai yā taritta pacu
   maṟupiṟappil, cāvaka nāṭṭut tavaḷavaraiyiṭattē vāḻnta maṇmukaṉ eṉṉum
   muṉivaṉpāl poṟkōṭum poṟ kompum uḷḷa pacuvāyp piṟantu vāḻntu vantatu.
   ataṉ vayiṟṟil poṟṟakaṭṭālāṉa muṭṭai yoṉṟil āputtiraṉ ceṉṟu taṅki ṉaṉ.
   itaṉai mukkālamum uṇarnta ammuṉivaṉ muṉpē aṟintu, itaṉpāl,
   maḻaivaḷam curappavum maṉṉuyi rōmpavum,
   [1] uyirkāvalaṉ vantu oruvaṉ tōṉṟum;
   [2] kuṭart toṭar mālai pūṇpā ṉallaṉ;
   [3] aṭarppoṉ muṭṭai akavaiyiṉāṉ ...eṉṟāṉ.
   piṉṉar.
   piṇinōy iṉṟiyum piṟantaṟam ceyya,
   maṇipallavat tiṭaimaṉṉuyir nīttōṉ;
   [4] taṟkāt taḷitta takai ā ataṉai,
   [5] oṟkā uḷḷat toḻiyā ṉātaliṉ,
   āṅkav [6]vāvayiṟṟu amararkaṇam uvappa,
   [7] tīṅkaṉi nāval ōṅkumit tīviṉukku
   orutāṉāki ulaku toḻat tōṉṟiṉaṉ.
   ivaṉ piṟanta nāḷ puttatēvaṉ piṟanta nāḷātaliṉ,
   maṇṇaka mellām māri yiṉṟiyum,
   [8] puṇṇiya naṉṉīr pōtoṭu corintatu.
   icciṟappaik kaṇṭa cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭattil vāḻum māta varkaḷ tiraṇṭu
   ceṉṟu kantiṟpāvaiyai viṉaviṉar. avarkaṭku appāvai āputtiraṉatu
   tōṟṟattaik kūṟi, ēṉaic ceytikaḷai aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷpāl kēṭkumāṟu
   viṭuttatu. avarkaṭku avvaṭi kaḷ, piṉvarum ceytiyaik kūṟiṉaṉ.
   [9]maṇṇāḷ vēntaṉ, maṇmuka ṉeṉṉum
   [10] puṇṇiya mutalvaṉ tiruntaṭi vaṇaṅki,
   'makkaḷai yillēṉ; mātavaṉ aruḷāl
   peṟṟēṉ putalvaṉai' eṉṟu avaṉ vaḷarppa,
   [11] araicāḷ celvam avaṉ pāl uṇmaiyiṉ,
   [12] niraitār vēntaṉ āyiṉaṉ; avaṉ tāṉ,
   [13] tuṟakka vēntaṉ, tuyppilaṉ kollō!
   [14]aṟakkōl vēntaṉ, aruḷilaṉ kollō !
   ivvaṇṇam kūṟippōnta aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷ, maṇimēkalaiyai nōkki,
   [15]nalattakai yiṉṟi nalluyirk kellām
   [16]alattaṟ kālai yākiyatu; āyiḻai,
   veṇtirai tanta amutai vāṉōr
   uṇ ṭoḻi miccilai [17]oḻittu vaittāṅku
   [18]vaṟaṉōṭulakil vāṉ tuyar keṭukkum
   aṟaṉōṭu oḻittal, āyiḻai, takātu
   eṉṟu moḻintār. atu kēṭṭatum, maṇimēkalai taṉṉaic cūḻa irunta tāyaruṭaṉ
   avar tiruvaṭiyil vīḻntu vaṇaṅki viṭai peṟṟu, pikkuṇik kōlattuṭaṉ,
   piccaippāttiram kaiyil viḷaṅ kak kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattup perunteruvai
   aṭaintāḷ. avaḷaik kaṇṭa ūravarum piṟarum,
   utaya kumaraṉ uḷamkoṇ ṭoḷitta
   [19] matumalark kuḻalāḷ vantu tōṉṟip
   piccaip pāttiram kaiyiṉ ēntiyatu
   [20] tippiyam eṉṟē cintainōy kūra,
   maṇimēkalai, maṉamakiḻcci mikavuṭaiyaḷāy, "pattiṉip peṇṭir iṭum
   piccaiyai mutaṟkaṇ ēṟṟal peruntakavākum" eṉṟaṉaḷ. avaṭkuk kāyacaṇṭikai
   eṉṉum viñcaiyar makaḷorutti, .
   [21] "kuḷaṉ aṇitāmaraik koḻumalar nāppaṇ,
   orutaṉi ōṅkiya [22]tirumalar pōṉṟu,
   [23]vāṉ taru kaṟpiṉ maṉaiyuṟai makaḷiriṉ
   tāṉ [24]taṉi yōṅkiya takaimaiya ḷaṉṟō
   ātirai nallāḷ; avaḷ maṉai, immaṉai;
   nī [25]pukal vēṇṭum, nēriḻai!" eṉṟaṉaḷ.
   ivvāṟu muṉmoḻinta kāyacaṇṭikai mēlē ātiraiyiṉ vara lāṟṟiṉaik
   kūṟaluṟukiṉṟāḷ.
   ----
   [1]. uyir kāvalaṉākiya oruvaṉ vantu tōṉṟum; uyiraikkākkum oruvaṉ
   varuvaṉ.
   [2]. kuṭarttoṭarmālai - kuṭarākiya mālai.
   [3]. poṉ aṭar muṭṭai akavayiṉāṉ - poṉ takaṭṭālāṉa muṭ ṭaiyiṉ
   uḷḷiṭattāṉ.
   [4]. taṟkāttaḷitta takai ā - taṉṉaip pālūṭṭi vaḷartta aḻakiya ā.
   [5]. oṟkā - olkā; taḷarāta.
   [6]. ā vayiṟṟu - āviṉ vayiṟṟil. amararkaṇam - tēvar kūṭṭam.
   [7]. tīṅkaṉi nāval - tīviya kaṉikaḷaiyuṭaiya nāval maram. tīvu -nāvalan
   tīvu.
   [8]. puṇṇiya naṉṉīr - puṇṇiyap poruḷākiya nalla nīr. pū - vāṉa var
   coriyum pū.
   [9]. maṇ - maṇṇulakam. vēntaṉ - pūmicantiraṉ eṉṉum aracaṉ.
   [10]. puṇṇiya mutalvaṉ - puṇṇiyat talaivaṉāṉa muṉivaṉ.
   [11]. araicu - aracu.
   [12]. niraitār vēntaṉ - varicaiyuṟattoṭutta mālaiyaṇinta aracaṉ.
   [13]. tuṟakka vēntaṉ - intiraṉ. tuyppu - aviyuṇavu.
   [14]. aṟakkōl - aṟam tiriyāta ceṅkōl.
   [15]. nalattakai - naṟpayaṉ; celvakkālam.
   [16]. alattaṟkālai - vaṟumaik kālam. ākiyatu - uṇṭāyiṟṟu.
   [17]. oḻittuvaittāṅku - payaṉpaṭuttātu ōriṭattē vaṟitē vait tatu pōla.
   [18]. vaṟaṉ ōṭu ulakil - vaṟumai viriyap parantuḷḷa ulakil, vāṉ
   tuyar - mikka tuṉpam. aṟaṉōṭu oḻittal - aṟam ceytaṟkuriya amuta
   curapiyākiya ōṭṭiṉaip payaṉpaṭuttātu vaittiruttal.
   [19]. matumalark kuḻalāḷ -tēṉaiyuṭaiya pūvaṇinta kūntalaiyuṭaiya maṇi
   mēkalai.
   [20]. tippiyam - viyapput taruvatu.
   [21]. kuḷaṉ aṇi tāmarai - kuḷattiṭattē aḻakuṟa malarnta tāmarai. nāppaṇ
   - naṭuvē.
   [22]. tirumalar - aḻaku ciṟanta tāmaraippū.
   [23]. vāṉ taru kaṟpu - maḻai peyvikkum kaṟpu.
   [24]. taṉi ōṅkiya - nikaraṟac ciṟanta. takaimaiyaḷ - takutiyuṭaiyaḷ.
   [25]. pukal vēṇṭum - virumpic cellavēṇṭum.
   -----------------

                     16. ātirai piccaiyiṭṭa kātai

   ātirai yeṉpavaḷ cātuvaṉ eṉṉum vaṇikaṟku maṉaivi. avaṉ toṭakkattē,
   kaṇikaimakaḷiriṉ kāmavalaippaṭṭut taṉ maṉaiviyaik kaiviṭṭoḻukiṉaṉ;
   avaṉpāl kaḷḷuṇṭal, kava ṟāṭal mutaliya tīcceyalkaḷ uḷavāyiṉa. avaṟṟāl
   avaṉ celva miḻantu vaṟiyaṉāyiṉaṉ. avaṉāṟ pēṇappaṭṭa kaṇikaiyarum
   avaṉaik "kāṇamili" eṉak kaiyutirtta ṉar. piṉṉar, 'avaṉ taṉ nilaiyiṉai
   niṉaintu varunti, vaṅkam pōkum vāṇikar tammuṭaṉ tāṉum ceṉṟaṉaṉ.
   vaḻiyil, avaṉ ēṟicceṉṟa vaṅkam tīvaḷi yāl kaṭaliṟ kaviḻntuviṭṭatu.
   uṭaikalappaṭṭa cātuvaṉ oṭi maram oṉṟaip paṟṟi, nākar vāḻum tīvoṉṟai
   yaṭaintu uṇarvaṟṟuk kiṭantāṉ.
   avaṉaip pōla vēṟōrāṟṟāl uyir tappic ceṉṟōr, ātiraipāl nikaḻntatu kūṟi,
   cātuvaṉ iṟantāṉ eṉṟaṉar.
   ātirai nallāḷ āṅkatu tāṉ kēṭṭu
   [1] ūrīrē yō! oḷḷaḻal īmam
   tārīrō eṉac [2]cāṟṟiṉaḷ kaḻaṟi,
   cuṭalaik kāṉil toṭukuḻip paṭuttu,
   [3] muṭalai viṟakiṉ muḷiyeri potti,
   "mikka eṉ kaṇavaṉ viṉaippayaṉ uyppap
   pukkuḻip pukuvēṉ" eṉṟu avaḷ
   tīyiṭai vīḻntāḷ. vīḻnta pōtu,
   [4] paṭuttuṭaṉ vaitta pāyaṟ paḷḷiyum,
   uṭutta kūṟaiyum oḷḷeri uṟā atu;
   [5] āṭiya cāntamum, acainta kūntalil
   cūṭiya mālaiyum [6]tol niṟam vaḻātu;
   [7] viraimalart tāmarai orutaṉi yirunta
   [8] tiruviṉ ceyyōḷ pōṉṟu iṉi tiruppa,
   avaḷ peritum kalakka muṟṟu,
   tīyum kollāt [9]tīviṉai yāṭṭiyēṉ,
   yātu ceyvēṉ eṉṟu avaḷ ēṅkiṉaḷ;
   ēṅkalum, vāṉattē meyyoli eḻuntu,
   ātirai, kēḷ : uṉ arumpeṟaṟ kaṇavaṉai,
   [10]ūrtirai koṇṭāṅku. uyppap pōki,
   [11] nakka cāraṇar nākar vāḻmalaip
   pakkam cērntaṉaṉ; palyāṇṭu irāṉ;
   cantiratattaṉ eṉumōr vāṇikaṉ
   vaṅkam taṉṉuṭaṉ vantaṉaṉ tōṉṟum;
   niṉ perun tuṉpam oḻivāy nī eṉa
   uraittatu. atu kēṭṭu maṉanteḷintu makiḻnta ātirai taṉ
   maṉaiyakamaṭaintu,
   [12] kaṇmaṇi yaṉaiyāṉ kaṭi tīṅ kuṟuka eṉap
   [13]puṇṇiyam muṭṭāḷ; poḻimaḻai tarūum
   [14]arumpeṟal marapiṉ pattiṉip peṇṭirum
   virumpiṉar toḻūum [15] viyappiṉa ḷāyiṉaḷ.
   ivvāṟu ātiraiyiṉ ceyal vakai kūṟiya kāyacaṇṭikai nākar tīvaṭainta
   cātuvaṉ ceyalaik kūṟaluṟṟu, nākar tīvil malaippakkattē uṇarvaṟṟuk
   kiṭanta cātuvaṉai, āṅkē vanta nākar cilar kaṇṭu, tamakku avaṉatu uṭampu
   nalluṇavāmeṉṟu karuti yeḻuppiṉar.
   eḻunta cātuvaṉ avar moḻiyai naṉkaṟintavaṉātaliṉ, ava ruṭaṉ iṉitē
   collāṭiṉaṉ. avarkaḷ avaṉait toḻutu, "iṅkē em talaimakaṉ uḷaṉ; avaṉpāl
   nī pōntaruḷ" eṉ ṟaṉar. cātuvaṉ ataṟku icaintu avaruṭaṉ nākar
   talaivaṉpāl ceṉṟaṉaṉ.
   kaḷḷaṭu [16]kuḻiciyum kaḻimuṭai nāṟṟamum
   veḷḷeṉ [17] puṇaṅkalum viraviya irukkaiyil,
   [18]eṇkutaṉ piṇavō ṭiruntatu pōlap
   peṇṭuṭaṉ irunta
   nākar talaivaṉaic cātuvaṉ kaṇṭāṉ. avaṉ ivaṉatu varavu viṉāvi,
   nikaḻntatu kēṭṭu, collāṭṭāl aṉpukoṇṭu, iccātuvaṉ,
   [19]aruntutal iṉṟi alai kaṭal [20] uḻantōṉ;
   varuntiṉaṉ; aḷiyaṉ. vammiṉ, mākkāḷ!
   nampikku iḷaiyaḷōr naṅkaiyaik koṭuttu,
   [21]veṅkaḷum ūṉum vēṇṭuva koṭum
   eṉṟu uraittāṉ. cātuvaṉ :
   [22]vevvurai kēṭṭēṉ; vēṇṭēṉ.
   nākar talaivaṉ mukañ cuḷittu:
   peṇṭirum uṇṭiyum iṉṟeṉil mākkaṭku
   uṇṭō [23]ñālattu uṟu payaṉ? uṇ ṭeṉil,
   kāṇkuvam, yāṅkaḷum; kāṭṭuvā yāka.
   cātuvaṉ :
   mayakkum kaḷḷum maṉṉuyir [24] kōṟalum
   [25] kayakkaṟu mākkaḷ kaṭintaṉar, kēḷāy :
   piṟantavar cātalum iṟantavar piṟattalum
   uṟaṅkalum viḻittalum pōṉṟatu uṇmaiyiṉ,
   nallaṟam ceytōr nallula kaṭaitalum,
   allaṟam ceyvōr arunara kaṭaitalum
   uṇṭeṉa vuṇartaliṉ, [26]uravōr kaḷaintaṉar;
   kaṇṭaṉai yāka.
   nā. talai:- (nakaittu)
   uṭampuviṭṭōṭum uyir urukkoṇṭu ōr
   iṭampukum eṉṟē emakku īṅku uraittāy;
   avvuyir evvaṇam pōyppukum? avvakai.
   [27]cevvaṉam urai.
   cātuvaṉ:- itu kēḷ,
   [28]uṟṟatai yuṇarum uṭal uyir vāḻvuḻi;
   [29] maṟṟaiya vuṭampē, maṉṉuyir nīṅkiṭiṉ,
   [30] taṭinteri yūṭṭiṉum tāṉuṇa rāteṉiṉ,
   uṭampiṭaip pōṉatoṉ ṟuṇṭeṉa uṇar, nī.
   [31] pōṉār tamakkōr pukkil uṇ ṭeṉpatu
   yāṉō allēṉ, yāvarum uṇarkuvar;
   uṭampīṇ ṭoḻiya uyirpala kāvatam
   kaṭantu [32]cēṇcēṟal kaṉaviṉum kāṇkuvai;
   āṅkaṉam pōki avvuyir ceyviṉai
   [33] pūṇṭa yākkaiyil pukuvatu teḷi, nī.
   nā. talaivaṉ:-- (vaṇaṅki)
   kaḷḷum ūṉum kaiviṭiṉ ivvuṭampu
   uḷḷuṟai vāḻuyir [34]ōmputa lāṟṟēṉ;
   [35] tamakkoḻi marapiṉ cāvuṟu kāṟum
   emakkām nalla ṟam eṭutturai.
   cātuvaṉ:
   naṉṟu coṉṉāy naṉṉeṟi [36]paṭarkuvai
   uṉ taṉakku ollum neṟiyaṟam uraikkēṉ;
   uṭaikala mākkaḷ uyiruyn tīṅkuṟiṉ
   [37] aṭutoḻil oḻintu avar āruyi rōmpi
   [38] mūttu viḷimā oḻittu evvuyir māṭṭum
   [39]tīttiṟam oḻika.
   ivvaṟaṅkaḷaik kēṭṭa nākar talaivaṉ perumakiḻvu koṇṭu, vēṇṭum poṉṉum
   poruḷum mika nalkiṉaṉ. avaṟṟaip peṟṟa cātuvaṉ cantiratattaṉ eṉṉum
   vaṇikaṉatu vaṅkamēṟi innakarai yaṭaintu taṉ maṉaivi ātiraiyuṭaṉ
   nallaṟam ceytu varuvāṉā yiṉaṉ; ivvā tiraipāl piccai peṟuvāyāka eṉak
   kāyacaṇṭikai colli muṭittāḷ. avvaṇṇamē maṇimēkalai ātirai illiṉ muṉ “
   puṉaiyā ōviyam ” pōla niṉṟaṉaḷ. ātirai nallāḷum ava ḷatu
   piccaippāttiram niṟaiyac cōṟu peytāḷ.
   ----
   [1]. ūrīrēyō - ūril uḷḷa cāṉṟōrkaḷē. īmam - viṟakaṭukku.
   [2]. cāṟṟiṉaḷ - colliṉaḷ.
   [3]. muṭalai viṟaku - muṟukkuṇṭu kiṭakkum viṟaku. eri potti - eri
   mūṭṭi. muḷiyeri - mikka neruppu.
   [4]. pāyaṟpaḷḷi - paṭukkaiyiṭam, uṭaṉ paṭuttu vaitta eṉa iyaikka. kūṟai
   - puṭaivai.
   [5]. āṭiya - pūcikkoṇṭa.
   [6]. tol niṟam - paḻaiya niṟamum oḷiyum. vaḻā atu - keṭāmal.
   [7]. virai - maṇam kamaḻum. [8]. tiruviṉ ceyyōḷ - tirumakaḷākiya
   ceyyavaḷ.
   [9]. tīviṉaiyāṭṭi - tīviṉai ceytavaḷ.
   [10]. ūr tirai - oṉ ṟaṉ piṉ oṉṟāka vūrntu cellum alaikaḷ. uyppa -
   celutta.
   [11]. nakka cāraṇar - uṭaiyillāmal tiripavar . itaṉāl nākar “nakka
   cāraṇar nākar' eṉappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.
   [12]. kaṇ maṇi yaṉai yāṉ - kaṇṇiluḷḷa maṇiyoppavaṉāṉa kaṇavaṉ .
   [13]. puṇṇiyam muṭṭāḷ - puṇṇiyam ceytalil kuṟaiyāḷāy.
   [14]. arumpeṟal marapu - peṟutaṟkariya muṟaimai (kaṟpu).
   [15]. viyappiṉaḷ - takutiyuṭaiyaḷ. peṇṭirum: ummai, uyarvuc ciṟappu.
   [16]. kuḻici - pāṉai. kaḻimuṭaināṟṟam - mikka pulāl nāṟṟam.
   [17]. uṇaṅkal - vaṟṟal. [18]. eṇku - karaṭi. piṇavu - peṇkaraṭi.
   [19]. aruntutal - uṇavu. [20]. uḻantōṉ - tuṉpuṟṟāṉ.
   [21]. veṅkaḷ - puḷippumikka kaḷ; virumpattakum kaḷḷumām.
   [22]. vevvurai - koṭiya col.
   [23]. ñālattu uṟupayaṉ - ulakattu uyir vāḻtalālākum payaṉ.
   [24]. kōṟal - kollutal.
   [25]. kayakkaṟumākkaḷ - kayaṅkutal illāta aṟivuṭaiyōr.kaṭintaṉar
   vilakkiṉar.
   [26]. uravōr - aṟivuṭaiya periyōr. [27]. cevvaṉam - teḷivāka.
   [28]. uyir vāḻvuḻi uṭal uṟṟatai yuṇarum eṉka. uṟṟatu - uṭaliṟ paṭṭa tu.
   [29]. uṭampu taṭintu, eriyūṭṭiṉum uṇarātu eṉka.
   [30]. taṭintu - kuṟaittu (veṭṭi).
   [31]. pōṉār - pōṉa uyir. pukkil - pukumiṭam.
   [32]. cēṇ cēṟal - neṭuntolaivu pōtal.
   [33]. pūṇṭa yākkai - uyir niṟṟaṟkuk koṇṭa uṭampu.
   [34]. ōmputal tēṟṟēṉ - kāttalaic ceyyēṉ; iḻantu viṭuvēṉ eṉpatām.
   [35]. tamakku oḻimarpiṉ nallaṟam eṉa iyaikka. tamakkeṉṟē varai yaṟutta
   muṟaimaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya nalla ṟam.
   [36]; paṭarkuvai - celvāy. [37]. aṭutoḻil - kollutal.
   [38]. mūttuviḷimā - mutumaiyuṟṟu iṟakkum uyirkaḷ.
   [39]. tīttiṟam – kolaittoḻil.
