                       17. ulaka aṟavi pukka kātai

   ātiraiyiṭṭa piccaiyuṭaṉ ceṉṟa maṇimēkalai pacinōy varutta varuntum
   makkaḷ palarkkum uṇavu iṭṭu varalāṉāḷ. ata ṉāl, appāttirattup piccai
   kuṟaiyātu niṟaintu niṉṟatu kaṇṭu, kāya caṇṭikai,
   [1] neṭiyōṉ mayaṅki nilamicait tōṉṟi
   [2] aṭalaru munnīr aṭaitta nāṉṟu,
   kuraṅku koṇarn teṟinta [3] neṭumalai yellām
   [4] aṇaṅkuṭai yaḷakkar vayiṟu pukkāṅku
   iṭṭatu āṟṟāk kaṭṭaḻaṟ kaṭumpacip
   paṭṭēṉ, eṉṟaṉ paḻaviṉaip payattāl;
   aṉṉai, kēḷ nī. [5] āruyir maruttuvi! [6]
   tuṉṉiya eṉ nōy tuṭaippāy eṉṟaṉaḷ.
   maṇimēkalai aṅṅaṉamē cōṟiṭalum, kāyacaṇṭikai koṇṭirunta yāṉait tī eṉṉum
   pacinōy nīṅkiṟṟu. avaḷ neñcu kuḷirntu, taṉakku an nōyuṇṭāṉa kāraṇattaik
   kūṟaluṟṟāḷ.
   veḷḷimalaiyiṉ pakutiyākiya cēṭi malaiyil uḷḷa kāñcaṉa purattu
   vittiyātara makaḷākiya yāṉ, eṉ kaṇavaṉuṭaṉ potiyil malaiyaik kāṇṭaṟku
   vantukoṇ ṭiruntēṉ. vaḻiyil ōr yāṟṟil eṉṉai yirutti eṉ kaṇavaṉ kaṉi
   koṇarac ceṉṟāṉ. aṅkē
   *muḷ, ¨
   viruccikaṉ eṉṉum mātavaṉ paṉṉīrāṇṭiṟ koru muṟai paḻuk kum nāvaṟkaṉi
   yoṉṟaik koṇṭuvantu tēkkilaiyil vaittu nīrāṭac ceṉṟāṉ. ataṉ uṇmainilai
   yuṇarātu cerukkic ceṉṟa yāṉ akkaṉiyaik kālāl eṟṟic citaittēṉ. tirumpip
   pōnta viruccikaṉ eṉ ceyalaik kaṇṭu ciṉantu,
   cīrtikaḻ nāvalil [7]patippiyamāṉatu,
   īrāṟāṇṭil orukaṉi taruvatu
   akkaṉi yuṇṭōr āṟīrāṇṭu
   makkaḷ yākkaiyiṉ varumpaci nīṅkuvar;
   paṉṉīrāṇṭil orunāḷallatu
   uṇṇā nōṉpiṉēṉ uṇkaṉi [8]citaittāy;
   [9] antarañ cellum mantiram iḻantu
   [10] tantit tīyāl taṉittuya ruḻantu
   munnālāṇṭil mutirkaṉi nāṉ īṅku
   uṇṇum nāḷ uṉ uṟu paci kaḷaika
   eṉac cāpa miṭṭakaṉṟāṉ. piṉpu vanta eṉ kaṇavaṉ eṉ aṟiyā maik kiraṅki,
   varunti, muṭivil,
   [11]camput tīviṉuḷ tamiḻaka maruṅkil
   [12] kampamillāk kaḻiperuñ celvar,
   āṟṟāmākkaṭku āṟṟun tuṇaiyāki,
   nōṟṟōr uṟaivatōr [13] nōṉakar uṇṭāl;
   palanāḷāyiṉum [14] nilaṉoṭu pōki,
   appatip pukuka,
   eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭup pirintaṉaṉ. yāṉum innakarai yaṭaintu iṅku
   vativēṉāyiṉēṉ. intiraviḻāk kālattē eṉ kaṇavaṉ pōntu eṉṉaik kaṇṭu
   ekuvatu vaḻakkam. iṉṟu nī pōntu eṉ pacit tuyar kaḷaintu paṇṭaiyiyalpu
   peṟuvittāy. iṉi yāṉ eṉ nakark kuc celvēṉ. nī innakaril uḷḷa
   cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭattu ulakavaṟavikkuc ceṉṟu, aṅkē,
   [15] ūrūrāṅkaṇ uṟupaci yuḻantōr,
   [16] ārum iṉmaiyiṉ arumpiṇi yuṟṟōr,
   [17] iṭuvōrt tērntāṅku iruppōr palarāl;
   [18]. vaṭuvāḻ kūntal! ataṉ pāṟa pōkuka
   eṉṟu colliviṭṭu akkāyacaṇṭikai pōyviṭṭaṉaḷ.
   avaḷ colliyavaṇṇamē, maṇimēkalai avvulaka aṟavikkuc ceṉṟu, campāpati
   kōyilai yaṭaintu mummuṟai valam vantu vaṇaṅkip piṉ kantiṟpāvaiyaik
   kaitoḻutu, pacitta mākkaṭkup pēruṇavu vaḻaṅki avar paci nōy tīrkkum
   perun toṇṭu purintu varalāṉāḷ.
   ----
   [1]. neṭiyōṉ - tirumāl. mayaṅki - amparīṭaṉukku muṉivar iṭṭa cāpattai
   yēṟṟataṉāl mayaṅki.
   [2]. aṭalaru munnīr - taṭuttaṟkariya kaṭal.
   [3]. neṭumalai - neṭum pāṟaikaḷ.
   [4]. aṇaṅkuṭai yaḷakkar - muḻaṅku talaiyuṭaiya kaṭal. muḻaṅkukaṭalai,
   varuttattāl pulampuvatākak karutik kūṟutaliṉ, " aṇaṅkuṭai yaḷakkar "
   eṉṟāḷ. paṭṭēṉ - varuntiṉēṉ.
   [5]. āruyir marut tuvi - āruyirkaṭku maruntākiya uṇavu aḷippavaḷ.
   uyirkaḷ eytum nōyvakai yevaṟṟiṟkum periya nōy paciyātaliṉ, atu nīkkum
   uṇavai, " āruyir maruntu " eṉṟum, ataṉait tarupava ḷātaliṉ, " āruyir
   maruttuvi " yeṉṟum kūṟiṉāḷ.
   [6]. tuṉṉiya - neruṅki nīṅkātu niṉṟa.
   [7]. tippiyamāṉatu - teyvat taṉmaiyuṭaiyatu.
   [8]. citaittāy - kālāl iṭaṟik keṭuttāy.
   [9]. antaram cellum - vāṉattūṭu cellum.
   [10]. tantit tī - yāṉait tī yeṉṉum nōy.
   [11]. camput tīvu - nāvalantīvu.
   [12] . kampam - naṭukkam; īṇṭuk kēṭṭiṉ mēṟṟu. kaḻiperuñ cel var - mikka
   periya celvamuṭaiyōr.
   [13]. nōṉ nakar - aṭuttarrait tāṅkum vaṉmai paṭaitta nakaram.
   (kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉam)
   [14]. nilaṉoṭu pōki - nilattē naṭantu ceṉṟu.
   [15]. ūrūrāṅkaṇ - ūrtōṟum.
   [16]. ārum - pātukāppōr oruvarum.
   [17].iṭuvōr - uṇavu iṭuvōr. tērntu - nōkki.
   [18]. vaṭuvāḻ kūntal - vakirnta kūntalaiyuṭaiya maṇimēkalaiyē.
   -------------

                   18. utayakumaraṉ ampalam pukka kātai

   maṇimēkalai ivvāṟu piccaiyēṟṟaḷikkum pēraṟat tuṟavi yāy oḻukukiṉṟa
   ceyti, cittirāpati eṉṉum mātaviyiṉ tāykku eṭṭiyatu. perumpuṇṇil
   ericerukiyatu pōl, aḵtu avaḷ neñcil peruntuyaraic ceytatu. ataṉāl avaḷ,
   ēṉai nāṭaka makaḷirai nōkki,
   kōvalaṉ iṟantapiṉ [1] koṭuntuyar eyti
   mātavi [2] mātavar paḷḷiyuḷ aṭaintatu
   [3] nakutak kaṉṟē, nalneṭum pērūr;
   itu [4]tak keṉpōrkku eḷḷurai yāyatu;
   [5] kātalaṉ vīyak kaṭuntuya reytip
   [6] pōtal ceyyā uyiroṭu pulantu,
   [7] naḷiyirum poykai yāṭunar pōla
   [8] muḷiyerippukūum mutukuṭip piṟanta
   pattiṉip peṇṭi rallēm; palartam
   [9] kaittūṇ vāḻkkai kaṭaviyam aṉṟē;
   pāṇmakaṉ paṭṭuḻip paṭūum [10]pāṉmaiyiṉ,
   yāḻiṉam pōlum iyalpiṉam; aṉṟiyum,
   [11] naṟuntā tuṇṭu nayaṉil kālai
   [12] vaṟumpūt tuṟakkum vaṇṭu pōlkuvam;
   [13] viṉai yoḻi kālait tiruviṉ celvi
   aṉaiyē māki āṭavart tuṟappēm,
   tāpatak kōlam tāṅkiṉam eṉpatu
   yāvarum nakū um iyalpiṉatu aṉṟē !
   mātavi īṉṟa maṇimē kalaivalli,
   [14] pōtaviḻ cevvi poruntutal virumpiya
   utaiya kumaraṉām ulakāḷ vaṇṭiṉ
   [15] citaiyā vuḷḷam cevvitiṉ arunta,
   kaikkoṇ ṭāṅkavaḷ ēntiya [16]kaṭiñaiyaip
   [17] piccai mākkaḷ piṟarkaik kāṭṭi
   maṟṟu avaṉ taṉṉāl maṇimē kalai taṉaip
   poṟṟērk koṇṭu pōtē ṉākil,
   [18] cuṭumaṇ ēṟṟi araṅku cūḻ pōki
   [19] vaṭuvoṭu vāḻum maṭantaiyar tammō
   raṉaiyē ṉāki araṅkak kūttiyar
   [20] maṉaiyakam pukā a marapiṉaṉ
   eṉṟu vañciṉaṅ kūṟi, iḷaṅkōvākiya utayakumaraṉ irunta pō kattai
   yaṭaintāḷ. āṅkē,
   [21] arava vaṇṭoṭu tēṉiṉa mārkkum.
   [22] tarumaṇal ñemiriya tirunāṟu oruciṟaip
   pavaḻat tūṇattup pacumpoṟ ceñcuvart
   tikaḻoḷi nittilac cittira vitāṉattu,
   viḷaṅkoḷi paranta paḷiṅku cey maṇṭapattu,
   [23] tuḷaṅkumā ṉūrtit tūmalarp paḷḷi
   [24] veṇṭirai virinta veṇṇiṟac cāmarai
   koṇṭu iru maruṅkum [25]kōtaiyar vīca,
   utayakumaraṉ vīṟṟiruntāṉ. avaṉpāl, cittirāpati, maṇimēkalai yiṉ iḷamaic
   cevviyum, āṭalpāṭalkaḷil niṟainta pulamaiyum aḻakuṟak kūṟi, avaḷ
   ulakavaṟaviyil iruntu aṟam ceyvataṉaiyum eṭuttu moḻintāḷ. kaṭalil kalam
   kaviḻappeṟṟa oruvaṉukkup periya puṇai kiṭaittāṟpōṉṟa ic ceytiyaik
   kēḷviyuṟṟa avaṉ, muṉpu am maṇimēkalaiyaik kaṇṭatumutal, maṇimēkalā
   teyvam tōṉṟi, aracumuṟai kūṟi akaṟṟiyatu īṟāka naṭantavai yellām colli,
   taṉ maiya laṟivaip pulappaṭuttiṉāṉ.
   cittirāpati avaṉ kūṟiyatu kēṭṭuc ciṟunakai ceytu, “aracē, ivai yāvum
   kāmak kaḷḷāṭṭil nikaḻum iṭaiyīṭukaḷ. ivaipōl vaṉa palavum tēvarkkum
   nikaḻntuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu kūṟi, intiraṉ āyiraṅkaṇ peṟṟatum, tīkkaṭavuḷiṉ
   maṉaiyāḷ, muṉimakaḷir eḻu varuḷ aruntatiyoḻiya ēṉaiyōr pōlat tōṉṟi, ak
   kaṭavuḷiṉ kāmattuyar tuṭaittatum avaṉ aṟiya uraittāḷ. mēlum avaḷē
   kūṟaluṟṟu,
   [26] vāṭṭiṟaṟ kurucil !
   kaṉṉik kāvalum, [27] kaṭiyiṟ kāvalum,
   taṉṉuṟu kaṇavaṉ cāvuṟiṟ kāvalum
   niṟaiyiṟ kāttup piṟarpiṟark kāṇātu,
   koṇṭō ṉallatu teyvamum pēṇāp
   peṇṭirtam kuṭiyil piṟantā ḷallaḷ;
   nāṭavar kāṇa nallaraṅ kēṟi,
   āṭalum pāṭalum aḻakum kāṭṭic
   [28] curuppunāṇ karuppuvil aruppukkaṇai tūvac
   [29] cerukkayal neṭuṅkaṇ curukkuvalaip paṭuttuk
   kaṇṭōr neñcam koṇṭakam pukkup
   [30] paṇtēr moḻiyiṟ payaṉ pala vāṅki
   vaṇṭiṉ tuṟakkum [31]koṇṭi makaḷiraip
   [32] pāṉmaiyiṟ piṇittup paṭiṟṟurai yaṭakkutal
   kōṉ muṟai yaṉṟō kumaraṟku,
   eṉṟu av vatayakumaraṉ uḷḷam piṟaḻntu am maṇimēkalaiyai viṭātu
   toṭarumāṟu tūṇṭiṉāḷ.
   utayakumāraṉ, uṭaṉē, viraipari pūṭṭiya neṭuntēr ēṟi, maṇi mēkalai
   yirunta iṭattai yaṭaintāṉ. āṅkē, paciyāl vāṭu vōrkkup peruñcōṟu
   vaḻaṅkikkoṇṭirunta maṇimēkalaiyaik kaṇṭu, iṭaṅkaḻi kāmattaṉāy, avaḷai
   aṇmi,
   uṭampōṭu eṉ ṟaṉ uḷḷakam pukuntu eṉ
   [33] neñcam kavarnta vañcak kaḷvi,
   [34] nōṟṟūṇ vāḻkkaiyiṉ nocitavam tāṅki
   [35] ēṟṟūṇ virumpiya kāraṇam eṉ?
   eṉa viṉavum eṇṇamuṭaiyaṉāy, vāyviṭṭu,
   nallāy, eṉ kol naṟṟavam purintatu? –
   collāy eṉṟu tuṇintuṭaṉ kēṭpa,
   maṇimēkalai, "ivaṉ paṇṭaippiṟappil eṉakkuk kaṇavaṉāṉa irākulaṉ; ivaṉai
   yāṉ vaṇaṅkalum, ivaṉ eṉ kaippaṟṟalum kuṟṟamākā" eṉa eṇṇiṉaḷ. eṉiṉum,
   maṉam naṭuṅki,
   kēṭṭatu moḻivēṉ: [36]kēḷvi yāḷariṉ
   [37] tōṭṭa ceviyai nī yākuvai yām eṉil,
   eṉṟu muṉmoḻintu, piṉpu,
   piṟattalum mūttalum piṇippaṭ ṭiraṅkalum
   iṟattalum uṭaiyatu; [38] iṭumpaik koḷkalam;
   makkaḷ yākkai itu, eṉa uṇarntu
   mikka nallaṟam virumputal purintēṉ;
   [39] maṇṭamar murukkuṅ kaḷiṟaṉai yārkkup
   peṇṭir kūṟum pēraṟi vuṇṭō ?
   kēṭṭaṉai yāyiṉ, [40] vēṭṭatu ceyka
   eṉṟu colliviṭṭu, am maṇimēkalai arukirunta campāpati kōyiṟkuḷ nuḻaintu,
   at teyvattait toḻutu, maṇimēkalā teyvam kaṟpitta vēṟṟurukkoḷḷum
   mantirattai ōti, kāya caṇṭikaiyiṉ vaṭivukoṇṭu, piccaippāttirattuṭaṉ
   veḷiyē vantāḷ. avaḷaik kaṇṭa utayakumaraṉ uṇmai yuṇarāṉāy, “ik
   kāyacaṇṭikaipāl ip pāttirattait tantu maṇimēkalai maṟain taṉaḷ " eṉṟu
   niṉaintu, tāṉum, ac campāpati muṉ ceṉṟu niṉṟu,
   piccaip pāttiram, [41]perumpaci yuḻanta
   kāyacaṇ ṭikaitaṉ kaiyiṟ kāṭṭi
   [42] māyaiyiṉ oḷitta maṇimē kalai taṉai
   īṅ [43] kim maṇṇīṭ ṭiyāreṉa uṇarkēṉ;
   āṅkavaḷ ivaḷ eṉṟu aruḷā yāyiṭiṉ,
   paṉṉāḷāyiṉum [44]pāṭu kiṭappēṉ;
   iṉṉum kēḷāy; [45] imaiyōr pāvāy!
   [46] pavaḷac cevvāyt tavaḷavāḷ nakaiyum,
   añcaṉam cērāc ceṅkayal neṭuṅkaṇum,
   [47] murintu kaṭaineriya varintacilaip puruvamum,
   [48] kuvimuṭ karuviyum, kōṇamum, kūr nuṉaik
   kavaimuṭ karuviyu mākik kaṭikoḷak
   [49] kalvip pākariṉ kāppuvalai ōṭṭi
   [50] valvāy yāḻiṉ melli tiṉ viḷaṅka,
   [51] mutukkuṟai mutumoḻi eṭuttuk kāṭṭi,
   [52] putukkōḷ yāṉai vēṭṭam vāytteṉa
   mutiyā ḷuṉ ṟaṉ kōṭṭam pukunta
   mativāḷ mukattu maṇimēkalai taṉai
   yoḻiyap pōkēṉ; [53] uṉ aṭi toṭṭēṉ;
   itu kuṟai,
   eṉak kuṟaiyirantu niṟpāṉāyiṉaṉ.
   ---
   [1]. koṭuntuyar - mikka tuṉpam.
   [2]. mātavar paḷḷi - aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷ uṟaiyumiṭam.
   [3]. nakutakkaṉṟu - piṟar eḷḷi nakaittaṟkuriya toṉṟām.
   [4]. takku - takkatu. eḷḷurai - ikaḻcciyurai.
   [5]. kātalaṉ vīya - kōvalaṉ iṟakka.
   [6]. pōtal ceyyā - nīṅkāta.
   [7]. naḷiyirum poykai - mikka nīr niṟainta poykai.
   [8]. muḷiyeri - mikka tī. mutukuṭi - paḻaṅkuṭi.
   [9]. kait tūṇ vāḻkkai kaṭaviyam - kaiyiṭattatākiya uṇavaip peṟṟuṇṭu
   vāḻum takutiyuṭaiyōm.
   [10]. pāṉmaiyiṉ - muṟaimaiyuṭaiya.
   [11]. naṟuntātu - naṟiya tēṉ. nayaṉ - tēṉākiya pacai.
   [12]. vaṟumpū - tēṉ illāta vaṟiya pū.
   [13]. viṉai yoḻikālai - nalviṉaippayaṉ tirum kālattē tāṉum nīṅki viṭum.
   [14]. pōtu aviḻ cevvi - maṇattukkuriya camayam. maṇimēkalaiyai valliyeṉ
   ṟataṉāl, avaḷ maṇattukkuriya cevvi eytiya nilaiyai, .. pōtaviḻ cevvi"
   yeṉ ṟāḷ. utayakumaraṉām vaṇṭu eṉka.
   [15]. citaiyā uḷḷam - vēṟoṉṟāl aḻiyāta uḷḷam.
   [16]. kaṭiñai - piccaikkalam. [17]. piccaimākkaḷ - piccaiyeṭuttuṇpavar.
   [18]. cuṭumaṇ - ceṅkal. araṅku - nāṭakavaraṅku.
   [19]. vaṭu - kuṟṟam. kuṭik kuṟṟappaṭṭa makaḷir talaimēl ceṅkal ēḻu ēṟṟi
   araṅkucūḻa varuvittal paṇṭaimarapu.
   [20]. maṉaiyakam pukā marapiṉaṉ - maṉaiyakat tuṭpukēṉ eṉṟavāṟu.
   [21]. arava vaṇṭu - icai yolikkum vaṇṭu. tēṉ, vaṇṭu, curumpu. miṇīṟu
   eṉpaṉa vaṇṭiṉ vakai.
   [22]. tarumaṇal ñemiriya - putu maṇal parappiya. tirunāṟu oru ciṟai -
   aḻaku tikaḻum oru pakkattē .
   vitāṉam - mēṟkaṭṭi. poṉṉāl cuvarum, pavaḻattāl tūṇum, muttāl vitāṉamum
   viḷaṅka, paḷiṅkiṉāl ceyta maṇṭapam eṉka.
   [23]. tuḷaṅkum māṉ - oḷiyāl acaitalaiyuṭaiya ciṅkam. ūrti - īṇṭu
   irukkai yeṉṉum poruḷatu.
   [24]. veṇṭirai virinta - veḷḷiya alai pōla virinta.
   [25]. kōtaiyar - makaḷir.
   [26]. vāḷ tiṟal kurucil - vāṭpōril cīriya tiṟalpaṭaitta araca kumaraṉē.
   [27]. kaṭiyiṟ kāval - tirumaṇattāl piṟakkum kāval.
   nallaraṅku - ēḻukōl akalamum, eṇkōl nīḷamum, orukōl kuṟaṭṭuyaramum
   vāytali raṇṭumuṭaiya nāṭakamēṭai.
   [28]. curumpākiya nāṇ pūṭṭi, karumpākiya villai vaḷaittu, arumpākiya
   ampu toṭuttu eyya.
   [29]. cerukkayal neṭuṅkaṇ - cerucceyyum kayal mīṉ pōlum uṇṭa kaṇṇāl.
   curukku valai paṭuttu neñcam akappaṭuttukkoṇṭu - curukku valai vīci
   neñciṉai ataṉkaṇ akappaṭuttikkoṇṭu.
   [30]. paṇ tērmoḻiyiṉ - paṇ ṇicaipōlum iṉiya coṟkaḷāl. payaṉ - poruḷ.
   vaṇṭiṉ - vaṇṭupōla.
   [31]. koṇṭi makaḷir - poruḷaik kavarntu koḷḷutalaiyuṭaiya makaḷir.
   [32], pāṉmaiyiṉ - koḷḷutaṟkuriya muṟaippaṭi. paṭiṟṟurai - poyyurai.
   [33]. utayakumaraṉ neñcil niṉaikkuntōṟum avaṉ maṉakkaṇṇil maṇimēkalai
   taṉ muḻumēṉiyum tōṉṟa niṟṟaliṉ, uṭampōṭu...kaḷvi" eṉkiṉṟāṉ. taṉ
   neñciṉai avaḷ kavarntēkiyataṉait tāṉ aṟiyāmai yiṉ, "vañcak kaḷvi"
   eṉṟāṉ,
   [34]. nōṟṟūṇ vāḻkkai - viratam kāttup piccai yēṟṟuṇṭu vāḻum vāḻkkai .
   nocital - nuṇukal. tavamuṭaiyār toḻil tavat tiṉmēl niṉṟatu.
   [35]. ēṟṟūṇ - irantuṇṭal.
   [36]. 'kēḷviyāḷariṉ - nuṇṇiya nūṟporuḷaik kēṭṭa cāṉṟōrka ḷāl.
   [37]. tōṭṭa cevi - tuḷaikkappaṭṭa cevi. pala nuṇporuḷaik kēṭṭup
   payiṉṟōr ceviyaik ' kēḷviyāl tōṭkappaṭṭa cevi,, yeṉ ṟal marapu. "
   kēḷviyāl tōṭkappaṭāta cevi " eṉpatu tiruk . kuṟaḷ.
   [38]. iṭumpaik koḷkalam- tuṉpamellām koṇṭuvaikkum kalam.
   [39]. maṇṭu amar murukkum kaḷiṟu - neruṅkiya pōril muntuṟṟuc ceṉṟu
   porum kaḷiṟṟiyāṉai.
   [40]. vēṭṭatu - virumpiyatu.
   [41]. perum paci - yāṉait tī eṉṉum kōyā luṇṭākiya paciyāl.
   [42]. māyaiyiṉ - māyamāka.
   [43. im maṇṇiṭṭu yār - im maṇṇāṟ ceyta pāvaikaḷuḷ yār.
   [44]. pāṭukiṭappēṉ - varaṅkiṭappēṉ.
   [45]. imaiyōr pāvai - tēvarkaḷuṭaiya talaivi,
   [46]. pavaḷac cevvāy - pavaḷampōl civanta vāy. tavaḷa vāḷ nakai -
   veḷḷiya oḷiporuntiya paṟkaḷ,
   [47]. murintu - vaḷaintu.
   [48]. kuvimuḷ, kōṇam (tōṭṭi), kavaimuḷ mūṉṟum yāṉaiyai aṭakkum karuvi,
   kaṭi koḷa - kāval koḷḷa.
   vāḷ nakai, kuvimuḷḷum; kaṇ kōṇamum; puruvam, kavai, muḷḷu mākak koḷka.
   [49]. kalvippākar - kalviyākiya pākar. kāppu valai ōṭṭi - kātta lākiya
   valaiyaic citaittu.
   [50]. " valvāy yāḻiṉ " eṉṟāṉ, valiya yāṉai yāḻicaikku mayaṅki
   vaṇaṅkutal pōlat taṉṉait taṉ yāḻicaipōlum moḻiyāl vaṇak kiṉamai tōṉṟa.
   [51]. mutukkuṟai mutumoḻi - pēraṟivu cāṉṟa aṟivu moḻi.
   [52]. putukkōḷ yāṉai - putitākakkoṇṭa yāṉai.
   [53]. uṉ aṭi toṭṭēṉ - uṉ tiruvaṭi toṭṭuc cūḷ ceytēṉ.
   ---------

          19. ciṟaikkōṭṭam aṟakkōṭṭam ākkiya kātai

   "maṇimēkalai yoḻiyap pōkēṉ; uṉ aṭi toṭṭēṉ " eṉṟu campāpati muṉ vañciṉam
   kūṟiya utayakumaraṉ kēṭka, aṅ kuḷḷa cittiraṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟil niṉṟu,
   [1]ēṭaviḻ tārōy ! emkō makaḷ muṉ
   nāṭātu tuṇintu [2] nānal kūrntaṉai
   eṉṟoru teyvam kūṟiṟṟu. ataṉaik kēṭṭa utayakumāṉ maṉam kalaṅki,
   meyvarunti,
   aṅkavaḷ taṉ tiṟam [3] ayarppāy eṉṟē
   ceṅkōl kāṭṭiya teyvamum tippiyam;
   [4] paiyara valkul palarpaci kaḷaiyak
   kaiyil ēntiya pāttiram tippiyam;
   [5] muttai mutalvi aṭipiḻait tāy eṉac
   cittiram uraitta itū um tippiyam;
   innilai yellām [6] iḷaṅkoṭi ceytiyiṉ
   piṉṉaṟivām,
   eṉṟu eṇṇikkoṇṭē avviṭam viṭṭup peyarntu nīṅkiṉaṉ. mālaippōtum vantatu.
   iṉi, vēṟṟurukkoṇṭu niṉṟa maṇimēkalai, palapaṭaniṉaintu,
   mātavi makaḷāy maṉṟam tiritariṉ,
   kāvalaṉ makaṉō [7] kaiviṭa līyāṉ;
   [8] kāypaci yāṭṭi kāyacaṇ ṭikaiyeṉa
   ūrmuḻu taṟiyum uruvam koṇṭē,
   [9] āṟṟā mākkaṭku āṟṟum tuṇaiyāki
   ēṟṟalum iṭutalum irappōr kaṭaṉ; avar
   mēṟceṉ ṟaḷittal [10] viḻuttakait teṉṟē
   nūṟporu ḷuṇarntōr [11] nuṉittaṉar,
   eṉṟu karuti, vēntaṉatu āṇai piḻaittōrai oṟukkum ciṟaik kōṭṭam pukuntu,
   āṅkē paciyāl varunti melintu kiṭantōrk kup pēruṇavu tantu pacippiṇiyaip
   pōkkiṉaḷ. avaḷ pāttiram oṉṟē koṇṭu mikap palarkkup pacitīra uṇavaḷittu
   niṟkum ceya laic ciṟaik kōṭṭak kāvalar kaṇṭu peruviyappuk koṇṭu, ic
   ceytiyai aracaṟku uṇarttuvatu takkatu eṉṟu eṇṇi avaṉ pāṟ ceṉṟaṉar.
   akkālaiyil, aracar perumāṉ taṉ maṉaivi cīrttiyeṉpāḷuṭaṉ,
   viḷaiyāṭṭukkaruti yoru pūmpoḻiṟkuc ceṉṟiruntāṉ. aṅkē,
   kompart tumpi [12]ku ḻalicai kāṭṭap
   [13] poṅkar vaṇṭiṉam nalliyāḻ ceyya,
   varikkuyil pāṭa mā mayil āṭum,
   aḻakiya pūmpantar oṉṟu aracap perumakkaṭku iṉiya kāṭci payantatu.
   aracaṉ,
   [14] puṇartuṇai nīṅkiya poykai yaṉṉamoṭu
   maṭamayiṟ pēṭaiyum tōkaiyum kūṭi
   [15] iruciṟai virittāṅku eḻuntuṭaṉ koṭpaṉa
   oruciṟaik kaṇṭu āṅku uḷ makiḻ veyti,
   māmaṇi vaṇṇaṉum [16] tammuṉum piññaiyum
   āṭiya kuravai iḵtāmeṉa nōkkiyum,
   [17] kōṅkalar cērnta māṅkaṉi taṉṉai,
   [18] pāṅkuṟa irunta palpoṟi maññaiyaic
   cempoṉ taṭṭil tīmpāl ēntip
   paiṅkiḷi yūṭṭum [19] ōr pāvaiyām eṉṟum,
   [20] aṇimalarp pūmpoḻil akavayiṉ irunta
   [21] piṇavukkuraṅ kēṟṟip perumatar maḻaikkaṇ,
   [22] maṭavōrk kiyaṟṟiya māmaṇi yūcal
   [23] kaṭuva ṉūkkuvatu kaṇṭunakai yeytiyum
   iṉpuṟṟuc ciṟantāṉ. kūttar, avinayappulavar, nāṭaka nūl vallavar,
   icaippulavar, taṇṇumaip pulavar, kuḻal icaippōr, pāṭṭicai cuvaippōr,
   muttukkōppavar, cāntam camaippōr, pūttoṭup pōr mutaliya palarum
   aracaṉaic cūḻaviruntu iṉpuṟuttiṉar. aracaṉ, avviṉpattāl ārāmai yeyti,
   mēlum ceṉṟu,
   kuruntum [24] taḷavum tiruntu malarc ceruntiyum,
   [25] murukuviri mullaiyum [26]karuviḷam poṅkarum,
   poruntupu niṉṟu tiruntu nakai ceytu
   [27]kuṟuṅkāl nakulamum neṭuñcevi muyalum
   [28]piṟaḻntu pāy māṉum [29] iṟumpu akalā veṟiyum
   aracikkuk kāṭṭi makiḻcci mikkāṉ. piṉpu,
   entirak kiṇaṟum, [30] iṭumkaṟ kuṉṟamum,
   vantu vīḻ aruviyum, malarppūm pantarum,
   [31] parappu nīrp poykaiyum, [32]karappu nīrk kēṇiyum,
   [33] oḷittuṟai yiṭaṅkaḷum paḷikkaṟaip paḷḷiyum
   piṟavumākiya iṭaṅkaṭkuc ceṉṟu viḷaiyāṭṭayarntu, kaṭaiciyil,
   [34] makata viṉaiñarum marāṭṭak kammarum,
   avantik kollarum yavaṉat taccarum
   [35]taṇ tamiḻ viṉaiñar tammoṭu kūṭik
   koṇṭu iṉitu iyaṟṟiya kaṇkavar ceyviṉaip
   pavaḷat tiraḷ kāl paṉmaṇip [36]pōtikait
   [37] tavaḷa nittilat tāmam tāḻnta
   [38] kōṇac canti māṇviṉai vitāṉattut
   [39] tamaṉiyam vēynta vakaipeṟu vaṉappiṉ
   cantaṉa meḻukiya pacumpoṉ maṇṭapattai yaṭaintu iṉitirun tāṉ. akkālattē
   ciṟaikkōṭṭak kāvalar vantu, cēymaikkaṇ niṉṟu vaṇaṅkip piṉ tirumuṉpu
   aṇuki, “ etirttu mēlvanta cēraraiyum pāṇṭiyaraiyum,
   [40] ārpuṉai teriyal iḷaṅkōṉ taṉṉāl
   [41] kāriyāṟṟuk koṇṭa kāval veṇkuṭai
   [42] vali keḻu taṭakkai māvaṇ kiḷḷi !
   [43]oḷiyoṭu vāḻi ūḻitō ṟūḻi
   vāḻi yeṅkō! maṉṉavar peruntakai!
   kēḷ, itu maṉṉō; keṭukaniṉ pakaiñar;
   mānakart tiriyum ōr [44] vampa mātar
   aruñciṟaik kōṭṭat [45] takavayiṟ pukuntu
   [46] perumpeyar maṉṉa! niṉpeyar vāḻtti
   [47] aiyap pāttiram oṉṟu koṇṭu, āṅku, [48]
   moykoḷ mākkaḷ mocikka ūṇ curantaṉaḷ;
   ūḻitō ṟūḻi ulakam kāttu
   vāḻi eṅkō maṉṉava,
   eṉṟu terivittu niṉṟaṉar. aracaṉ, viraiyac ceṉṟu am maṭak koṭiyai iṅkē
   aḻaittu varuka eṉa, kāvalar ceṉṟu uraitta lum maṇimēkalai, aracaṉmuṉ
   pōntu avaṉai vāḻtti niṉṟāḷ.
   vēntaṉ :
   [49] tāṅkarum taṉmait tavattōy! nīyār?
   [50] yāṅku ākiyatu iv vēntiya kaṭiñai?
   maṇimēkalai :
   [51] viraittār vēntē !. nī nīṭu vāḻi
   [52] viñcai makaḷyāṉ; [53]viḻavaṇi mūtūr
   vañcam tirintēṉ; vāḻiyar peruntakai !
   [54]vāṉam vāykka; maṇ vaḷam perukuka;
   tītiṉ ṟāka kōmakaṟku caṅku; ītu
   aiyak kaṭiñai; [55] ampala maruṅku ōr
   teyvam tantatu; tippiya māyatu;
   yāṉait tīnōy arumpaci keṭuttatu;
   [56] ūṉuṭai mākkaṭku uyirmaruntu itu.
   vēntaṉ :- iḷaṅkoṭi; yāṉ ceyaṟpālatu eṉ?
   maṇimēkalai:
   ciṟaiyōr kōṭṭam [57]cīttu, aruḷneñcattu
   aṟavōrkku [58]ākkumatu; vāḻiyar
   itukēṭṭu uḷḷam icainta aracar peruntakai, ac ciṟaik kōṭṭattuḷḷārkkuc
   ciṟaivīṭu ceytu, ak kōṭṭattaiyum iṭittut tūymai ceytu, aṟavōr vāḻum
   aṟakkōṭṭamākkiṉaṉ.

               20. utayakumaraṉai vāḷāl eṟinta kātai

   maṇimēkalaiyāl aracaṉ ciṟaikkōṭṭattaic cīttuc ceyta aṟak kōṭṭattē,
   [1] poruḷpuri neñciṟ pulavōṉ kōyilum
   aruḷ puri neñcattu aṟavōr paḷḷiyum
   [2] aṭṭiṟ cālaiyum [3]aruntunar cālaiyum
   uḷavāyiṉa. in nikaḻcci muṟṟum aṟintāṉ utayakumaraṉ. uṭaṉē
   avaṉ, avaḷai, evvāṟēṉum koḷḷal vēṇṭumeṉṟu karuti,
   [4] matiyōr eḷḷiṉum maṉṉavaṉ kāyiṉum,
   [5] potiyil nīṅkiya poḻutiṟ ceṉṟu,
   paṟṟiṉaṉ koṇṭu eṉ poṟṟēr ēṟṟi,
   [6] kaṟṟaṟi viccaiyum kēṭṭu avaḷ uraikkum
   [7] mutukkuṟai mutumoḻi kēṭkuvaṉ eṉṟē
   maṉaṅkoṇṭu, maṇimēkalai taṅkiyirunta ulakavaṟavikkuc ceṉ ṟāṉ. akkālai,
   kāyacaṇṭikaiyiṉ kaṇavaṉāṉa kāñcaṉaṉ, muṉivaṉ uraittavāṟu paṉṉīrāṇṭu
   kaḻintum taṉ maṉaivi tirumpa vārāmai kaṇṭu, in nakarkkaṇ avaḷaip
   palaviṭaṅkaḷilum tēṭit tirintu muṭivil, piccaip pāttiram ēntip
   palarkkum uṇavūṭṭi varuvatu kaṇṭu taṉ paḻaimaik kiḻamaitōṉṟap pala
   uraiyāṭiṉaṉ. utaya kumaraṉum aṅkē uṭaṉiruntāṉ. maṇimēkalai kāyacaṇṭikai
   vaṭi vil niṉṟāḷāyiṉum, avaḷ, akkāñcaṉaṉpāl cellātu, utaya kumaraṉ
   mukanōkki, avaṉ uḷḷattaiyum māṟṟa niṉaintāḷ. ap pōtu avvaḻiyē pōnta
   toṇṭukiḻavi yoruttiyaik kāṭṭi,
   [8] taṇṇaṟal vaṇṇam tirintuvē ṟāki
   veṇmaṇa lākiya kūntal kāṇāy;
   [9] piṟai nutal vaṇṇam kāṇā yō, nī,
   naraimaiyiṉ tiraitōl [10] takaiyiṉ ṟāyatu;
   [11] viṟalviṟ puruvam ivaiyum kāṇāy,
   [12] iṟaviṉ uṇaṅkal pōṉṟu vē ṟāyiṉa;
   [13] kaḻunīrk kaṇ, kāṇ, [14] vaḻunīr cumantaṉa;
   [15] kumiḻmūkku, ivai,kāṇ, umiḻcī oḻukkuva;
   niraimut taṉaiya [16] nakaiyum kāṇāy,
   [17] curaivit tēyppap piṟaḻntuvē ṟāyiṉa;
   [18] ilavitaḻc cevvāy, kāṇā yō, nī,
   [19] pulavup puṇ pōl pulālpuṟat tiṭuvatu;
   [20] vaḷḷait tāḷpōl vaṭikātu ivai kāṇ,
   uḷḷūṉ vāṭiya uṇaṅkal pōṉṟaṉa;
   [21] iṟumpūtu cāṉṟa mulaiyum kāṇāy,
   veṟumpai pōla vīḻntu vē ṟāyiṉa;
   [22] tāḻntoci teṅkiṉ maṭalpōl [23]tiraṅki,
   vīḻntaṉa, iḷa[24]vēyt tōḷum kāṇāy,
   narampoṭu viṭutōl [25]ukirttoṭar kaḻaṉṟu,
   tiraṅkiya viralkaḷ ivaiyum kāṇāy; -
   vāḻait taṇṭē pōṉṟa [26]kuṟaṅkiṇai,
   tāḻait taṇṭiṉ uṇaṅkal kāṇāy;
   [27] āvak kaṇaikkāl kāṇā yō, nī,
   mēviya narampōṭu eṉpupuṟaṅ [28]kāṭṭuva;
   [29] taḷiraṭi vaṇṇam kāṇā yō, nī,
   [30] muḷimutir teṅkiṉ utirkāy uṇaṅkal,
   pūviṉum cāntiṉum [31]pulāl maṟaittu yāttu,
   [32] tūciṉum aṇiyiṉum tollōr vakutta
   vañcam teriyāy, maṉṉavaṉ makaṉ !
   eṉṟu avaḷatu uṟuppukkaḷiṉ paṇṭai vaṉappum, iṉṟaiya aḻi vum eṭuttu
   viḷakkiṉaḷ.
   ic ceytiyaik kāñcaṉaṉ kaṇṭu maṉattē poṟāmaiyum ciṉamum poṅki mikat taṉ
   neñciṟkuḷ,
   [33]taṟpā rāṭṭum eṉ coṟpayaṉ koḷḷāḷ;
   piṟaṉ piṉ cellum; [34] piṟaṉ pōl nōkkum;
   [35] matukkamaḻ alaṅkal maṉṉavaṉ makaṟku
   mutukkuṟai mutumoḻi eṭuttuk kāṭṭip
   [36] pavaḷak kaṭikaiyil tavaḷavāḷ nakaiyum,
   kuvaḷaic ceṅkaṇum [37]kuṟippoṭu vaḻā aḷ;
   īṅku ivaṉ kātala ṉātaliṉ, ēntiḻai
   īṅku oḻintaṉaḷ
   eṉa niṉaintu, avviṭattē orucār taṅkiyiruntāṉ. utayakumā ṉum, am
   maṇimēkalai yuraitta mutukkuṟai mutumoḻikaḷaic cevi yiṟ koḷḷātu,
   vaḷai cēr ceṅkai maṇimē kalaiyē,
   kāyacaṇṭikaiyāyk kaṭiñai yēnti,
   [38] māya viñcaiyiṉ maṉammayak kuṟuttaṉaḷ;
   ampala maruṅkil ayarntu aṟi vuraitta iv
   [39] vampavaṉ taṉṉoṭu iv vaikiruḷ oḻiyāḷ;
   iṅku ivaḷ ceyti [40]iṭaiyiruḷ yāmattu,
   vantu aṟikuvaṉ,
   eṉa uṭkoṇṭu, iṭaiyiruḷ yāmattē mīṇṭuvarat tuṇintu tērēṟic ceṉṟāṉ.
   aṟṟaippōtu kaḻintatu; iṭaiyiruḷ yāmamum. eytuvatā yiṟṟu. utayakumaraṉ
   araṇmaṉaiyiṉ nīṅki, maṇimēkalai yirunta am palam nōkki vantāṉ. kāñcaṉaṉ
   maṇimēkalai yirunta iṭattiṟ kēkum neṟikkaṇ uṟakkamiṉṟik kiṭantāṉ;
   ataṉai yuṇarātu pōnta utayakumaraṉ,
   [41] vēka ventī nākam kiṭanta
   [42] pōkuyar puṟṟaḷai pukuvāṉ pōla,
   [43] ākam tōynta cāntu [44] alar uṟutta
   [45]ūḻaṭi yiṭṭu ataṉ uḷḷakam pukutalum,
   āṅkumuṉ ṉirunta alartār viñcaiyaṉ,
   " īṅku ivaṉ vantaṉaṉ ivaḷ pāl " eṉṟē,
   veñciṉa aravam [46]nañceyiṟu arumpa,
   taṉ peru vekuḷiyiṉ eḻuntu [47]pai viritteṉa,
   [48] iruntōṉ eḻuntu perumpiṉ ceṉṟu, avaṉ
   [49] curumpu aṟai maṇittōḷ tuṇiya vīci,
   uḷḷē ceṉṟu, kāyacaṇṭikaiyākiya maṇimēkalaiyait tāṉē valitiṟ kaikkoṇṭu
   cellak karutic ceṉṟāṉ. appōḻtil āṅkirunta kantiṟpāvai,
   aṇukal, aṇukal; viñcaik kāñcaṉa!
   maṇimēkalai avaḷ; maṟainturu veytiṉaḷ;
   kāyacaṇ ṭikaitaṉ kaṭumpaci nīṅki,
   vāṉam pōvuḻi vantatu kēḷāy
   eṉṟu toṭaṅkik kāyacaṇṭikai vāṉavaḻiyē celluṅkāl vinta malaiyiliruntu
   ataṉaik kākkum vintākaṭikai, taṉmēl kāyacaṇṭi kaiyiṉ cāyai tākka,
   ataṉāl, avaḷai yiḻuttut taṉ vayiṟṟil aṭakkikkoṇṭāḷ.
   [50]kaimmai koḷḷēl, kāñcaṉa ! itukēḷ :
   ūḻviṉai vantu iṅku utaya kumaraṉai,
   āruyir uṇṭatu; āyiṉum, aṟiyāy :
   [51] vevviṉai ceytāy, viñcaik kāñcaṉa !
   avviṉai niṉṉaiyum akalātu āṅkuṟum
   eṉṟatu. itukēṭṭu viñcaiyaṉum varuntiya uḷḷattaṉāyt taṉ viñcaiyarulakai
   yaṭaintāṉ.

   [34]. piṟaṉ pōl - ayalāṉ pōla.
   [35]. matukkamaḻ alaṅkal - tēṉ maṇakkum mālai.
   [36]. pavaḷak kaṭikai - pavaḷat tuṇṭam. [37]. kuṟippu - iṅkitam.
   [38]. māyaviñcai - māyavittai . [39]. vampalaṉ - putiyōṉ; ayalāṉ.

   [46]. eyiṟu nañcu arumpa - pal viṭattaik kakka.

                 21. kantiṟpāvai varuvatu uraitta kātai

   campāpati kōyiliṉ uḷḷē kiṭanta maṇimēkalai kāñcaṉaṉ ceytiyum,
   utayakumaraṉ vīḻcciyum, kantiṟpāvai ak kāñcaṉaṟ kuraittatum kēṭṭu,
   eḻuntu, taṉ uṇmai vaṭivukoṇṭu veḷip pōntu, aracakumaraṉatu
   uṭalaikkaṇṭu, tuyarmikuntu,
   [1] tiṭṭi viṭamuṇa niṉṉuyir pōmnāḷ
   [2]kaṭṭaḻal īmattu eṉ uyir cuṭṭēṉ;
   uvavaṉa maruṅkil niṉpāl uḷḷam
   [3] tavirvilē ṉātaliṉ, talai makaḷ tōṉṟi
   maṇippal lavattiṭai eṉṉai āṅ kuyttup
   [4]piṇippaṟu mātavaṉ pīṭikai kāṭṭi
   eṉ piṟap puṇarnta eṉ muṉ tōṉṟi,
   uṉ piṟap pellām [5]oḻiviṉ ṟuraittaliṉ,
   piṟantōr iṟattalum, iṟantōr piṟattalum,
   [6] aṟantaru cālpum, maṟantaru tuṉpamum,
   yāṉ niṉak kuraittu niṉ [7]iṭarviṉai yoḻikkak
   kāyacaṇ ṭikaivaṭi vāṉēṉ, kātala!
   [8] vaivāḷ viñcaiyaṉ mēyakkuṟu vekuḷiyiṉ
   vevviṉai yuruppa viḷintaṉaiyō
   eṉac collip pulampaluṟṟaṉaḷ. appōḻtil, kantiṟpāvai maṇimēkalaiyai
   yaḻaittu,
   cellal, cellal; [9]cēyari neṭuṅkaṇ!
   [10] alliyan tārōṉ taṉpāṟ cellal,
   niṉakku ivaṉ [11]makaṉāt tōṉṟi yatū um,
   maṉakkiṉi yāṟku nī [12] makaḷā yatū um
   paṇṭum paṇṭum palpiṟap puḷavāl;
   kaṇṭa piṟaviyē alla, kārikai!
   [13]taṭumāṟu piṟavit tāḻtaru tōṟṟam
   viṭumāṟu muyalvōy, [14] viḻumam koḷḷēl
   eṉṟu uraittatu. atukēṭṭa maṇimēkalai, "ivvampalattu uṟai yum
   teyvamākiya nī, ceṉṟa piṟappil ivaṉ tiṭṭiviṭattāl uyiriḻantatum,
   ippiṟappil iṉṟu īṅku viñcaiyaṉāl veṭṭuṇṭu vīḻntatum aṟivāyaṉṟō .
   aṟintaṉaiyāyiṉ, ivaṉ vāḷ vāy vīḻnta kāraṇam viḷampa vēṇṭukiṉṟēṉ ”
   eṉṟāḷ.
   uṭaṉē at teyvam,“muṉṉaip piṟaviyil, kāyaṅkarai eṉṉum āṟṟaṅkaraiyil, nī
   niṉ kaṇavaṉ irākulaṉōṭu iruntapōtu, aṅku uṅkaḷpāl vanta piramataruma
   muṉivaṉukku uṇavucamaittal vēṇṭi maṭaiyaṉai viṭiyalil varap paṇittīr;
   avaṉ avvāṟē varuṅkāl, aṭitaḷarntu maṭaikkalam citaiya vīḻntāṉ. ataṉai
   nōkkātu, muṉivarkkuc ceytaṟkuriya paṇikku muṭṭuppāṭu uṇṭākkiṉāṉeṉa am
   maṭai yaṉai irākulaṉ ciṉantu taṉ kaivāḷāl tuṇittuk koṉṟāṉ. ak
   kolaiviṉaiyē, avaṉai ippiṟappil viñcaiyaṉāl veṭ ṭuṇṭu vīḻac ceytatu,
   kāṇ" eṉṟu colli, mēlum, viṉaiyiṉ iyalpu ciṟitu kūṟaluṟṟa atteyvam,
   talaivaṉ kākkum, tamporuṭ ṭākiya
   [16]avala vevviṉai yeṉpōr aṟiyār;
   aṟañcey kātal aṉpiṉi ṉāyiṉum,
   [17]maṟamcey tuḷateṉiṉ valviṉai yoḻiyātu;
   āṅku avviṉai vantu aṇukuṅ kālait
   [18] tīṅkuṟum uyirē ceyviṉai maruṅkiṉ
   mīṇṭuvaru piṟappil mīḷiṉum mīḷum;
   āṅku avviṉai kāṇ, āyiḻai ! [19]kaṇavaṉai
   īṅku vantiv viṭarcey toḻintatu,
   eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭu, ataṉmēlum, maṇimēkalaikku nikaḻaviruppaṉa vaṟṟaiyum
   muṉpē moḻiyat toṭaṅki,
   iṉṉuṅ kēḷāy : iḷaṅkoṭi nallāy!
   maṉṉavaṉ makaṟku [20]varuntu tuyar eyti
   mātavar uṇarttiya vāymoḻi kēṭṭu,
   kāvalaṉ niṉṉaiyum [21]kāval cey tāṅkiṭum;
   īṭu ciṟai nīkki, irācamā tēvi
   kūṭavaikkum [22]koṭpiṉa ḷāki,
   mātavi [23]mātavaṉ malaraṭi vaṇaṅkit
   tītu kūṟa avaḷ taṉṉoṭum cērntu
   mātavaṉ uraitta vāymoḻi kēṭṭuk
   [24] kātali niṉṉaiyum kāval nīkkuvaḷ;
   araicāḷ celvattu āput tiraṉ pāl,
   [25] puraiyōrp pēṇip pōkalum pōkuvai;
   pōṉā lavaṉoṭum [26]poruḷurai porunti,
   [27] mānīr vaṅkattu avaṉoṭum eḻuntu,
   [28] māyamil ceyti maṇipal lavameṉum
   tīvakattu [29]. iṉṉum cēṟalumuṇṭāl;
   tīva tilakaiyiṉ taṉ tiṟam kēṭṭuc
   [30]cāvaka maṉṉaṉ taṉ nā ṭaṭaintapiṉ āṅkat
   [31]tīvamviṭṭu aruntavaṉ vaṭivāyp
   pūṅkoṭi! vañci mānakar pukuvai;
   āṅku an nakarattu [32]aṟiporuḷ viṉāvum
   [33] ōṅkiya kēḷvi uyarntōr palarāl.
   ivvāṟu palarāya camayakkaṇakkar tam tiṟam kēṭṭu, avarkaṭkut takuvaṉa
   kūṟic ciṟappeytuvāy; ātalāl, tīviṉai vantu uṟuttalum cettōr piṟattalum
   uṇmaiyeṉṟu tuṇintu niṉ maṉamayakkam oḻivāyāka eṉṟu kūṟiṟṟu.
   ivvaṇṇam collip pōnta at teyvam, tāṉ kantiṟ pāvaiyāy niṉṟu makkaṭku
   varuvaturaittu varum tiṟattaik kūṟat toṭaṅki,
   eṉ tiṟam kēṭṭiyō : iḷaṅkoṭi nallāy!
   [34] maṉperun teyva kaṇaṅkaḷi ṉuḷḷēṉ,
   tuvatikaṉ eṉpēṉ; [35] toṉṟu mutir kantiṉ
   mayaṉ eṉak koppā vakutta pāvaiyiṉ
   nīṅkēṉ yāṉ; eṉ nilaiyatu kēḷāy;
   māntar aṟivatu vāṉavar aṟiyār;
   ōviyac cēṉaṉ eṉ [36] uṟu tuṇait tōḻaṉ
   āvatai innakarkku ārurait taṉarō ?
   avaṉuṭaṉ yāṉ ceṉṟu āṭiṭa mellām
   [37] uṭaṉuṟain tārpōl oḻiyā teḻutip
   pūvum pukaiyum [38]poruntupu puṇarntu
   [39] nānaṉi varuntaeṉ nalampā rāṭṭaliṉ
   maṇimē kalai!yāṉ varuporuḷ ellām
   tuṇivuṭaṉ uraittēṉ; eṉ col [40]tēṟuka.
   eṉṟu collavē, maṇimēkalai, " yāṉ tēṟātoḻiyēṉ; iṉi eṉakku muṭivukāṟum
   nikaḻaviruppaṉavaṟṟaiyum kūṟuka" eṉṟu kēṭṭāḷ. avvāṟē, avaṟṟaiyum collat
   toṭaṅkiya at teyvam, kāñcimānakar vaṟampaṭutalum, āṅkē maṇimēkalai
   ceṉṟu, taṉ piccaippāttirattāl uyirōmputalum, mēlum aṅku nikaḻum
   nikaḻccikaḷum, camayakkaṇakkar palaroṭu kēṭṭavaṟṟai aṟavaṇavaṭi kaṭku
   uraippatum, avar taṉ ceyal ceppuvatum, piṉpu maṇimēkalai makatanāṭṭil
   āṇmakaṉāyp piṟantu aruḷaṟam pūṇṭu, putta tēva ṉukku mutal
   māṇavaṉākutalum piṟavum kūṟi, nī muṟpiṟappil cātu cakkaramuṉivaṟku
   amutaḷitta nalviṉaip payaṉāl, maṇimēkalā teyvam niṉṉai
   maṇipallavattukkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟu putta pīṭikai kaṇṭu paḻampiṟappai nī
   yaṇarntukoḷḷumāṟu ceytatu” eṉṟum colli muṭittatu.
   ---
   [1]. tiṭṭiviṭam - kaṇṇil nañcuṭaiya pāmpu.
   [2]. kaṭṭaḻal - mikka neruppu. [3]. tavirvilēṉ - nīṅkātu celuttiṉēṉ.
   [4]. piṇippaṟu mātavaṉ - paṟṟaṟṟa mātavaṉākiya puttaṉ.
   [5]. oḻiviṉṟu - kuṟaiyāmal; ciṟitum viṭāmal.
   [6]. aṟantaru cālpu - aṟattāl uṇṭākum iṉpa niṟaivu.
   [7]. iṭar viṉai - tuṉpattaic ceyyum viṉai; kātaṟ ceykai.
   [8]. vai vāḷ - kūriya vāḷ. [9]. mayakkuṟu vekuḷi . aṟivai mayakkum
   ciṉam.
   [10]. cēyari keṭuṅkaṇ . civanta ariparanta neṭiya kaṇṇaiyuṭaiyāy.
   [11]. alliyan tār - pūviṉ akavitaḻkaḷāl toṭukkappaṭṭa mālai.
   [12]. makaṉ - kaṇavaṉ. [13]. makaḷ - maṉaivi.
   [14]. taṭumāṟu piṟavi - makaṉumakaḷumāy māṟip piṟantu iṟantu varum
   piṟavi. tāḻtaru - taṅkum.
   [15]. viḻumam - tuṉpam.
   [16]. avalavevviṉai - melivai yuṇṭupaṇṇum koṭiya viṉai. viṉaiyiṉiṉṟum
   tammait talaivaṉākiya kaṭavuḷ kāppaṉ eṉpōr aṟiyā tār eṉka. iḵtu
   iṟaivaṉoruvaṉuṇmai tuṇintu viṉaittuyar nīṅka aṉpu neṟiyil vaḻipaṭum
   caivar mutalaryiṉārkku maṟuppu.
   [17]. maṟam ceytuḷatu - pāvam ceyyappaṭṭatāyiṉ.
   [18]. tīṅku - tuṉpam. [19]. kaṇavaṉai - utayakumaraṉai.
   [20]. varuntu tuyar - piriveṇṇi varuntu taṟku ētuvākiya tuyaram.
   [21]. kāval - ciṟai.
   [22]. koṭpu - koḷkai. [23]. mātavaṉ - aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷ,
   [24]. kātali - aṉpuṭaiyaḷākiya.
   [25]. puraiyōr - uyarntōr; īṇṭuc caṅkattār. pōkalum pōkuvai - pōvāy.
   [26]. poruḷurai - aṟavurai. porunti - kēṭṭu.
   [27].. mānīr - kaṭal. [28]. māyam il ceyti - kuṟṟamillāta
   ciṟappiṉaiyuṭaiya.
   [29].. iṉṉum cēṟal - maṟupaṭiyum celvatu. maṇimēkalai āputtira ṉuṭaṉ
   puṟappaṭṭu, avaṉ vaṅkattil vara, tāṉ vicumpu vaḻiyāka maṇi pallavam
   aṭaintāḷ. uru -ām kātai kāṇka.
   [30]. cāvka maṉṉaṉ - āputtiraṉ. [31]. tīvam - maṇipallavam.
   [32]. aṟiporuḷ - aṟinta camayap poruḷ.
   [33]. ōṅkiya - uyarnta. uyarntōr palarāl - uyarntōr palar uḷar.
   [34]. maṉ - nilaipeṟṟa. kaṇam - kūṭṭam.
   [35]. toṉṟu mutir kantu - mikap paḻamaiyāṉa tūṇ, mayaṉ . teyvat taccaṉ.
   [36]. uṟu tuṇai - mikka tuṇaivaṉ.
   [37]. uṭaṉ uṟaintār - uṭaṉiruntu kaṇṭavar,
   [38]. poruntupu - porunta, [39]. nā naṉi varunta - nā mikat taṭippēṟa.
   [40]. tēṟuka - teḷivāy.
   -----------

                            22. ciṟaicey kātai

   poḻutu pularntatum, campāpatiyaiyum kantiṟpāvaiyaiyum vaḻipaṭa vantavar
   utayakumaraṉ veṭṭuṇṭu kiṭappataik kaṇṭu; āṅku vāḻum muṉivarkaḷukku
   uraittaṉar. avarkaḷ maṇi mēkalaiyai viṉavi nikaḻntataṟintu avaḷaiyum
   utayakumaraṉ uṭalaiyum ōriṭattē oḷittuviṭṭu, naṭanta ceytiyai
   aracarkkuṇarttac ceṉṟaṉar. ceṉṟavaruḷ oruvar, aracaṉaik kaṇṭu,
   uyarntōṅ kucci [1] uvāmati pōla
   [2] nivantōṅku veṇkuṭai maṇṇakam niḻaṟceya,
   vēlum kōlum [3]aruṭkaṇ viḻikka;
   tītiṉ ṟuruḷka nī ēntiya [4]tikiri;
   niṉakkeṉa [5]varainta āṇṭuka ḷellām
   maṉakkiṉi tāka : vāḻiya vēntē!
   iṉṟē yalla; ippati maruṅkil
   [6]kaṉṟiya kāmak kaḷḷāṭ ṭayarntu
   pattiṉip peṇṭir pāṟceṉ ṟaṇukiyum
   naṟṟavap peṇṭir piṉṉuḷam pōkkiyum
   [7] tīviṉai yuruppa uyir [8]īṟu ceytōr
   [9]pārāḷ vēntē ! paṇṭum palarāl;
   eṉṟu moḻintu, ivaṟṟukku eṭuttukkāṭṭākap pārppaṉi marutiyeṉṉum pattiṉip
   peṇṭirpāl karuttuc celutti uyiriḻanta kakantaṉ maintaṉ ceytiyum,
   vicākai yeṉṉum naṟṟava makaḷ mītu kātaluṟṟa am maintaṉukku muṉ piṟantōṉ
   ceytiyum eṭuttuk kūṟi aracaṉai vāḻtti niṉṟaṉar. atukēṭṭa vēntaṉ,
   iṉṟē yalla eṉṟeṭut turaittu,
   [10]naṉṟaṟi mātavir? [11]nalampala kāṭṭiṉir;
   iṉṟum uḷatō ivviṉai ? uraiyum,
   eṉṟu viṉaviṉaṉ; avaṉukku ammā tavar kūṭṭattu oruvar muṉ vantu, "
   tītiṉṟāka, ceṅkōl vēntē!" eṉṟu toṭaṅki,
   [12] muṭi poruḷ uṇarntōr [13]mutu nīrulakil
   [14] kaṭiyappaṭṭaṉa aintuḷa, avaṟṟil
   kaḷḷum poyyum kaḷavum kolaiyum
   taḷḷā tākum kāmam; tampāl
   āṅ [15]katu kaṭintōr [16]allavai kaṭintōreṉa
   nīṅkiṉa raṉṟē niṟaitava mākkaḷ;
   nīṅkā raṉṟē, nīḷnila vēntē
   [17]tāṅkā narakam taṉṉiṭai uḻappōr
   eṉṟu toṭaṅki, maṇimēkalaiyiṉ varalāṟum, avaḷ pāl utaya kumaraṉ karuttuc
   celuttiyuḻaṉṟa tiṟamum, avaḷ kāyacaṇṭikai yiṉ vaṭivukoṇṭu tirinta
   vakaiyum, viñcaiyaṉ ceytiyum viriyak kūṟi,
   ...... tīviṉai urutta tākaliṉ,
   [18] matimaruḷ veṇkuṭai maṉṉa ! niṉ makaṉ
   utaya kumaraṉ oḻiyā ṉāka,
   āṅkavaḷ taṉṉai ampalat tēṟṟi,
   ōṅkiruḷ yāmattu ivaṉai āṅ kuyttuk
   kāyacaṇ ṭikaitaṉ kaṇava ṉākiya
   [19] vāyvāḷ viñcaiyaṉ taṉṉaiyum kūuy
   viñcai makaḷ pāl ivaṉ van taṉaṉeṉa
   [20] vañca viñcaiyaṉ maṉattaiyum kalakki
   āṅkavaṉ taṉkai vāḷāl ampalattu
   īṅkivaṉ taṉṉai eṟintatu,
   eṉṟu coṉṉār. atukēṭṭa aracaṉ neñcil taṉ makaṉ ceykaiyai aruvaruttu,
   arukukiṉṟa cōḻika ēṉātiyai nōkki,
   yāṉ [21]ceyaṟpālatu iḷaṅkōṉ taṉṉait
   tāṉ cey tataṉāl [22]takavilaṉ viñcaiyaṉ;
   mātavar nōṉpum maṭavār kaṟpum
   kāvalaṉ kāval iṉ ṟeṉiṉ iṉṟāl;
   makaṉai muṟaiceyta [23]maṉṉavaṉ vaḻi, ōr
   [24] tuyarviṉai yāḷaṉ tōṉṟiṉaṉ eṉpatu,
   vēntar tamcevi [25]uṟuvataṉ muṉṉam
   īṅku ivaṉ taṉṉaiyum īmat tēṟṟik
   [26] kaṇikai makaḷaiyum kāval ceyka
   eṉṟu paṇittāṉ. maṇimēkalaiyum ciṟaiyiṭap peṟṟāḷ.
   ---
   [1]. uvāmati - muḻut tiṅkaḷ.
   [2]. nivantōṅku - mikavuyarnta, oru poruṭ paṉmoḻi.
   [3] aruṭkaṇ viḻikka - aruḷākiya aṟamē taḻaippikka.
   [4]. tikiri - aracāṭci. aracāṭciyai uruḷai (tikiri) eṉ ṟamaiyiṉ
   ataṟkēṟpa, "uruḷka" eṉṟār. celka eṉpatu karuttu.
   [5]. varainta - aṟutiyiṭṭa. [6]. kaṉṟiya - ceṟinta; aṭippaṭṭa.
   [7]. tīviṉai uruppa - tīviṉai vantu tūṇṭa. [8]. īṟu - kēṭu.
   [9]. pār - cōṇāṭu. [10]naṉṟu - aṟam.
   [11]. nalam pala - naṟceytikaḷ pala.
   [12]. muṭiporuḷuṇarntōr - "pāramārttika ñāṉikaḷ'.
   [13]. mutu nīr ulaku - paḻaimaiyāṉa kaṭal cūḻnta ulakil.
   [14]. kaṭiyappaṭṭaṉa - vilakkappaṭṭaṉa. avai, kaḷ, poy, kaḷavu. kolai,
   kāmam.
   [15]. atu - kāmam.
   [16]. allavai - ēṉaiya kaḷ mutaliya nāṉku.
   [17]. tāṅkā narakam - poṟukkamāṭṭāta tuṉpam tarum narakam.
   [18]. matimaruḷ veṇ kuṭai-muḻumati pōlum veṇkoṟṟakkuṭai.
   [19]. vāy vāḷ - veṭṭutalil tappāta vāḷ.
   [20]. vañca viñcaiyaṉ - kolaiyai maṉattē koṇṭirunta kāñcaṉaṉ. at
   tīviṉai, avaḷai ēṟṟi, ivaṉai uyttu, viñcaiyaṉaik kūuy, maṉattaiyum
   kalakki, vāḷāl eṟintatu eṉa iyaikka.
   [21]. ceyaṟpālatu - ceytaṟkuriya kolait taṇṭattai.
   [22]. takavilaṉ - aracu neṟiyaṟiyum takutiyillāṉ.
   [23]. maṉṉavaṉ - maṉu vēntaṉ.
   [24]. tuyar viṉai yāḷaṉ - tīviṉai yāḷaṉ (utaya kumaraṉ)
   [25]. uṟuvataṉmuṉ - aṭaivataṟku muṉpē .
   [26]. kaṇikai - mātavi. makaḷ - maṇimēkalai. taṉ makaṉ kolai yuṇṭaṟkum,
   taṉ kuṭi paḻi yeytu taṟkum kāraṇamātaliṉ, maṇimēkalai yait tāymuṟai
   koṇṭu, 'kaṇikai makaḷ" eṉa aracaṉ ikaḻntāṉ eṉka .
   -------------

                          23. ciṟaiviṭu kātai

   taṉ putalvaṉ, utayakumaraṉ kolaiyuṇṭiṟantataṉāl, cōḻa vēntaṉ maṉaivi
   irācamātēvikku uṇṭāṉa tuyarattiṟku ellai yillaiyāyiṟṟu. aracaṉ kōyilil,
   aracarkkāyiṉum, aracakumarark kāyiṉum, araciyarkkāyiṉum takuvaṉa kūṟit
   tuyarāṟṟuvikkum cāṉṟōr palar iruntaṉar. avaruḷ vācantavai-yeṉpāḷum
   orutti. ivaḷ oru peru mutiyōḷ. irācamātēvikku nērnta tuyaram tuṭaikkum
   karuttiṉaḷāy avaḷ araciyiṉ kōyiṟku vantāḷ. vantavaḷ, araciyiṉ aṭiyil
   vīḻnturaikkum amaitiyai vilakki,
   aḻutu aṭi vīḻātu āyiḻai taṉṉait
   toḻutu muṉ niṉṟu [1]tōṉṟa vāḻttik
   [2]koṟṟam koṇṭu kuṭipuṟaṅ kāttuc
   [3]ceṟṟa tevvar tēentama tākkiyum,
   taruppaiyiṟ kiṭatti vāḷiṟ pōḻntu .
   [4] ceruppukal maṉṉar [5]celvuḻic celkaeṉa
   mūttu viḷital ik kuṭippiṟan tōrkku
   [6]nāppuṭai peyarātu; nāṇuttaka vuṭaittē.
   eṉṉeṉap paṭumō niṉmakaṉ muṭintatu !
   [7]maṉpatai kākkum maṉṉavaṉ taṉmuṉ
   tuṉpaṅ koḷḷēl eṉṟu avaḷ
   colliviṭṭup pōyiṉaḷ. irācamātēviyum oruvāṟu tēṟi, taṉ makaṉ iṟattaṟkuk
   kāraṇamāy niṉṟa maṇimēkalaiyai vañcaṉai yāl muṭikkat tuṇintu, puṟattē
   meyyaṉpum teḷinta vuṇarvu muṭaiyāḷ pōl naṭittu, orunāḷ aracaṉai naṇṇi,
   avaṉ aṭi vīḻntu toḻutu,
   piṟarpiṉ cellāp [8]pikkuṇik kōlattu
   aṟivu [9]tirintōṉ [10]araciyal tāṉ ilaṉ;
   karumpuṭait taṭakkaik kāmaṉ [11]kaiyaṟa
   arumpeṟal iḷamai [12]perumpiṟi tākkum
   [13]aṟivu talaippaṭṭa āyiḻai taṉakkuc
   ciṟai [14]takkaṉṟu ceṅkōl vēntē!
   eṉṟu collavē, aracaṉ avaḷatu vañcamaṟiyātu, ciṟanta aṟiviṉāl āyntu,
   [15]ciṟappiṉ pālār makkaḷ; allār
   maṟappiṉ pālār maṉṉarkku eṉpatu,
   aṟintaṉai yāyiṉ ivvāyiḻai taṉṉaic
   ceṟinta [16]ciṟainōy tīrkka eṉṟu
   aracikkut terivittu ēvalarai nōkki maṇimēkalaiyaic ciṟai viṭumāṟu
   paṇittāṉ. uṭaṉē, irācamātēvi, " aracē,
   eṉṉōṭu iruppiṉum irukka, iv viḷaṅkoṭi
   taṉ ōṭu eṭuppiṉum [17]takaikkunar il"
   eṉṟuraittu, maṇimēkalaiyai mellak koṇarntu taṉ akanakark kaṇṇē
   taṅkumāṟu ceytukoṇṭāḷ. piṉpu, maṇimēkalaikkup pittuṇṭāmāṟu maruntoṉṟait
   tantāḷ; maṟupiṟap puṇarntava ḷātaliṉ, avaḷai am maruntu oṉṟum
   ceyyavillai. piṉporu kāl, araci, kallā iḷaiñaṉ oruvaṉai yaḻaittu avaṉ
   kainnirampap poṉ koṭuttu, maṇimēkalai mārpil puṇarkuṟi ceytu paḻip
   puṇṭāmāṟu ceyya viṭuttāḷ; avaṉ varukaikkuṟip paṟintatum maṇimēkalai,
   vēṟṟurukkoḷḷum mantiram ōti, āṇurukkoṇ ṭiruntāḷ. vantavaṉ, " aracar
   urimai makaḷir irukkumiṭattē āṭavar kuṟukār; iṅkōr āṭavaṉ uḷaṉ; ītu ētō
   vañcam " eṉṟu añci yōṭiviṭṭāṉ. vēṟoru nāḷ, maṇimēkalai nōyāl
   uṇavukoḷvatilaḷ eṉap poy kūṟi, avaḷai, araci, oru puḻukkaṟaiyil uṇavu
   koṭātu aṭaittuvaittāḷ; āyiṉum, am maṇimēkalai, ūṇoḻi mantiram ōti, mēṉi
   varuntātu iṉi tiruntāḷ. ic ceykaikaḷāl avaṭkuc ciṟitum tīṅku nērātatu
   kaṇṭatum irācamātēvi mikavum accamuṟṟu, avaḷait tāḷ toḻutu, taṉ makaṉ
   mēl vaitta kātalāl tāṉ piḻaiyākac ceytavaṟṟaip poṟukka vēṇṭiṉaḷ.
   maṇimēkalai avvāṟē poṟuttu, "araci, niṉmakaṉ, ceṉṟa piṟappil irākulaṉ
   eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ vāḻntu tiṭṭi viṭattāl tīṅkuṟṟu iṟantāṉ; ap pōtu
   avaṉporuṭṭu yāṉ uyir tuṟantēṉ. nī yātu ceytaṉai? ippiṟappil avaṉ
   poruṭṭu varuntum nī muṉpiṟappil avaṉuk kāka aḻutāyallaiyē.
   pūṅkoṭi nallāy! poruntātu ceytaṉai.
   uṭaṟkaḻu taṉaiyō? uyirkkaḻu taṉaiyō?
   uṭaṟkaḻu taṉaiyēl uṉ makaṉ taṉṉai
   eṭuttup puṟaṅkāṭ ṭiṭṭaṉar yārē!
   uyirkkaḻu taṉaiyēl, uyir pukum [18]pukkil
   [19] ceyappāṭṭu viṉaiyāl [20]terintuṇarvariyatu;
   avvuyirk kaṉpiṉai yāyiṉ, āytoṭi!
   evvuyirk kāyiṉum iraṅkal vēṇṭum.
   niṉmakaṉ iṟattaṟkuk kāraṇamākiyatu, muṟpiṟappil maṭaikkalam
   citaittāṉeṉṟu maṭaiyaṉaik koṉṟa tīviṉaiyē. atuvē iṉṟu viñcaiyaṉ vāḷāl
   ivaṉ veṭṭuṇṭu vīḻac ceytatu. itaṉai yevvāṟu aṟintāyeṉiṉ, kūṟuvēṉ, eṉṟu
   tāṉ malar vaṉam pukuntatu mutal ulakavaṟaviyil teyvam uraittatu īṟāka
   nikaḻntavai muṟṟum, tēvi neñcu koḷḷumāṟu kūṟi muṭittāḷ. ataṉmēlum,
   irācamātēvi nikaḻttiya tīṅkukaḷaiyum, avaṟṟil tāṉ uyntu tīrnta
   vakaiyaiyum colli,
   [21] antaram cēṟalum [22]ayaluruk kōṭalum
   cintaiyiṟ koṇṭilēṉ, ceṉṟa piṟaviyiṉ
   [23]kātalaṟ payantōy, kaṭuntuyar kaḷaintu
   tītuṟu vevviṉai tīrppatu poruṭṭāl,
   eṉat taṉ koḷkaiyai yuraittāḷ. piṉpu, kāmam, kolai, poy, kaḷavu mutaliya
   kuṟṟaṅkaḷaik kaṭital vēṇṭumeṉṟu colli, mēlum cila aṟaṅkaḷaik kūṟaluṟṟu,
   [24]ceṟṟam ceṟuttōr, muṟṟa uṇarntōr;
   [25] mallal mā ñālattu vāḻvō reṉpōr,
   [26] allal mākkaṭku illatu nirappunar;
   tiruntēr elvaḷai ! [27]cellula kaṟintōr,
   varunti vantōr arumpaci kaḷaintōr;
   tuṉpa maṟukkum [28]tuṇiporuḷ uṇarntōr,
   maṉpataik kellām aṉpoḻiyār
   eṉṟa ivvaṟaṅkaḷai irācamātēvikku ōti, avaḷuṟṟa varuttat taip pōkki,
   maṉam teḷivaṭaiyac ceytāḷ.
   [29]ñāṉa naṉṉīr naṉkaṉam teḷittut
   [30] tēṉā rōti cevi mutal vārttu
   [31]makaṉ tuyar neruppā maṉamviṟa kāka
   akañcuṭu ventī āyiḻai yavippa,
   [32]tēṟupaṭu ciṉṉīr pōlat teḷintu
   [33] māṟu koṇ ṭōrā maṉattiṉa ḷāki
   āṅku avaḷ toḻutalum, āyiḻai poṟāaḷ
   tāṉ toḻu tēttit [34] takuti ceytilai;
   [35] kātalaṟ payantōy; aṉṟiyum, kāvalaṉ
   māperun tēvi," eṉṟu etir vaṇaṅkiṉa ḷeṉ.

   [7]. maṉpatai - makkaḷ.

   [10]. araciyal - aracarukkuriya takuti. [11]. kaiyaṟa - ceyalaṟcoḻiya.
   [12]. perumpiṟi tākkum - payaṉpaṭā vakaiyiṟ keṭukkum.
   [13]. aṟivu - tavañāṉam.
   [14]. takkaṉṟu - takkataṉṟu.
   [15]. ciṟappiṉ pālār - kalvi, aṟivu, oḻukkam eṉṟa ciṟappiṉ pakuti
   yuṭaiyavarkaḷē. makkaḷ - putalvar.
   [16]. ciṟai nōy - ciṟait tuṉpam. [17]. takaikkunar - taṭuppavar.
   [18]. pukkil – pukumiṭam.
   [19]. ceyappāṭṭu viṉaiyāl - ceyyappaṭuvaṉa vākiya iruvakai viṉai yālum.
   [20]. viṉaiyāl uyir pukum pukkil terintuṇartal aritu eṉka.
   [21]. antarañ cēṟal - vicumpāṟākac cellutal.
   [22]. ayaluru - vēṟṟuruvam.
   [23] kātalaṉ - irākulaṉ. avaḷē utayakumāraṉākap piṟantāṉ. kaṭuntuyar -
   makaṉai yiḻanta mikka tuṉpam.
   [24]. ceṟṟam ceṟuttōr - pakaimai neṭitu niṟkum ciṉa mākiya kuṟṟattai
   aṟac citaittavar. [25]. mallal - vaḷam.
   [26]. allal mākkaḷ - tuṉpamuṟum makkaḷ. nirappunar - nirampak
   koṭuppavar.
   [27]. cel lulaku -eṭutta uṭampiṉ nīṅkic ceṉṟaṭaiyum nallulakam.
   [28]. tuṇiporuḷ - ' paramārtta tattuva ñāṉam."
   [29]. ñāṉa nal nīr - ñāṉamākiya nalla nīr.
   [30]. tēṉārōti cevi mutal - tēṉ poruntiya kūntalaiyuṭaiya
   irācamātēviyiṉ ceviyiṉkaṇ. tēṉārōti - aṉmoḻit tokai.
   [31]. makaṉai yiḻanta tuyarākiya neruppu maṉam viṟakākap paṟṟi, uḷ
   ḷattaic cuṭṭeḻunta koṭiya tī eṉka. āyiḻai - maṇimēkalai.

   ciṟitākiya nīraic ciṉṉīr eṉṟal marapu.
   [33]. māṟu koṇṭu - pakaimai koṇṭu. ōrā - teḷiyāta.
   [34]. takuti - takka ceyal. [35]. kātalaṉ - utaya kumaraṉ.

                   24. āputtiraṉāṭu aṭainta kātai

   cittirāpati yeṉṉum mutukaṇikai, taṉ cūḻcciyāl utaya kumaraṉ ulakavaṟavi
   pukku uyiriḻantāṉ eṉpatu uṇarntu neñcu naṭuṅki meyvarunti,
   irācamātēviyai aṭaintu, avaḷ tiru vaṭiyil vīḻntu vaṇaṅkit toḻutu,
   [1]toṉṟu paṭu mānakart tōṉṟiya nāḷ mutal
   yāṉuṟu tuṉpam yāvarum paṭṭilar;
   māperun tēvi ! mātar yāriṉum,
   [2] pūvilai yīttavaṉ poṉṟiṉaṉ eṉṟu,
   mātavi mātavar paḷḷiyuḷ aṭaintatum,
   [3] parantupaṭu maṉai toṟum pāttiram ēnti
   [4] araṅkak kūtticeṉṟu aiyaṅ koṇṭatum,
   nakuta lallatu [5]nāṭakak kaṇikaiyar
   takuti yeṉṉār, taṉmai yaṉmaiyiṉ;
   maṉṉavaṉ makaṉē yaṉṟiyum mātarāl,
   innakar uṟūum iṭukkaṇum uṇṭāl,
   eṉṟu coṉṉavaḷ, muṉporukāl, cōṇāṭṭai yāṇṭa neṭu. muṭikkiḷḷi yeṉpāṉ kaṭaṟ
   kāṉaṟ pūmpoḻilil pīlivaḷai yeṉṟa oru nākanāṭṭu makaḷaik kaṇṭu avaḷ
   nalattiṟ karuttaip pōkkip pētuṟṟu niṟkaiyil, avaḷ maṟaintaṉaḷ; avaḷait
   tēṭit tirinta avaṉmuṉ, tēvacāraṇaṉ oruvaṉ tōṉṟa, avaṉai yaracaṉ
   vaṇaṅkik kēṭpa, avaṉ,
   kaṇṭilēṉ; āyiṉum kārikai taṉṉaip
   [6]paṇṭaṟi vuṭaiyēṉ; [7] pārttipa ! kēḷāy.
   nāka nāṭu [8]naṭukkiṉ ṟāḷpavaṉ,
   [9]vākai vēlōṉ, vaḷaivaṇaṉ; tēvi,
   vāca mayilai vayiṟṟuḷ tōṉṟiya
   pīlivaḷai yeṉpōḷ; piṟanta annāḷ .
   iravikulat toruvaṉ iṇai mulai tōyak
   [10]karuvoṭu varum eṉak [11]kaṇiyeṭut turaittaṉṉ,
   āṅkap putalvaṉ varūu mallatu,
   pūṅkoṭi vārāḷ; [12]pulampal; itu kēḷ;
   eṉa ac cāraṇaṉ mēlum collaluṟṟu, innakaril intiraviḻā āṇṭutōṟum .
   nikaḻtal vēṇṭum; tavaṟiṉ innakar kaṭalāṟ koḷḷappaṭum. itu maṇimēkalā
   teyvattiṉ vāymai moḻi; intiraṉatu cāpamum uṇṭu eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭu nīṅkiṉaṉ;
   kiḷḷiyum taṉ nakarai yaṭaintāṉ. aṉṟu mutal iṉṟuvarai ip pūmpukār
   nakarattār intiraviḻā eṭuttu varukiṉṟaṉar. avar maṉattē itupaṟṟiya
   accam iruntukoṇṭē varukiṟatu. iṉi, maṇi peyaraiyuṭaiyāṭku uṇṭākiya
   tuṉpam kāraṇamāka varumō eṉṟu añcukiṉṟēṉ; ātalāl, am maṇimēkalaiyaic
   ciṟai viṭuttu eṉpāl taruka eṉa vēṇṭiṉaḷ.
   ivvāṟu cittirāpati vēṇṭic coṉṉataik kēṭṭa irāca mātēvi,
   kaḷḷum poyyum kāmamum kolaiyum,
   [13]uḷḷak kaḷavu meṉṟu uravōr tuṟantavai
   [14] talaimaiyāk koṇṭaniṉ talaimaiyil vāḻkkai
   [15] pulaimaiyeṉ ṟañcip pōnta pūṅkoṭi
   niṉṉoṭu pōntu niṉmaṉaip pukutāḷ;
   eṉṉō ṭirukkum
   eṉṟu iyampiṉaḷ. cittirāpati ēkkamuṟṟāḷ. innilaiyil maṇimēkalai
   ciṟaippaṭṭamai cutamatiyāl aṟinta mātavi aṟavaṇa vaṭikaḷai yaṭaintu
   avarai vaṇaṅki, nikaḻntatu kūṟi, maṇimēkalai yiṉ ciṟaivīṭu virumpi
   avaruṭaṉ irācamātēvipāl vantaṉaḷ aṭikaḷ varakkaṇṭa irācamātēvi
   porukkeṉa eḻuntu irukkai tantu avarkku vēṇṭiya ciṟappukkaḷaic ceytu,
   [16]yāṇṭu pala pukka num iṇaiyaṭi varunta eṉ
   [17]kāṇtaku nalviṉai nummaiyīṅ kaḻaittatu;
   [18]nāttolai villai yāyiṉum, taḷarntu
   mūttatu iv yākkai; vāḻkapal lāṇṭu
   eṉṟu mukamaṉ moḻintāḷ. kēṭṭa aṭikaḷ, makiḻvuṟṟu,
   tēvi ! kēḷāy : [19] ceytava yākkaiyiṉ,
   mēviṉē ṉāyiṉum [20]vīḻkatir pōṉṟēṉ :
   piṟantār mūttār piṇinō yuṟṟār
   iṟantār eṉkai iyalpē; itukēḷ :
   pētaimai ceykai yuṇarvē [21]aruvuru
   vāyil ūṟē nukarvē vēṭkai
   paṟṟē [22]pavamē [23tōṟṟam viṉaippayaṉ
   iṟṟeṉa vakutta iyal pī rāṟum
   piṟantōr aṟiyiṉ [24]perum pēṟaṟikuvar
   …. …. ….
   araicaṉ tēviyoṭu āyiḻai nallīr !
   [25]puraitīr nallaṟam pōṟṟik kēṇmiṉ,
   eṉap palarkkum potuppaṭak kūṟi, maṇimēkalaiyaic ciṟappāka nōkki,
   maṟupiṟap puṇarnta maṇimēkalai, nī
   [26]piṟavaṟaṅ kēṭṭa piṉṉāḷ vantu uṉakku
   ittiṟam palavum ivaṟṟiṉ pakutiyum
   [27]muttēr nakaiyāy! muṉṉuṟak kūṟuval
   eṉṟu uraittuviṭṭuc ceṉṟār. piṉpu maṇimēkalai yeḻuntu, am makaḷirai
   vaṇaṅki, "nīṅkaḷ itukāṟum kēṭṭa nallaṟaṅkaḷai maṟavātu
   kaṭaippiṭittoḻukumiṉ; yāṉ iṉimēl iṅku uṟai vēṉāyiṉ, ivaḷ utayakumāṉ
   iṟattaṟku ētuvāyiṉaḷ eṉpa rātaliṉ, yāṉ āputtiraṉ nāṭu celvēṉ. ataṉpiṉ,
   maṇipalla vam ceṉṟu pīṭikai toḻutu, vañcikkuc ceṉṟu pattiṉik kaṭavuṭku
   nallaṟam ceyvēṉ. eṉakku iṭar uṇṭāmeṉṟu nīvir iraṅkutal vēṇṭā" eṉṟu
   colliviṭṭu, antimālai vantatum, ulakavaṟaviyum campāti kōyilum,
   kantiṟpāvaiyum vaṇaṅkip paravi, vāṉvaḻiyāka, āputtiraṉ nāṭṭaiyaṭaintu
   āṅku oru poḻilakattē taṅki iḷaippāṟiṉaḷ. ciṟitu pōtil, avaḷ aṅkē vāḻnta
   tarumacāvakaṉ eṉṉum muṉivaṉ oruvaṉaik kaṇṭu,
   innakarp pēr yātu? innaka rāḷum
   maṉṉavaṉ yār?
   eṉṟāḷ. avaṭku, am muṉivaṉ,
   nākapuram itu; nalnaka rāḷvōṉ
   pūmi cantiraṉ makaṉ, puṇṇiyarācaṉ :
   īṅku ivaṉ piṟanta annāḷ toṭṭum
   ōṅkuyar vāṉattup peyal [28]piḻaippaṟiyātu;
   maṇṇum [29] maraṉum vaḷampala tarūum;
   [30] uṇṇiṉṟu urukkum nōy uyirkku illai
   eṉṟu uraittāṉ.
   ----
   [1]. toṉṟupaṭu mānakar - paḻamai poruntiya kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉat tiṉ kaṇ.
   [2]. pūvilai īttavaṉ - mātavi kūttum pāṭṭum eṉṟa kalaivakai nirampip
   pūtta kālattu aṉṟaip pariyam koṭuttuc cērnta kōvalaṉ . mātavar paḷḷi -
   aṟavaṇa vaṭikaḷ uṟaiyum paḷḷi.
   [3]. parantu paṭu maṉai - mikap palavāy virinta maṉaikaḷ,
   [4. araṅkak kūtti - araṅkamēṟik kūttāṭum pāṉmaiyaḷāṉa maṇi mēkalai.
   [5]. nāṭakak kaṇikaiyar - nāṭakamāṭum kaṇikai makaḷir .
   [6]. paṇṭu aṟivu uṭaiyēṉ - muṉpu kaṇṭa aṟivu uṭaiyēṉ; muṉpu kaṇṭuḷḷēṉ
   eṉpatu.
   [7]. pārttipa - aracē. [8]. naṭukkiṉṟu - muṟai piṟaḻviṉṟi.
   [9]. vākai vēl - veṟṟimālai cūṭiya vēl. tēvi - maṉaivi. tēvi yākiya
   vācamayilai yeṉpavaḷ.
   [10]. karuvoṭu varum - karuppamuṟṟu varuvāḷ .
   [11]. kaṇi - cōtiṭaṉ. [12]. pulampal - varuntātē.
   [13]. uḷḷak kaḷavu - uḷḷattāl piṟar poruḷai maṟaiyiṟ koḷḷak karututal
   ituvum kuṟṟameṉpatu paṭa, " uḷḷak kaḷavu" eṉṟār.
   [14]. talaimaiyāk koṇṭa talaimaiyil vāḻkkai - uravōr vilak kiyavaṟṟait
   talaimaip poruḷākak koṇ ṭoḻukutal kaṭaippaṭṭa 'parattaimai vāḻvu.
   [15]. pulaimai - pulaittaṉmai yuṭaiyatu.
   [16]. yāṇṭu pala pukka num iṇaiyaṭi - yāṇṭu pala ātaliṉ mutumai yuṟṟa
   num aṭikaḷ iraṇṭum. -
   [17]. kāṇtaku nalviṉai - aḻakiya nalviṉaiyāṉatu.
   [18]. nā tolaivillai - aṟam uraikkum umatu nā melivaṭaintatillai.
   [19]. ceytava yākkaiyiṉ mēviṉēṉ - tavam ceytaṟkamainta iv yākkaiyoṭu
   kūṭi yiruntēṉ.
   [20]. vīḻ katir - maṟaiyum ñāyiṟu.
   [21]. aruvuru - pētaimai mutal viṉaippayaṉ #mukak kūṟiya paṉṉiru
   nītāṉaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. " aruvuru eṉpatu avvuṇarvu cārnta, uyirum
   uṭampumākum eṉpa." (maṇi. 30: 84-5.)
   [22]. pavam-" pavameṉappaṭuvatu karumavīṭṭam, tarumuṟaiyituveṉat tāntām
   cārtal.' (maṇi. 30: 93-4.)
   [23]. tōṟṟam - piṟappu. [24] perum pēṟu - pari niruvāṇam.
   [25]. puraitīr - kuṟṟamillāta. kuṟṟamāvatu - piṟavikkētuvāya tuṟakka
   iṉpan tarutal.
   [26]. piṟa aṟam - maṟṟaiya camaya vaṟaṅkaḷ.
   [27]. muttēr nakai - muttuppōlum, pal.
   [28]. piḻaippaṟiyātu - peyyā toḻital iṉṟām.
   [29]. maraṉ - maraṅkaḷ.
   [30]. uḷ niṉṟu urukkum - uṭaṟkuḷḷēyiruntu uyirai varuttum.
   ------------

           25. āputtiraṉōṭu maṇipallavam aṭainta kātai

   puṇṇiyarācaṉākiya āputtiraṉ taṉ urimai maṉaiviyuṭaṉ maṇimēkalai
   yaṭaintirunta poḻilaiyaṭaintu, āṅkē vāḻntirunta tarumacāvakaṉ eṉṉum
   muṉivaṉ aṭivaṇaṅki,
   aṟaṉum [1]maṟaṉum anittamum [2]nittat
   tiṟaṉum tukkamum [3]celluyirp pukkilum
   [4]cārpiṉ tōṟṟamum cārpaṟut [5]tuytiyum
   [6]āriyaṉ amaitiyum amaivuṟak kēṭṭu
   varukaiyil, avaṉ irunta poḻilil maṇimēkalai piccaippāttira (mēnti
   niṟpatu kaṇṭu viyantu,
   [7]peṇṇiṇai yillāp peruvaṉap puṟṟāḷ,
   kaṇṇiṇai yiyakkamum [8]kāmaṉō ṭiyaṅkā,
   aṅkaiyiṟ pāttiram koṇṭu aṟam kēṭkum
   iṅku iṇaiyillāḷ ivaḷ yār eṉṉa,
   arukē caṭṭaiyaṇinta kāvalaṉ oruvaṉ, aracaṉai vaṇaṅki, “aracē, ivaḷai
   yoppār nāvalantīvil oruvarum ilar; orukāl yāṉ kiḷḷivaḷavaṉuṭaiya
   keḻutakaimai vēṇṭik kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉam ceṉṟirunta kālattu yāṉ
   ivaḷaippaṟṟi yaṟintēṉ. āṅkē ivaḷ piṟappai yuṇarntu eṉakku
   aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷ urait tār eṉpatait taṅkaṭku muṉpē uraittuḷḷēṉ; avaḷē
   appati yiṉ nīṅki ippōḻtil iṅkē vantuḷḷāḷ ” eṉṟāṉ. eṉṟatum, maṇimēkalai,
   aracaṉai nōkki, “ aracē,
   niṉkaip pāttiram, eṉkaip pukuntatu;
   [9]maṉperuñ celvattu mayaṅkiṉai yaṟiyāy;
   appiṟap paṟintilai yāyiṉum, [10]āvayiṟṟu,
   ippiṟap paṟintilai; eṉ cey taṉaiyō?
   maṇippallavam valam koṇṭā lallatu,
   [11]piṇippuṟu piṟaviyiṉ peṟṟiyai yaṟiyāy;
   āṅku varuvāy, araca, nī"
   eṉṟuraittu, vāṉvaḻiyē paṭarntu pakalōṉ kuṭapāl maṟaivataṟ kuḷ
   maṇipallavattai yaṭaintāḷ. aṅkē avaḷ, puttapīṭikaiyaik kaṇṭu valamvantu
   niṟpa, aḵtu avaṭkup paḻam piṟappuṇarvai: nalkiṟṟu. avaḷ, appiṟappil
   kāyaṅkaraiyil kaṇṭa piramatarum muṉivaṉai muṉṉilaip paṭuttikkoṇṭu,
   [12]"perumakaṉ taṉṉoṭum peyarvōrk kellām
   vilaṅkum narakarum pēykaḷu mākkum
   [13] kalaṅkañart tīviṉai kaṭimiṉ; kaṭintāl,
   tēvarum makkaḷum [14] piramarum āku tir;
   ākaliṉ nalvaṉai ayarātu ōmpumiṉ;
   pulavaṉ, muḻutum poyyiṉ ṟuṇarntōṉ,
   ulakuyak kōṭaṟku oruvaṉ tōṉṟum;
   annāḷ avaṉ aṟam kēṭṭōr, allatu
   [15] iṉṉāp piṟavi iḻukkunar illai;
   [16] māṟṟarum kūṟṟam varuvataṉ muṉṉam
   pōṟṟumiṉ aṟam' eṉac [17]cāṟṟik kāṭṭi
   [18]nākkaṭippāka vāyppaṟai yaṟaintīr !
   avvurai kēṭṭu num aṭitoḻu tētta
   [19]vevvurai eṅkaṭku viḷampiṉir;
   aṉṟiyum, appō til, yāṉ, puttaṉ tōṉṟāmuṉpē intiraṉ ippīṭikai avaṟku iṭṭa
   kāraṇam eṉṉai? eṉṟu viṉaviṉēṉ; ataṟku, puttaṉ tōṉṟiya piṉ avaṉ
   piṟappait tāṉ aṟintu koḷvataṟkāka intiraṉ itaṉai amaittāṉ; mēlum
   ippīṭikai ap puttaṉaiyallatu piṟaraip poṟātu eṉṟu kūṟiyatuṭaṉ av
   vintiraṉē, ip pīṭikaiyaip puttaṉākiya
   [20]perumakaṟku amaittup piṟantār piṟaviyait
   [21]tarumapīṭikai cāṟṟuka eṉṟē
   aruḷiṉaṉ; ātaliṉ, āyiḻai piṟaviyum
   [22] iruḷaṟak kāṭṭum eṉṟu eṭutturaitta
   vāymoḻi eṉakku iṉṟum aṉṟēpōl viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu", eṉṟu kūṟip pārāṭṭi yētti
   niṉṟāḷ.
   iḵtiṅṅaṉamāka, maṇimēkalai kūṟiyavaṟṟaik kēṭṭuk kalakka muṟṟa vēntaṉ,
   nērē taṉ tāy amaracuntaripāṟ ceṉṟu taṉ piṟappu varalāṟu muḻutum kēṭṭu,
   iṟanta piṟaviyiṉ [23]yāycey tatū um,
   piṟanta piṟaviyiṉ [24]peṟṟiyum niṉaintu,
   ceruvēl maṉṉar [25]cevvi pārttuṇaṅka,
   araicu vīṟṟiruntu [26]puraiyōrp pēṇi,
   nāṭakam kaṇṭu pāṭaṟ pāṉmaiyiṉ
   [27]kēḷvi iṉṉicai kēṭṭut tēviyar
   ….. …… ……
   matimukak karuṅkaṇ [28]ceṅkaṭai kalakkak
   karuppu villi [29]yaruppukkaṇai tūvat
   [30] tarukkiya kāmak kaḷḷāṭ ṭikaḻntu
   [31] tūvaṟat tuṟattal naṉṟeṉac cāṟṟit
   teḷinta nātaṉ eṉ cevi mutal iṭṭa vittu
   [32]ētamiṉṟāy iṉṟu viḷaintatu;
   maṇimēkalai tāṉ kāraṇamāka,
   eṉṟu taṉ araciyaṟ cuṟṟattārkkut terivittaṉaṉ. ivaṟṟaik kēṭṭōruḷ,
   caṉamittiraṉ eṉṉum amaiccaṉ, “ maṉṉaṉ maṉam vēṟāyiṉaṉ" eṉṟu naṉkaṟintu,
   aracaṉai aṭivaṇaṅki,
   eṅkō vāḻi; eṉ col kēṇmati :
   nuṅkōṉ uṉṉaip peṟuvataṉ muṉṉāḷ,
   paṉṉī rāṇṭip [33]patikeḻu naṉṉāṭu maṉṉuyir
   [34] maṭiya maḻaivaḷam karantu. iṅku.
   īṉṟāḷ kuḻavikku iraṅkāḷākit
   tāṉ taṉi tiṉṉum [35]takaimaiya tāyatu;
   kāy [36]veṅ kōṭaiyil kārtōṉṟiya teṉa,
   nī tōṉṟiṉaiyē; niraittār aṇṇal!
   [37]tōṉṟiya piṉṉart tōṉṟiya uyirkaṭku
   vāṉam poyyātu; maṇvaḷam piḻaiyātu;
   ūṉuṭai yuyirkaḷ uṟupaci aṟiyā;
   nī oḻikālai, niṉnā ṭellām,
   [38]tāy oḻi kuḻavi pōlak kūun;
   [39] tuyar nilai yulakam kāttaliṉṟi, nī
   [40]uyarnilai yulakam vēṭṭaṉai yāyiṉ,
   iṟuti uyirkaḷ eytavum, iṟaiva !
   [41]peṟuti virumpiṉai yākuvai yaṉṟē?
   taṉṉuyirk kiraṅkāṉ, piṟavuyi rōmpum
   maṉṉuyir mutalvaṉ aṟamumītu aṉṟāl;
   [42]matimāṟu ōrntaṉai, maṉṉava!
   eṉṟu eṭutturaittāṉ. itaṉālum, aracaṉ eṇṇam ciṟitum māṟātāyiṟṟu. ākavē,
   avaṉ,
   maṇipallavam valam koḷḷutaṟ keḻunta
   [43] taṇiyā vēṭkai taṇittaṟku aritāl;
   aracum urimaiyum akanakarc [44]cuṟṟamum
   orumati ellai kāttal niṉ kaṭaṉ,
   eṉṟu kūṟi viṭaipeṟṟuc ceṉṟu, vaṅkamēṟi maṇipallavam ceṉṟu cērntāṉ.
   avaṉai maṇimēkalai etirkoṇṭaḻaittuc ceṉṟu, at tīvakattuc cōlaiyai
   valamvantu puttapīṭikaiyaik kāṭṭiṉaḷ. vēntaṉ ataṉaikkaṇṭu valam vantu
   paṇintuniṟpa, ap pīṭikai, avaṉatu paḻampiṟappiṉ nikaḻcciyai,
   kaiyakat teṭuttuk kāṇpōr mukattai
   maiyaṟu maṇṭilam pōlak kāṭṭa, aṟinta anta vēntaṉ,
   eṉ piṟap paṟintēṉ; eṉ iṭar tīrntēṉ;
   teṉ tamiḻ [45]maturaic ceḻuṅkalaip pāvāy!
   māri naṭunāḷ [46] vayiṟukāy paciyāl
   āriru ḷañcātu ampalamaṇaintu āṅku
   [47]irantūṇ vāḻkkai eṉpāl vantōrkku
   [48]aruntūṇ kāṇā taḻuṅkuvēṉ kaiyil
   nāṭuvaṟaṅ kūriṉum ivvōṭuvaṟaṅ kūrātu;
   ēṭā! aḻiyal; eḻuntu itu koḷka eṉa
   amuta curapi aṅkaiyil tantu eṉ
   [49] pavam aṟuvitta vāṉōr pāvāy!
   uṇarvil tōṉṟi uraipporuḷ uṇarttum
   [50]maṇitikaḻ aviroḷi maṭantai niṉṉaṭi,
   tēvarāyiṉum [51] pirama rāyiṉum
   [52]nā mācu kaḻūum nalamkiḷar tiruntaṭi,
   piṟanta piṟavikaḷ [53]pēṇuta lallatu,
   maṟantu vāḻēṉ, maṭantai!
   eṉac cintātēviyai muṉṉilaippaṭuttu vaṇaṅki, maṇimēkalai yuṭaṉ, ap
   pīṭikaikkut teṉmēṟkil uḷḷa kōmuki yeṉṉum poykaik karaikkuc ceṉṟu, oru
   puṉṉaimarattiṉ nīḻalil iruntaṉaṉ. ivviruvaratu varavum, puṉṉainiḻalil
   porunti yiruppatum uṇarnta tīvatilakai yeṉpāḷ, avar muṉ pōntu,
   aracaṉaic ciṟappāka nōkki,
   "aruntuyir maruntu muṉ aṅkaiyiṟ koṇṭu
   peruntuyar tīrtta apperiyōy ! vantaṉai;
   annāḷ niṉṉai [54]yayarttup pōyiṉar,
   piṉ nāḷ vantu niṉ [55] peṟṟimai nōkki,
   [56]niṉkuṟi yiruntu tammuyir nīttōr,
   oṉpatu ceṭṭikaḷ uṭaleṉpu ivaikāṇ;
   āṅkavar iṭavuṇṭu avaruṭaṉ vantōr,
   ēṅki [57]meyvaittōr eṉpum, ivaikāṇ;
   [58]ūrtirai tokutta uyarmaṇal putaippa,
   [59]āymalarp puṉṉai yaṇiniḻal kīḻāl
   aṉpuṭai āruyir aracaṟ [60]karuḷiya
   eṉpuṭai yākkai iruntatu, kāṇāy,
   [61]niṉṉuyir koṉṟāy; niṉṉuyirk kiraṅkip
   piṉṉāḷ vanta piṟaruyir[62] koṉṟāy;
   kolaivaṉ allaiyō ! koṟṟava ṉāyiṉai !"
   eṉṟivvāṟu puṇṇiyarācaṉukkuc colli vantavaḷ, piṉpu maṇi 'mēkalaiyai
   nōkki,
   "palar toḻupāttiram kaiyiṉ ēntiya
   [63]maṭavaral nallāy! niṉ ṟaṉ mānakar,
   kaṭalvayiṟu pukkatu; kāraṇam kēḷāy"
   eṉṟu toṭaṅki, “ nākanāṭṭaracaṉ pīlivaḷai yeṉpāḷ, cōḻa ṉukkup piṟanta
   taṉmakaṉuṭaṉ ittīvakam pōntu pīṭikaiyai valaṅ koṇ ṭēttiniṟpa,
   kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattiṟkuc cellum kam paḷac ceṭṭiyiṉ vaṅkam vantu niṟkak
   kaṇṭu avaṉpāl makaṉait tantu, aracaṉpāl cērppikkumāṟu koṭuttaṉaḷ. ac
   ceṭṭi avvāṟē koṇṭu celluṅkāl, avaṉatu marakkalam kaṭalil aṉṟu iravē
   kaviḻntu viṭṭatu. itaṉai yavaṉ tappic ceṉṟu, aracaṟ kuṇartta, avaṉ
   makaṉai yiḻanta tuyaram poṟātu, kāṉalum, kaṭalum, kaṭaṟkaraiyum tēṭit
   tirintaṉaṉ. ataṉāl, annakaram intira viḻā āṟṟātu maṟantoḻintatu. uṭaṉē,
   maṇimēkalā teyvam an nakarattaik kaṭal koḷḷumāṟu cāpamiṭṭāḷ; ataṉāl atu
   kaṭaṟku iraiyāyiṟṟu; aracaṉāṉa neṭumuṭikkiḷḷiyum taṉippaṭṭup pōyiṉaṉ
   eṉṟu colli, ic ceytiyait taṉakku at teyvam kūṟiyatākavum terivittu, in
   nikaḻcciyil, " niṉ tāyarum aṟavaṇa vaṭikaḷuṭaṉ vañcimānakar
   ceṉṟaṭaintaṉar; iṉi, niṉkula mutal vaṉ oruvaṉai am maṇimēkalā teyvam
   muṉṉāḷil ātaravu cey taḷittatum, avaṉ piṉpu aṟavaracāṇṭatum piṟavum
   aṟavaṇaṉpāl kēṭṭaṟikuvai" eṉṟu terivittuviṭṭu, attīvatilakaiyum
   avarkaḷai viṭṭu nīṅkiṉaḷ.
   piṟaku, puṇṇiyarācaṉ maṇimēkalaiyuṭaṉ taṉ muṉpiṟappiṉ uṭampu
   putaiyuṇṭirunta iṭamaṇuki, akaḻntu nōkka, aṅkē, ataṉ eṉpukkūṭu
   tōṉṟakkaṇṭu maṉaṅ kalaṅkiṉaṉ. avaṉai maṇimēkalai pārttu,
   "eṉṉuṟ ṟaṉaiyō ! [64]ilaṅku itaḻttārōy!
   niṉṉāṭu aṭaintuyāṉ niṉṉai īṅkaḻaittatu,
   maṉṉā ! niṉṟaṉ maṟupiṟap puṇartti,
   [65]antarat tīviṉum akaṉ perun [66]tīviṉum
   niṉpeyar [67] niṟutta; nīḷnila māḷum,
   aracar tāmē aruḷaṟam pūṇṭāl,
   poruḷu muṇṭō piṟapurai[68] tīrttaṟku?
   aṟameṉap paṭuvatu yāteṉak kēṭpiṉ,
   maṟavātu itukēḷ : maṉṉuyirk kellām,
   uṇṭiyum uṭaiyum uṟaiyuḷu mallatu,
   kaṇṭa tu il,"
   eṉak kaṭṭuraittāḷ. itaik kēṭṭa av vēntaṉ,
   "eṉ nāṭ ṭāyiṉum piṟar nāṭṭāyiṉum,
   naṉṉutal ! uraitta nallaṟam ceykēṉ :
   eṉ piṟap puṇartti eṉṉai nī [69] paṭaittaṉai;
   [70]niṉ tiṟam nīṅka lāṟṟēṉ yāṉ ",
   eṉṟāṉāka, iccoṟkaḷaik kēṭṭatum, maṇimēkalai muṟuvalittu,
   [71]"puṉkaṇ koḷḷal; nī pōntataṟ kiraṅki, niṉ
   maṉperu nāṭu [72]vāyeṭut taḻaikkum;
   [732]vaṅkat tēkuti; vañciyuṭ celval",
   eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭut tāṉ vāṉvaḻiyāka vañci mānakarkkuc ceṉṟāḷ.
   ---
   [1]. maṟaṉ - pāvam.
   [2]. nittat tiṟaṉ - nittap poruḷkaḷiṉ vakai..
   [3]. celluyirp pukkil - uṭampiṉ nīṅkic cellum uyirkkup puku miṭam,
   [4]. cār piṉ tōṟṟam - pētaimai mutalākak kūṟiya paṉṉiraṇṭu : avai oṉ
   ṟiṉoṉṟu cārntu tōṉṟuvaṉavātaliṉ, " cārpiṉ tōṟṟa " meṉṟār.
   [5]. uyti - vīṭupēṟu.

                      26. vañcimā nakar pukka kātai

   tāy kaṇṇakiyaiyum, tantai kōvalaṉaiyam tāṉ kāṇa vēṇṭumeṉṟu eḻunta
   vēṭkai maṇimēkalai yuḷḷattai yalaippa, avaḷ vañci nakariṉ puṟattē,
   avarkaḷuṭaiya paṭimam niṟuviya kōyiṟkuḷ nuḻaintu taṉ vēṭkai tīra,
   vaṇaṅki niṉṟu [1]kuṇam pala ētti,
   [2]“aṟpukkaṭaṉ nillātu, naṟṟavam paṭarātu,
   kaṟpuk kaṭaṉ pūṇṭu num [3]kaṭaṉ muṭittatu
   aruḷal vēṇṭum,"
   eṉṟu aḻutu, kaṇṇakip paṭimattiṉ muṉ niṉṟāḷ. appōtu, kaṇṇaki,
   "em [4]iṟaikkuṟṟa iṭukkaṇ poṟātu,
   [5]vemmaiyiṉ maturai vevvaḻaṟ paṭunāḷ
   maturāpati yeṉum māperun teyvam,
   itu nīr muṉ cey viṉaiyiṉ payaṉāl;
   eṉṟu tam paḻampiṟappil nikaḻnta nikaḻcciyai viḷaṅkavuraittu,
   appiṟappil, taṉ kaṇavaṉ caṅkamaṉaip parataṉ eṉpāṉ paṟṟik kolai
   purivittāṉāka, avaṉ maṉaivi nīli eṉpāḷ iṭṭa cāpamē it tuṉpattukku
   ētuvāyiṟṟu eṉa am maturāpati vaṟpuṟuttic colliyum,
   cīṟṟam koṇṭu ceḻunakar citaittēṉ;
   mēṟcey nalviṉai yiṉ [6] viṇṇavarc ceṉṟēm;
   avviṉai yiṟutiyiṉ [7]aṭuciṉap pāvam,
   evvakai yāṉum eytuta loḻiyātu;
   [8]umpar ilvaḻi impariṟ [9]palpiṟappu
   yāṅkaṇum iruviṉai yuyttu umaippōla,
   nīṅkarum piṟavik kaṭaliṭai nīntip
   piṟantum iṟantum [10]uḻalvōm; piṉṉar,
   maṟantum maḻaimaṟā makata naṉṉāṭṭukku
   oruperun [11]tilakameṉṟu uravōr uraikkum
   [12] karavarum perumaik tapilaiyam patiyil,
   aḷapparum [13] pāramitai aḷaviṉṟu niṟaittut
   [14] tuḷakkamil putta ñāyiṟu tōṉṟi,
   [15]pōti mūlam porunti vantaruḷi.....
   eṇṇarum [16] cakkaravāḷam eṅkaṇum
   aṇṇal [17] aṟakkatir virikkuṅkālai ....
   tuṉpak katiyil [18] tōṟṟara viṉṟi,
   aṉpuṟu maṉattōṭu avaṉ aṟam kēṭṭut
   [19]tuṟavi yuḷḷam tōṉṟit toṭarum
   [20] piṟavi nītta peṟṟiya mākuvam;
   attiṟa māyiṉum anēka kālam
   ettiṟat tārkkum [21] iruttiyum ceykuvam."
   ivvāṟu tām ceytaṉavum ceyyaviruppaṉavum kūṟiya kaṇṇakiyār, mēlē
   maṇimēkalaikkuc cila kūṟaluṟṟu,
   [22-naṟaikamaḻ kūntal naṅkai ! nīyum,
   muṟaimaiyiṉ inta mūtūrakattē,
   avvavar camayattu [22]aṟiporuḷ kēṭṭu,
   [23] meyvakai yiṉmai niṉakkē viḷaṅkiya
   piṉṉarp periyōr [24]piṭakaneṟi kaṭavāy,
   iṉṉatu ivviyalpu,"
   eṉa eṭutturaittu, mēlum, "nī iḷaiyaḷ vaḷaiyaḷ eṉṟu karuti niṉakku
   yāvarum meypporuḷai yuṇartta muṉvārār; ātaliṉ, nī vēṟṟuruk koḷka" eṉṟu
   paṇittaṉuppiṉaḷ. maṇimēkalaiyum oru mā tavaṉ vaṭivu koṇṭāḷ.
   mātavaṉ vaṭiviṟ puṟappaṭṭa maṇimēkalai,
   [25]tēvakulamum [26]teṟṟiyum paḷḷiyum
   pūmalarp poḻilum poykaiyum [27]miṭaintu,
   naṟṟava muṉivarum [28]kaṟṟaṭaṅkiṉarum,
   naṉṉeṟi kāṇiya [29]toṉṉūṟ pulavarum,
   eṅkaṇum viḷaṅkiya [30] eyiṟ puṟavirukkaiyiṉ
   ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ eṉum ceṅkōl vēntaṉ
   iruntu aracupuriyum vañcimā nakarkkuṭ pukuntaṉaḷ.

   [20]. piṟavi nītta peṟṟiyam - piṟavittoṭarpaṟṟa niruvāṇameyti
   yavarākuvam.
   [21]. irutti - citti. [22]. naṟai - tēṉ.

   [23]. meyvakai yiṉmai - avai poyyāntaṉmai.
   [24]. piṭakaneṟi - puttaṉaruḷiya ākamamākiya piṭaka nūl neṟi. ip

   [25]. tēvakulam - kōyil. [26]. teṟṟi - mēṭai; tiṇṇaiyumām. ¨

   taka niṟpavar.

            27. camayak kaṇakkar tam tiṟam kēṭṭa kātai

   vañci nakarkkuṭ ceṉṟa maṇimēkalai aṅkē irunta camayak kaṇakkar
   palaraiyum kaṇṭu avaravar camayattiṟaṅkaḷaik kēṭkalā ṉāḷ, avaruḷ
   vaitika mārkkattu aḷavai vātiyai nōkki, " niṉ kaṭaippiṭiyātu?" eṉa
   viṉava, avaṉ,
   vētavi yātaṉum kiruta kōṭiyum
   [1]ētamil caimiṉi eṉum ivvāciriyar
   pattum eṭṭum āṟum paṇpuṟat ta
   ttam vakaiyāl tām pakarn tiṭṭaṉar;
   kāṇṭa l, karutal, uvamam, ākamam,
   [2]āṇṭaiya aruttā pattiyōṭu iyalpu,
   aitikam, [3] apāvam, mīṭci, oḻivu,aṟivu
   [4]eyti uṇṭāmneṟi eṉṟu ivai tammāl
   poruḷiṉ uṇmai [5]pulaṅkoḷal vēṇṭum eṉṟum
   ivai yovvoṉṟiṉ ilakkaṇamum muṟaiyē kūṟi, piramāṇā pācaṅkaḷ eṭṭu uḷa
   eṉṟum, avai,
   [6] cuṭṭuṇarvoṭu, tiriyakkōṭal, aiyam,
   tērātu teḷital, kaṇṭuṇarāmai,
   eytum ilvaḻakku, uṇarntatai yuṇartal,
   niṉaippu eṉa nikaḻva
   eṉṟum, ivaṟṟiṉ iyalpukaḷaiyum teriyak kūṟiṉaṉ. ataṉ mēlum, avaṉē,
   [7] pāṅkuṟum ulōkāyatamē, pauttam, -
   cāṅkiyam, naiyā yikam vaicēṭikam,
   mīmāñ cakamām : camayavāciriyar
   tām, pirukaṟpati, ciṉaṉē, kapilaṉ,
   akkapātaṉ, kaṇātaṉ, caimiṉi;
   meyp pirattiyam, aṉumāṉam, cāttam,
   uvamāṉam, aruttā patti, aṭāvam
   [*]ivaiyē ippōtu iyaṉṟuḷa aḷavaikaḷ
   eṉṟu kūṟiṉaṉ. avaṉai nīṅkiyatum, īcaṉē iṟaivaṉ eṉak koḷḷum caivavāti
   maṇimēkalai muṉ nērpaṭṭāṉ. avaṉai viṉaviya maṇimēkalaikku, avaṉ,
   [8] irucuṭarōṭu iyamāṉaṉ aimpūta meṉṟu
   eṭṭuvakaiyum uyirum yākkaiyu māyk
   [9] kaṭṭi niṟpōṉum [10]kalaiyuruvi ṉōṉum
   paṭaittu viḷaiyāṭum paṇpi ṉōṉum
   [11] tuṭaittut tuyar tīr tōṟṟat tōṉum
   taṉṉil vēṟutāṉ oṉṟilōṉum
   aṉṉōṉ iṟaivaṉākum,
   eṉṟāṉ; piṟaku, piramavāti tōṉṟi, “ pērulakamellām tēvaṉ iṭṭa
   muṭṭaiyākum " eṉṟāṉ; vaiṇavavāti, " kaṭalvaṇaṉ, nāraṇaṉē iṟaivaṉ "
   eṉṟāṉāka, vētavāti mēlvantu colla luṟṟāṉ :
   kaṟpam kai, cantamkāl, eṇ kaṇ,
   teṟṟeṉ niruttam cevi, cikkai mūkku,
   uṟṟa viyākaraṇam mukam peṟṟuc
   ----------
   [*] camayam āciriyar aḷavai
   'ulōkāyatam - pirukaṟpati pirattiyakkam
   pauttam - ciṉaṉ pirattiyakkamum aṉumāṉamum cāṅkiyam - kapilaṉ
   pirattiyakkamum aṉumāṉamum ākamamum
   ivvāṟē piṟavaṟṟaiyum ōṭṭikkoḷka. cāttam - ākamam
   ----
   [12]cārpil tōṉṟā āraṇa [13]vētakku
   ātiyantam illai; atu neṟi,
   eṉṟa avvētiyaṉ urai, meyttiṟam, vaḻakku eṉa varaikkum evvakaiyālum
   icaivataṉṟu eṉa uṭkoṇṭa maṇimēkalai, āṅkē tōṉṟiya ācīvakaṉaik kaṇṭu,
   "niṉ iṟaivaṉ yāvaṉ? niṉ nūṟporuḷ yātu ?" eṉa viṉaviṉaḷ. avaṉ,
   ellaiyil poruḷkaḷil eṅkum eppoḻutum,
   [14]pullik kiṭantu pulappaṭukiṉṟa
   [15]varampil aṟivaṉ iṟai; nūṟporuḷkaḷ aintu,
   eṉṟu kūṟi, avvaintum, uyirum, nilam, nīr, tī, kāṟṟu eṉum nālvakai
   yaṇukkaḷumām eṉṟu vakuttum,
   avvaṇu uṟṟum kaṇṭum uṇarttiṭap
   [16]peyvakai kūṭip pirivatum ceyyum :
   nilam nīr tīk kāṟṟeṉa nāl vakaiyiṉa
   malaimaram uṭampeṉat tiraḷvatum ceyyum :
   vevvēṟāki virivatum ceyyum;
   avvakai yaṟivatu uyireṉappaṭumē
   eṉa uyiraiyum, ivvāṟē ēṉai nālvakai yaṇukkaḷaiyum virittum kūṟiṉaṉ.
   aṅṅaṉam kūṟumiṭattu, aṇukkaḷiṉ potuvi yalpai,
   tītuṟṟu yāvatum citaivatu [17]ceyyā;
   putitāyp piṟantoṉṟu oṉṟiṟ pukutā; mu
   tu nīraṇu nilavaṇuvāyt tiriyā;
   oṉṟu iraṇṭākip piḷappatum ceyyā;
   aṉṟiyum [18]avalpōl parappatum ceyyā,
   eṉṟum, uyirkaḷ ceṉṟeytum piṟappum vīṭupēṟum kūṟaluṟṟu,
   [19]karumm piṟappum karunīlap piṟappum
   pacumm piṟappum cemm piṟappum
   poṉṉ piṟappum veṇṇ piṟappum
   eṉṟu ivvāṟu piṟappiṉum mēvip
   paṇpuṟu varicaiyiṟ [20]pāṟpaṭṭup piṟantōr,
   [21]kaḻiveṇ piṟappiṟ kalantu vīṭaṇukuvar
   eṉṟum, uyirkaḷ,
   peṟutalum ikaḻtalum iṭaiyū ṟuṟutalum,
   [22] uṟumiṭat teytalum tukkacuka muṟutalum,
   [23]peritavai nīṅkalum piṟattalum cātalum
   [24] karuviṟ paṭṭa poḻutē kalakkum;
   iṉpamum tuṉpamum ivaiyum aṇuveṉat takum;
   muṉṉuḷa ūḻē piṉṉum uṟuvippatu
   maṟkali nūliṉ vakai itu"
   eṉṟum kūṟi muṭittāṉ. avaṉai viṭṭu, nikaṇṭavātiyai nōkki, maṇimēkalai,
   "niṉṉāl pukaḻum talaivaṉ yār ? niṉ nūṟporuḷ yāvai?” eṉṟāḷ. nikaṇṭavāti,
   intirar toḻappaṭum [25]iṟaivaṉ em iṟaivaṉ;
   tanta nūṟporuḷ [26]taṉ māttikāyamum,
   [27]ataṉmāttikāyamum kālā [28]kāyamum
   tītil cīvaṉum paramāṇukkaḷum
   nalviṉaiyum tīviṉaiyum avviṉaiyāl
   ceyvuṟu [29]pantamum vīṭum ittiṟatta
   eṉat taṉ nūṟporuḷait tokuttum, piṉpu ovvoṉṟaiyum virittum kūṟi muṭivil,
   vīṭu pēṟṟiyalpai,
   cīrcāl nalviṉai tīviṉai yavai ceyum
   varuvaḻi yiraṇṭaiyum māṟṟi muṉ cey
   aruviṉaip payaṉ aṉupavit [30]taṟuttiṭutal;
   atu vīṭākum,
   eṉṟaṉaṉ. avaṉpiṉ cāṅkiyaṉ tōṉṟit taṉ cāṅkiya nūṟ karuttai yuraikkat
   toṭaṅki,
   taṉaiyaṟivaritāyt tāṉ mukkuṇamāy
   maṉanikaḻ viṉṟi [31] māṇpamai potuvāy
   ellāp poruḷum tōṉṟutaṟ kiṭameṉac
   collutal mūlap pakuti
   eṉṟum, cittam, māṉ, putti, vāṉ, kāṟṟu, tī, nīr, maṇ eṉṟa ivai
   oṉṟiliruntu oṉṟu veḷippaṭum eṉṟum, ivaṟṟiṉ kūṭṭattiliruntu maṉamum,
   maṉattu āṅkāramum, kaṉmēntiriya ñāṉēntiriya vikāraṅkaḷum tōṉṟi ulakāy
   nikaḻum eṉṟum,
   vanta vaḻiyē ivai ceṉ ṟaṭaṅki
   antamil piraḷayamāy [32]iṟumaḷavum
   oṉṟāy eṅkum parantu nittiyamāy
   aṟitaṟ keḷitāy mukkuṇa maṉṟip
   [33]poṟiyuṇarvikkum potuvu maṉṟi
   epporuḷum tōṉṟutaṟ kiṭamiṉṟi
   apporuḷellām aṟintiṭaṟku uṇarvāy
   oṉṟāy eṅkum parantu nittiyamāy
   niṉṟuḷa uṇarvāy nikaḻtarum puruṭaṉ
   [34]pulamār poruḷkaḷ irupat taintuḷa
   eṉṟum, avai nilam mutalāka uyireṉum āṉmā īṟāka irupat taintum niralē
   kūṟi muṭittāṉ. maṇimēkalai, uṭaṉē arukil niṉṟa vaicēṭikaṉai "niṉ
   vaḻakkiṉai uraippāyāka," eṉṟu kēṭṭāḷ. anta vaicēṭikaṉ, poruḷ, kuṇam,
   toḻil, cāmāṉi yam, vicēṭam, camavāyam eṉa nūṟporuḷ āṟākum eṉṟum
   avaṟṟuḷ,
   poruḷ eṉpatu,
   kuṇamum toḻilu muṭaittāy, ettokaip
   poruḷukkum ētuvām; apporuḷ oṉpatām;
   ñālam, nīr, tī, vaḷi, ākāyam, ticai,
   kālam. āṉmā, maṉam; ivaṟṟuḷ nilam,
   oli ūṟu niṟam cuvai nāṟṟamoṭu aintum
   payil kuṇam uṭaittu; niṉṟa nāṉkum
   [35]cuvai mutal orō kuṇam avaikuṟai vuṭaiya,
   eṉa ovvoṉṟiṉ iyalpukaḷaiyum virittuk kūṟiṉaṉ. avaṉpiṉ, pūtavāti tōṉṟi,
   [36]tātakip pūvum kaṭṭiyum iṭṭu
   maṟṟum kūṭṭa [37]matukkaḷi piṟantāṅku
   uṟṟiṭum pūtattu uṇarvu tōṉṟiṭum;
   avvuṇarvu avvappūtat taḻivukaḷiṉ
   vevvēṟu piriyum; [38] paṟaiyōcaiyiṟ keṭum;
   uyiroṭum kūṭṭiya uṇarvuṭaip pūtamum,
   uyirillāta uṇarvil pūtamum
   avvap pūtavaḻi avai piṟakkum;
   [39]meyvakai ituvē;
   eṉṟum, yām kāṭciyaḷavai yaṉṟik karuttu mutaliya aḷavaikaḷaik koḷḷēm
   eṉṟum, ip piṟappē, immaiyum immaip payaṉu mām; maṟumaiyum viṉaippayaṉ
   tuyttal uṇṭu eṉpatum veṟum poyyē eṉṟum kūṟiṉaṉ. ivaṟṟaik kēṭṭu
   maṇimēkalai māṟu vēṟu kūṟātu nakaittaṉaḷ; avaṉ, nakaippatu eṉṉai ?
   eṉṟāṉāka, tāṉ paḻam piṟappuṇarnta ceyti kūṟiṉaḷ. ataṉaik kēṭṭatum
   avaṉ nakaittu,
   [40]teyvamayakkiṉum kaṉāvuṟu tiṟattiṉum
   [41]maiyaluṟuvār maṉam vēṟām vakai
   aiyamaṉṟi illai,
   eṉṟāṉ. avaṉai maṇimēkalai nōkki, "kāṭciyē yaḷavaiyāyiṉ
   niṉ
   tantai tāyarai aṉumāṉattāl alatu
   inta ñālattu evvakai aṟivāy?
   meyyuṇar viṉṟi meyp poruḷ uṇarvariya
   aiyamallatu itu collap peṟāy,
   eṉat tāṉ koṇṭirunta mātavaṉ vaṭivilē niṉṟu tāṉ uṉṉiya poruḷai uraittu,
   iṅkē kāṭṭiya camaya nūṟporuḷ kaḷai aṟintāḷ.

   cempiṟappu, poṉ piṟappu, veṇ piṟappu eṉa varuvaṉa.

   [22]. uṟumiṭattu - eytu taṟkuriya viṭattu.
   [23]. avai peritu nīṅkal - avaṟṟiṉ muḻutum nīṅkal.
   [24]. karuviṟ paṭṭapoḻutē - karuppattilē karuvākiyapōtē.
   [25]. iṟaivaṉ - arukaṉ. intirar - nūṟuvakai intirar.
   [26]. taṉmāttikāyam - eṅkum uḷatāki eññāṉṟum poruḷkaḷai naṭattuvatu
   (maṇi. 27: 187-8).
   [27]. ataṉmāttikāyam - poruḷkaḷaṉaittaiyum niṟkacceyvatu.

   [29]. pantam - kaṭṭu.

   [32]. iṟumaḷavum - aḻiyuṅkāṟum.
   [33]. poṟiyuṇarvikkum potu - poṟikaṭku viṭayamākum cāmā" ṉiyattaṉmai ."

                       28. kaccimā nakar pukka kātai

   iṉi, avvañci mānakarattē maṇimēkalai taṉ tāyaraiyum aṟa
   vaṇavaṭikaḷaiyum kāṇa virumpi avarait tēṭikkoṇṭu puṟañcēri kaṭantu,
   kalavai nīrum, cāntukaḻi nīrum, virainīrum eṉṟa palavakai nīrē eṅkum
   pāytalāl, tāmarai, kaḻunīr, āmpal mutaliya pūkkaḷ malā, vaṇṭāṟṟa
   viḷaṅkiya akaḻiyaiyum, palvakaip poṟiyamaittu, viṉai māṇpu ciṟantu
   niṉṟa matilaiyum, kaṭantu ceṉṟu, koṭinilavum vāyil vaḻiyāka nakarkkuḷḷē
   ceṉṟaṉaḷ. aṅkē, kāvalāḷar, mīṉ vilaiñar, uppuppakarukar, kaḷviṟpōr, [1]
   kāḻiyar, [2]kūviyar eṉpavar mutalākap pala toḻilāḷarum vaṇikarum
   nirampak kāṇappaṭṭaṉar. avar kūṭṭattaik kaṭantu celpavaḷ, kaṇṭu
   perumakiḻvu koṇṭu, intira vikāram pōl eḻilpeṟṟu viḷaṅkiya oru mātavap
   paḷḷiyaik kaṇṭāḷ.
   nalantaru paṇṇum [3]tiṟaṉum vāyppa
   [4]nilam kalam kaṇṭam nikaḻak kāṭṭum
   pāṇar eṉṟivar palvakai maṟukum,
   [5]vilaṅkaram porūum [6]veḷvaḷai pōḻnarōṭu
   ilaṅku maṇi viṉaiñar [7] irīiya maṟukum,
   [8]vēttiyal potuviyal eṉṟiv viraṇṭiṉ
   kūttiyalpu aṟinta kūttiyar maṟukum,
   [9]pālvēṟāka eṇvakaip paṭṭa
   kūlam [10]kuvai iya kūlamaṟukum,
   [11]mākatar cūtar vētāḷikar maṟukum,
   pōkam purakkum potuvar mali maṟukum,
   [12] kaṇ nuḻaikallā nuṇṇūṟ kaiviṉai,
   vaṇṇa aṟuvaiyar vaḷam tikaḻ maṟukum,
   poṉṉurai kāṇpōr naṉmaṉai maṟukum,
   paṉmaṇi pakarvōr [13]maṉṉiya maṟukum,
   maṟaiyōr [14]aruntoḻil kuṟaiyā maṟukum,
   araciyal maṟukum, amaicciyal maṟukum,
   eṉaip peruntoḻil cey ēṉōr maṟukum,
   [15]maṉṟamum, [16]potiyilum, cantiyum, [17]catukkamum,
   [18]putuk kōḷ yāṉaiyum [19]poṟṟārp puraviyum,
   [20] katikkuṟa vaṭippōr kaviṉpeṟu vītiyum,
   cēṇōṅ karuvi tāḻnta cey kuṉṟamum,
   [21]vēṇavā mikukkum virai maraikkāvum,
   viṇṇavar, taṅkaḷ vicumpiṭam maṟantu
   naṇṇutaṟku otta [22]naṉṉīriṭaṅkaḷum,
   cālaiyum kūṭamum [23] tamaṉiyappotiyilum
   [24]kōlam kuyiṉṟa koḷkai yiṭaṅkaḷum
   aṅku uṟainta mātavarkaḷil, kōvalaṉ tantaiyākiya mācāttuvāṉum tavattiṟam
   pūṇṭiruntāṉ. avaṉaik kaṇṭatum, maṇimēkalai avaṉ tiruvaṭi paṇintu, tāṉ
   amuta curapi peṟṟatu mutal, camayak kaṇakkar tiṟam kēṭṭatum, avar
   kūṟiyavaṟṟaik koḷḷātu, putta tēvaṉatu nallaṟam kēṭka nayantu
   aṟavaṇaṉait tēṭi vantatum īṟāka nikaḻn tavai yaṉaittum colli muṭittāḷ.
   mācāttuvāṉ, iṉit taṉ varalāṟu kūṟaluṟṟu,
   tūyōy! niṉṉai eṉ
   nalviṉaip payaṉ kol nāṉ kaṇṭatu;
   taiyal! kēḷ : niṉtātaiyum tāyum
   ceyta tīviṉaiyiṉ ceḻunakar kēṭuṟat
   tuṉpuṟa [25]viḷintamai kēṭṭuc cukataṉ
   aṉpu koḷ aṟattiṟku [26]arukaṉēṉātaliṉ,
   maṉaittiṟa vāḻkkaiyai māyameṉ ṟuṇarntu
   tiṉaittaṉaiyāyiṉum celvamum yākkaiyum
   nilaiyā eṉṟē nilaipeṟa uṇarntē
   [27] malaiyā aṟattiṉ mātavam purintēṉ;
   eṉṟavaṉ, tāṉ inta vañcimānakarkku vanta kāraṇam kūṟat toṭaṅki,
   kuṭakkōc cēralaṉ [28] kuṭṭuvar peruntakai
   [29]viṭarccilai poṟitta vēntaṉ muṉṉāḷ,
   [30]tuppaṭu cevvāyt tuṭiyiṭaiyāroṭum –
   ippoḻil pukuntāṅku irunta ellaiyuḷ,
   ilaṅkait tīviluḷḷa camaṉoḷi eṉṉum malaikkuc ceṉṟu ataṉai valaṅkoṇṭu
   pōnta tarumacā raṇar, ip poḻiṟkaṇ vantu kaṟ ṟalattil iruntaṉar.
   avarkaḷaik kaṇṭa aracaṉ avarkaṭku vēṇ ṭum uṇavum vēṟu pala ciṟappum
   tantu iṟaiñci niṉṟāṉ. avarkaḷ makiḻntu,
   piṟappiṉ tuṉpamum [31]piṟavā iṉpamum
   [32] aṟattakai mutalvaṉ aruḷiya vāymai
   iṉpa vāramutu iṟaivaṉ cevimutal
   tuṉpam nīṅkac
   corintaṉar. annāḷil, avvēntaṟkuk kātaṟ pāṅkaṉāka - irunta vaṉ, niṉ
   tantai kōvalaṉukku oṉpatu talaimuṟai muṉṉavaṉā vāṉ; avaṉ peyarum
   kōvalaṉ eṉpatē. avaṉum avar kūṟiya aṟaṅkaḷaik kēṭṭu, taṉ muṉṉōr
   paṭaitta celvattaiyē yaṉṟit tāṉ paṭaittataṉaiyum kūṭṭi ēḻutāḷil
   iravalarkku īntu, puttaṉukku oru ciṟanta caittiyam amaittaṉaṉ; ataṉaik
   kaṇṭu vaṇaṅkutaṟkāka yāṉ vantēṉ. īṅkuṟaiyum mātavarkaḷ kāvirippūm
   paṭṭiṉattaik kaṭal koḷum eṉṟataṉāl, iṅkē iruntoḻintēṉ.
   iṉṉum kēḷāy : naṉṉeṟi mātē!
   tīviṉai [33]yuruppac ceṉṟa niṉ tātaiyum
   tēvariṉ tōṟṟi muṟceyviṉaip payattāl,
   āṅkat tīviṉai iṉṉum tuyttup
   pūṅkoṭi! muṉṉavaṉ pōtiyiṉ nallaṟam
   tāṅkiya tavattāl tāṉ tavam tāṅkik
   [34]kātali taṉṉoṭu kapilaiyam patiyil
   nātaṉ nallaṟam kēṭṭu vīṭueytu meṉṟu
   [35]aṟputak kiḷavi aṟintōr kūṟac
   coṟpayaṉ uṇarntēṉ; tōkai ! yāṉum,
   annāḷ āṅkavaṉ aṟaneṟi kēṭkuvaṉ
   eṉṟāṉ. ataṉpiṉ, maṇimēkalaikku, " niṉakku uḷavākum nikaḻcci kaḷai
   niṉakkuk kantiṟpāvaikkaṇ niṉṟa tuvatikaṉ uraittuḷāṉ aṉṟō ? avaṉ
   uraittaṉaṉ eṉṟa ceytiyai aṟavaṇaṉ collakkēṭṭuḷḷēṉ; avvaṟavaṇaṉē,
   niṉakku etu nikaḻcci nikaḻumiṭam kāñci eṉṟāṉ. avaṉ kāñcikkuc ceṉṟa
   nāḷil niṉ tāyarākiya mātaviyum cutamatiyam uṭaṉ ceṉṟaṉar, eṉṟu
   colliviṭṭu,
   aṉṉatai yaṉṟiyum, aṇiyiḻai! kēḷāy :
   poṉṉeyiṟ kāñci nāṭu kaviṉaḻintu,
   maṉṉuyirmaṭiya maḻaivaḷam [36]karattaliṉ,
   annakar mātavarkku aiyamiṭuvōr,
   iṉmaiyiṉ innakar eytiṉar, kāṇāy;
   [37] āruyir maruntē! annāṭ [38]ṭakavayiṉ
   [39]kāreṉat tōṉṟik kāttal, niṉ kaṭaṉ
   eṉa vaṟpuṟuttiṉaṉ. atukēṭṭa maṇimēkalai vañcinakariṉ mēṟkup
   pakkattiṉiṉṟu viṇṇiṭattil ōṅki, vaṭaticaiyil kāñci nōkkic ceṉṟu, atu
   vaḷaṅkuṉṟip poliviḻantu kiṭappataik kaṇṭu varunti, naṭunakarellaikkaṇ
   iṟaṅki, toṭukaḻaṟ kiḷḷiyiṉ piṉṉava ṉāṉa iḷaṅkiḷḷi eṭutta
   puttacaittiyattaip paravittoḻutu, annakariṉ teṉmēṟkilirunta pūmpoḻilai
   yaṭaintiruntāḷ. ataṉait tarumatavaṉam eṉpar. avaḷ varavai yaṟinta
   kañcuki yoru vaṉ, aracaṉpāl viraintu ceṉṟu, vaṇaṅki niṉṟu,
   kōvalaṉ maṭantai [40]kuṇa vatam purintōḷ;
   nāvalantīvil tāṉ naṉi [41]mik kōḷ,
   aṅkaiyiṉ ēntiya amuta curapiyoṭu
   taṅkātu, ippatit [42] tarumata vaṉattē
   vantu tōṉṟiṉaḷ māmaḻai pōla
   eṉṟu moḻintāṉ. kēṭṭa vēntaṉ mantiriccuṟṟam cūḻntu varap puṟappaṭṭut
   taṉakkuk kantiṟpāvai yuraittavai yaṉaittum uṇmaiyātal kaṇṭu viyanta
   uḷḷattaṉāy vantu maṇimēkalaiyaik kaṇṭu,
   ceṅkōl kōṭiyō, ceytavam piḻaittō,
   [43]koṅkaviḻ kuḻalār kaṟpukkuṟai paṭṭō,

   [45]alattaṟ kālai yākiyatu; aṟiyēṉ;

   kārikai tōṉṟum; avaḷ peruṅ [47]kaṭiñaiyiṉ

            29. tavattiṟam pūṇṭu tarumam kēṭṭa kātai

   taṉ aṭikaḷil vaṇaṅki niṉṟa maṇimēkalaiyai nōkki, aṟa. vaṇavaṭikaḷ,
   'nākanāṭṭaracaṉ makaḷāṉa pīlivaḷai tāṉ peṟṟa makaṉaik kampaḷac
   ceṭṭiyiṭam tantu cōḻaṉiṭam cērppikkac colla, am makaṉai aḻaittup pōnta
   aṉṟiravē, avaṉatu vaṅkam kaṭalil kaviḻntatu. tappik karaiyaṭainta ac
   ceṭṭi, kāvi rippūm paṭṭiṉattai yaṭaintu cōḻaṉiṭam nikaḻntatu kūṟi ṉaṉ..
   aracaṉ makaṉ piri vāṟṟātu avaṉait tēṭik kaṭaṟkaraiyilum kāṉaṟcōlaiyilum
   alaintu tiriyavē, pūmpukār nakaram intira viḻāvai! āṟṟātu nekiḻttatu.
   itu niṟka,
   taṉ viḻāt tavirtaliṉ [1]vāṉavar talaivaṉ,
   niṉṉuyirt [2] tantai neṭuṅkulat tutitta
   maṉṉuyir [3]mutalvaṉ makara [4]vēlaiyuḷ,
   [5]muṉṉiya vaṅkam [6] muṅkik kēṭuṟap
   poṉṉiṉ ūci pacuṅkam paḷattut
   tuḷḷiya teṉṉat toṭukaṭal uḻantuḻi,
   eḻunāḷ ellai [7] iṭukkaṇ vanteytā
   vaḻuvāc cīlam [8]vāymaiyiṟ koṇṭa
   pāṉmaiyiṉ, taṉātu pāṇṭu kampaḷam tāṉ
   [9]naṭukkuṟṟa taṉmai nōkki,
   ātimutalvaṉ pōti mūlattu
   nāta ṉāvōṉ naḷi nīrp parappil
   [10] evvamuṟ ṟāṉ taṉatu evvam tīr eṉa
   uraittāṉ. atukēṭṭa maṇimēkalā teyvam pōntu avaṉukku vēṇṭum utaviyiṉaic
   ceytatu. itaṉai muṉṉaṟiyāta avaṉukku,
   aṟavaracāḷavum aṟavāḻi yuruṭṭavum,
   piṟavi tōṟutavum peṟṟiyaḷ eṉṟu
   cāraṇar aṟintu kūṟiṉar. anta utavi niṉaintu niṉ tantai niṉakku
   maṇimēkalai yeṉṟa ippeyarai iṭṭaṉaṉ; aṉṟiravē avaṉ kaṉavil maṇimēkalā
   teyvam tōṉṟi naṉavupōla nī tuṟavupūṇum ceytiyai uraittuḷatu. inta
   maṇimēkalā teyvamē pukār nakaram intiraviḻāvai maṟantatu kaṇṭu, " in
   nakaraik kaṭal koḷḷuka” eṉṟu capikka, intiraṉ cāpamum uṭaṉ vantu
   oṉṟiyatu; nakarum kaṭalāl viḻuṅkappaṭṭatu.
   appāl yāṉum niṉ tāyarum niṉ poruṭṭu in nakarai yaṭaintōm," eṉṟu colli
   muṭittaṉar.
   maṇimēkalai, avar tāḷai maṟupaṭiyum vaṇaṅki, ic ceyti -yaiyē yāṉ
   puttapīṭikaiyaip pōṟṟiya kālattup pōnta tīva tilakaiyum uraittaṉaḷ.
   aṉṉa aṇinakar maruṅkē,
   vēṟṟuruk koṇṭu vevvē ṟuraikkum
   nūṟṟuṟaic camaya nuṇ poruḷ kēṭṭē,
   avvuruveṉṉa [11]aivakaic camayamum
   [12]cevvi taṉmaiyiṉ cintaiyil vaittilēṉ;
   aṭikaḷ ! meypporuḷ aruḷuka
   eṉa vēṇṭi niṉṟāḷ. aṟavaṇa aṭikaḷum, puttacamaya aḷavai yiyal kaḷaik
   kūṟaluṟṟu, āticiṉēntiraṉ aḷavai iraṇṭē eṉa vakut tāṉ; avai,
   pirattiyakkam, aṉumāṉam eṉpaṉa.
   cuṭṭuṇarvaip pirattiyakkam eṉac colli,
   viṭṭaṉar [13]nāmacātik kuṇak kiriyaikaḷ,
   maṟṟavai aṉumāṉattum [14]aṭaiyum eṉa,
   ….. …. …..
   ēṉai aḷavaikaḷellām karuttiṉil
   āṉa muṟaimaiyiṉ, aṉumāṉamām.
   ivvaṉumāṉattil, pakkam, etu, tiṭṭāntam, upanayam, nikamaṉam eṉa aintu
   uṟuppukkaḷ uḷa. avaṟṟil,
   [15] pakkam, immalai neruppuṭaittu eṉṟal;
   pukaiyuṭait tātalāl eṉal, poruntu ētu;
   vakaiyamai [16] aṭukkaḷai pōl, tiṭṭāntam;
   upanayam, malaiyum pukaiyuṭait teṉṟal;
   nikamaṉam, pukaiyuṭaittē neruppuṭait [17]teṉal.
   iṉi, pakkamutalāka nikamaṉam īṟākak kūṟiya aintaṉuḷ, upanayam nikamaṉam
   eṉṟa iraṇṭum tiṭṭāntattil aṭaṅkum. aṭaṅkavē, niṉṟa pakkam, ētu,
   tiṭṭāntam eṉṟa mūṉṟilum nallavum tīyavum eṉa irutiṟam uṇṭu. tīyapakkam
   pakkappōli eṉṟum, tīyaetu, ētup pōli eṉṟum, tīya tiṭṭāntam
   tiṭṭāntappōli yeṉṟum tiṭ ṭānta vāpācam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. ētuppōliyaiyum
   ētu vāpācam eṉpa. .

   veḷippaṭṭuḷa cāttiyataṉmattiṟam

   naṉku eṉ pakkameṉa nāṭṭuka........ nallētu,

   taṉmac corūpa viparīta cātaṉam,

   vakutturaikkiṉṟār. itaṉai mutaṟkaṇ, cātaṉmiya tiṭṭānta āpācam,
   vaitaṉmiya tiṭṭānta āpācam eṉa iraṇṭāka vakuttuk koḷḷukiṉṟār.

   ṭānta vāpācamum aivakaiya :
   cāttiyā viyā virutti,

   viparīta vetirēkam
   eṉpaṉavām. ivvāṟu tokuttum vakuttum viḷakkiya aṭikaḷ maṇimēkalaiyai

   meyyum poyyum ittiṟa vitiyāl,

   eṉṟu aṟivuṟukkiṉṟār.
